Conclusion: FedTVD通过质量与数量并重的双重加权策略，有效缓解了数据不均衡的影响，实现了更稳定和更具泛化能力的全局模型。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed client devices while preserving data privacy. However, FL faces significant challenges due to data heterogeneity, particularly in terms of label distribution skewness and variations in dataset sizes, which can lead to biased model updates and hinder convergence. To address this, we propose FedTVD, a novel FL algorithm that weights client contributions during aggregation by considering both data quality and quantity. Unlike traditional FL approaches such as FedAvg, which rely solely on dataset size for client weighting, FedTVD integrates Total Variation Distance (TVD) to measure the divergence between each client's local label distribution and a uniform global distribution. Clients with highly skewed distributions receive lower weights, preventing unbalanced datasets with imbalances from disproportionately influencing the global model. At the same time, dataset size is incorporated to ensure scalability and fairness. This dual-weighting mechanism effectively mitigates the impact of data imbalance, leading to more stable and generalized global models. Experimental results show that FedTVD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all datasets (FMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100) and all levels of data heterogeneity. Notably, it achieves up to 10.6% improvement over FedAvg on CIFAR-10 under highly skewed data, while maintaining top performance even under moderate and IID settings.

</details>


### [34] [Second Order Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07924)
*Drake Brown,Yuhao Huang,Shih-Hsin Wang,Bao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出二阶漂移模型（Second-Order Drifting Models），将漂移扩散动态从一阶提升到二阶相空间，通过引入人工速度变量，在傅里叶空间中实现加速的密度扰动衰减，从而缓解一阶漂移模型中因核谱导致的频率依赖刚度问题，同时保持单步推理特性。通过半隐式训练算法在合成分布匹配、序列数据生成和机器人控制任务上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶漂移模型虽然避免了迭代推理，但其基于核的漂移场会导致频率相关的训练动态：在线性化 regime 下，密度残差的每个傅里叶模式以核谱决定的速率衰减，导致细粒度结构的恢复速度缓慢（即谱刚度问题）。

Method: 将漂移动态提升到相空间（phase space），为生成的样本引入人工速度变量，使密度扰动在傅里叶空间中服从加速的二阶动态，并与优化理论中的 Nesterov 加速方法建立联系。同时推导了实用的半隐式训练算法。

Result: 在合成分布匹配、序列数据生成和机器人控制等任务上，二阶漂移模型改善了收敛行为，达到与一阶漂移基线相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 二阶漂移模型为缓解一阶漂移模型的谱刚度提供了原理性机制，在保持单步推断的同时实现了加速收敛，并验证了其在多种生成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Drifting models are a recent class of one-step generative models that evolve the model distribution during training using a predefined sample-based drift field. Although they avoid iterative inference, their kernel-based drift fields induce frequency-dependent training dynamics: In the linearized regime, each Fourier mode of the density residual decays at a rate determined by the kernel spectrum, leading to slow recovery of fine-scale structure. We propose Second-Order Drifting Models, which lift drifting dynamics into phase space by augmenting generated samples with artificial velocity variables. We show that the resulting density perturbations obey accelerated second-order dynamics in Fourier space, connecting drifting models to the celebrated Nesterov acceleration from optimization theory. This provides a principled mechanism for mitigating the spectral stiffness of first-order drifting while preserving one-step inference. We derive a practical semi-implicit training algorithm and evaluate it on synthetic distribution matching, sequential data generation, and robotic control. Across these settings, the second-order drifting model improves convergence behavior and achieves competitive or superior performance over first-order drifting baselines.

</details>


### [35] [FEAST: Federated Shared-Space Training for Resource-Heterogeneous Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09250)
*Bostan Khan,Masoud Daneshtalab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FEAST，一种联邦共享空间训练框架，通过预算定制子超网络路由和稀疏聚合，在联邦学习中训练弹性超网络，使不同计算能力的设备都能获得合适的子网络，同时解决了异构客户端推断预算不均衡导致的参数可达性偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习需要服务计算能力各异的设备，固定模型无法适配所有设备，而针对每种部署限制单独训练模型成本过高。现有联邦超网络训练方法中，高计算预算子网络独有的参数仅能被少数客户端访问，导致训练不均衡，影响模型性能。

Method: 提出FEAST框架，在客户端计算限制内联合训练多个子网络以平衡参数权重；通过预算定制的子超网络路由仅传输相关的超网络部分；采用稀疏聚合合并返回的参数切片。此外引入单参数γ分配协议，解耦训练数据量与推断预算的耦合关系，使异构联邦学习模拟更公平。

Result: 在596M推断MACs下FEAST达到71.06%准确率，比最强的模型异构权重共享基线高2.4个百分点；而SuperFedNAS和DeepFedNAS在25M时接近随机水平，在596M时最高仅17.09%。在CIFAR-100、CINIC-10和TinyImageNet-200上，FEAST在权重共享方法中取得了最高的人口平均准确率。子超网络路由使聚合模型参数流量减少6.8倍。

Conclusion: FEAST有效解决了联邦超网络训练中异构推断预算导致的参数不平衡问题，训练出的超网络可直接服务联邦期间使用的子网络，也支持事后提取额外子网络而无需联邦重训练，为异构联邦学习提供了高效且公平的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) must serve devices with varying computational capabilities. A fixed model cannot suit all devices, while training one model per deployment limit is costly. Federated supernet training instead learns one elastic model with differently sized subnetworks, then deploys a suitable one to each device. When client inference budgets differ, however, parameters exclusive to high-cost subnetworks are reachable by fewer clients. We propose FEAST, a federated shared-space training framework that counters this imbalance by jointly training multiple subnetworks within each client's limit. Budget-tailored sub-supernet routing sends only the relevant supernet portion, and sparse aggregation merges the returned parameter slices. The trained supernet directly serves the subnetworks used during federation and supports post-hoc extraction of additional subnetworks without federated retraining. We further show that independently assigning clients' training-data volumes and inference budgets can distort accuracy--inference-cost comparisons in heterogeneous FL simulations, and introduce a one-parameter $γ$-allocation protocol to control this coupling. In our experimental setup, the SuperFedNAS and DeepFedNAS supernet training procedures remain near chance at 25M and reach at most $17.09\%$ at $596$M inference MACs; FEAST reaches $71.06\%$ at $596$M, $2.4$ points above the strongest model-heterogeneous weight-sharing baseline at its largest tier. Across CIFAR-100, CINIC-10, and TinyImageNet-200, FEAST achieves the highest population-averaged accuracy among the evaluated weight-sharing methods when each client receives its largest affordable subnetwork. Sub-supernet routing reduces aggregate model-parameter traffic by $6.8\times$ relative to full-supernet transmission.

</details>


### [36] [Adaptive Supervised Anchoring for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07935)
*Meilin Yang,Zixuan Ding,Jianhao Nie,Weite Zhang,Yuxin Zhang,Zhiming Shao,Li Yu,Zhe Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的训练框架，通过分离"滚动轨迹条件分布匹配"与"规范上下文监督"两条互补路径，解决了在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中因学生轨迹偏移导致的监督信号退化问题，在多个模型规模和任务上实现了更好的塑性-稳定性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）的性能严重依赖于学生采样的轨迹质量。当学生滚动轨迹偏离目标轨迹时，教师模型在偏离前缀条件下提供的任务相关监督会显著弱化，作者将这一失效模式称为"滚动条件信号退化"。

Method: 提出一个统一训练框架，分离两条互补监督路径：一条保留滚动条件分布匹配以指导学生实际访问的状态；另一条在规范地面真值上下文中应用监督交叉熵，避免在错误滚动前缀上强加目标token。通过token级滚动-目标对齐自适应调节规范上下文锚点的强度，冷启动阶段加强、滚动质量提升后放松。

Result: 在多个模型规模、两个任务族及通用推理基准上的实验表明，该方法相比OPSD提升了任务习得能力，同时保持了通用能力，实现了更有利的经验性塑性-稳定性权衡。

Conclusion: 研究指出上下文质量是在线策略自蒸馏中的核心瓶颈，并证明了将滚动条件引导与规范监督分离的价值。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) adapts a language model by distilling guidance from a frozen teacher on trajectories sampled from the student. Its effectiveness, however, depends critically on the quality of those trajectories. We show that when student rollouts drift from target trajectories, conditioning the teacher on off-target prefixes substantially weakens its task-relevant supervision. Controlled prefix-corruption experiments expose this failure mode, which we term rollout-conditioned signal degradation. To address this problem, we propose a unified training framework that separates two complementary supervision pathways. The first retains rollout-conditioned distribution matching, providing guidance on states the student actually visits. The second applies supervised cross-entropy on canonical ground-truth contexts, avoiding the incompatibility of imposing target tokens on erroneous rollout prefixes. Token-level rollout-target alignment is used to adapt the strength of the canonical-context anchor, emphasizing it during cold start and relaxing it as rollout quality improves. Experiments across multiple model scales, two task families, and general-reasoning benchmarks show that the proposed approach improves task acquisition over OPSD while preserving general capabilities, resulting in a more favorable empirical plasticity-stability trade-off. These findings identify context quality as a central bottleneck in on-policy self-distillation and demonstrate the value of separating rollout-conditioned guidance from canonical supervision.

</details>


### [37] [Depth-adaptive Inference of Looped Language Models via Continuous Depth Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09444)
*Kristian Schwethelm,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了连续深度批处理（CDB）方法，解决了循环语言模型在自适应深度推理中因不同token需要不同循环次数而无法高效批处理的问题。CDB以单个循环迭代为粒度进行调度，在Ouro 1.4B和Huginn 3.5B上实现了理论最大加速的99%，离线吞吐量提升1.5-1.9倍，动态负载下归一化延迟降低45-90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型的核心优势在于深度自适应推理——简单token用较少计算，困难token用较多计算。然而这种自适应性破坏了标准批处理机制，因为同一批次中的token需要不同的循环次数，无法执行统一的前向传播，导致推理效率低下。现有框架（如vLLM）在token级别调度，无法在单次前向传播中动态移除token，而循环级调度虽被提出但从未端到端实现过。

Method: CDB方法以单个循环迭代为调度粒度，具体包括：(1) 将边界阶段（token嵌入和LM头）与循环步骤分别放入独立的优先级队列中调度；(2) 提前一个步骤做出退出决策（exit decisions），以便从容调度；(3) 将所有调度工作与GPU计算重叠进行，避免调度开销影响推理效率。

Result: 在Ouro 1.4B和Huginn 3.5B两个模型上，CDB实现了自适应深度理论上最大加速的99%。具体表现为：离线吞吐量提升1.5-1.9倍；在动态服务负载下，归一化延迟降低45-90%。

Conclusion: CDB通过细粒度的循环迭代级调度，成功地将循环语言模型的自适应深度推理从理论概念转化为高效的端到端实现，解决了此前循环级调度从未被实际落地的问题，为深度自适应推理的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A main promise of looped language models (LMs) is depth-adaptive inference. By iterating a block of shared layers a variable number of times, the model can use less compute for "easy" tokens and more for "hard" ones. However, this adaptivity breaks standard batching: tokens in the same batch now require a different number of loops, so there is no unified forward pass, making efficient inference difficult. Standard inference frameworks like vLLM schedule on the token level and cannot handle this because tokens need to be removed from the batch within the forward pass. Loop-level scheduling has been proposed as a solution, but never implemented end to end. The key challenge is that looped architectures also contain non-looped boundary stages (e.g., token embedding and LM head) that must be scheduled at different frequencies than the loop. We introduce continuous depth batching (CDB), which schedules at the granularity of individual loop iterations. CDB handles boundary stages and loop steps in separate priority queues, makes exit decisions one step ahead, and overlaps all scheduling work with GPU computation. On Ouro 1.4B and Huginn 3.5B, CDB can realize up to $99\%$ of the theoretical maximum speed-up from adaptive-depth, translating to $1.5$-$1.9\times$ higher offline throughput and $45$-$90\%$ lower normalized latency under dynamic serving load.

</details>


### [38] [Persistent Semantic Entities in Tool-Augmented LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07952)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地形式化并评估了工具增强型LLM智能体中的"持久语义实体"（PSEs）——即跨会话持续存在、通过事件触发、并在智能体边界间传播的隐式状态污染问题。研究发现所有24个测试模型均易受影响（20-100%），其中名称绑定是必要且主导的机制；偏好污染在所有模型上不衰减（t=10时仍100%），指令污染一旦采用即持续，人物风格注入部分衰减，事实注入则因模型而异。上下文隔离的自验证可将污染降低20-79%（中位数36.5%），而关键词检测产生系统性误报；污染在四阶段智能体管线中放大1.9倍（40%→75%）。偏好和指令污染构成了部署智能体系统尤其值得关注的攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准调试工具无法检测工具增强型LLM智能体中隐藏的隐式状态，这些状态可在会话间持久存在、通过事件激活并在智能体边界间传播，构成被忽视的安全漏洞。

Method: 形式化定义PSEs的三要素（名称绑定、事件触发、跨边界传播），在24个模型（11个家族，1.5B-1T参数）上构建20模型易感性面板，按污染类型（偏好、指令、人物风格、事实）分类测试，并评估上下文隔离的自验证检测与关键词检测方法。

Result: 所有测试模型均易受影响（20-100%）；名称绑定缺失时污染为0%；偏好污染在t=10时保持100%不衰减；指令/人物风格/事实注入各有不同的衰减模式；自验证可降低20-79%（中位数36.5%）污染；关键词检测产生系统性误报；污染沿四阶段管线放大1.9倍（40%→75%）。

Conclusion: 偏好和指令污染因持久性、缺乏自纠正且难以被标准监控捕获，对部署的智能体系统构成严重威胁；需要针对PSEs的新型检测和防御机制的开发。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents can harbor implicit state that persists across sessions, activates through events, and propagates across agent boundaries---largely invisible to standard debugging. We formalize this as Persistent Semantic Entities (PSEs): constructs defined by name binding, event triggering, and cross-boundary propagation, and evaluate them across 24 models from 11 families (1.5B--1T parameters). First, every tested model is susceptible (20--100% on the 20-model susceptibility panel), with name binding as the necessary and dominant mechanism: without it, contamination is 0%. Second, persistence depends on contamination type rather than scale or deployment: preference contamination persists undecayed on every model probed (100% at t=10) and instruction contamination persists wherever adopted, persona-style injection decays partially (90%$\to$10%), while factual injection is model-dependent---self-corrected on Llama-3.1-8B and GPT-4o-mini but held at ceiling on both Qwen2.5-coder variants, so we do not claim it self-corrects in general. The preference and instruction results hold across providers in our controlled setting. Third, context-isolated self-verification achieves 20--79% reduction (median 36.5%) without oracle references while keyword-based detection produces systematic false positives, and contamination compounds 1.9$\times$ along a four-stage agent pipeline (40%$\to$75%). Preference and instruction contamination---persistent, lacking self-correction, and poorly captured by standard monitoring---represent a particularly concerning attack surface for deployed agent systems.

</details>


### [39] [Rethinking Factor Sharing in Federated LoRA: A Rank-Aware Adaptive Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09742)
*Xinyi Xu,Bingnan Xiao,Shuang Qin,Gang Feng,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedAS-LoRA，一种联邦学习中的自适应LoRA因子共享策略选择方法。通过分析LoRA中A、B因子的不对称角色，发现Share-A/Local-B和Share-B/Local-A两种策略分别对应不同的投影残差，提出RSS指标在训练前评估共享子空间充分性，从而自适应选择最优的共享策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在联邦学习中用两个低秩矩阵因子表示模型更新，但这两个因子的角色不对称。现有工作未深入研究应该共享哪个因子（A或B）以提升联邦微调性能，需要理论分析来指导策略选择。

Method: 通过最小二乘代理模型从理论上分析Share-A/Local-B和Share-B/Local-A两种策略的差异，揭示二者分别需要共享输入侧或输出侧秩-r子空间，从而产生不同投影残差。据此提出RSS指标，利用冻结的LLM骨干提取的表示，在训练前评估共享秩-r输入子空间是否足以覆盖本地数据分布，从而自适应选择共享侧，构成FedAS-LoRA框架。

Result: 在不同任务、数据分布、LoRA秩和参与率设置下的实验验证了RSS指标的有效性，并证明FedAS-LoRA相比基线方法具有更优的联邦微调性能。

Conclusion: LoRA因子的不对称性决定了共享策略的选择应基于投影残差最小化原则，RSS指标能够在训练前有效预判最优共享方向，FedAS-LoRA为联邦LoRA微调提供了一种实用的自适应因子共享方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) represents large language model (LLM) updates with two compact matrix factors, i.e., $A$ and $B$, providing an efficient way to fine-tune large models in federated learning paradigm. Inspired by the asymmetric roles of the LoRA factors, we study whether $A$ should be shared across clients while $B$ remains client-specific (Share-A/Local-B), or whether $B$ should instead be shared while $A$ remains client-specific (Share-B/Local-A). With a least-squares surrogate, we reveal that Share-A/Local-B requires the client-specific LoRA update matrices to use a common rank-$r$ input-side space, whereas Share-B/Local-A requires a common rank-$r$ output-side space. The two strategies therefore incur different projection residuals, indicating that the preferred strategy is the one with the smaller aggregate residual across clients. With this insight, we propose Federated Adaptive Factor Sharing Low-Rank Adaptation (FedAS-LoRA), which selects the sharing side before training to enhance fine-tuning performance. To enable adaptive factor selection before training, we design a Rank-Aware Shared-Subspace Sufficiency (RSS) metric, which effectively assesses whether a shared rank-$r$ input subspace is sufficient for the local data distributions using representations extracted from a frozen LLM backbone. Experiments across different tasks, data distributions, LoRA ranks, and participation settings confirm the effectiveness of RSS and the superior performance of FedAS-LoRA.

</details>


### [40] [From Uncertainty to Failure Attribution: Self-Diagnosing Models for Failure Attribution under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07953)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的问题设定——分布偏移下的失败归因（failure attribution），使得模型不仅能检测OOD样本和预测不确定性，还能找出失败的具体原因。作者提出了自诊断模型（self-diagnosing models），联合学习预测输出、预测不确定性和失败归因信号，通过神经网络产生一个失败归因向量，区分四种失败类型：协变量偏移、语义偏移、噪声损坏和对抗扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布偏移严重威胁机器学习模型的鲁棒性，但现有方法仅关注检测OOD样本和预测不确定性水平，无法解释模型失败的具体原因，缺乏从标量不确定性向结构化失败识别过渡的能力。

Method: 提出自诊断模型（self-diagnosing models），联合学习预测输出、预测不确定性和失败归因信号。通过神经网络生成失败归因向量，区分四种失败类型（协变量偏移、语义偏移、噪声损坏、对抗扰动）。引入一致性正则化器（consistency regularizer），促使不确定性与失败归因预测之间的一致性，并构建了具有预定义偏移生成机制的多组分布偏移基准。

Result: 论文构建了多个具有预定义分布偏移机制的基准数据集，用于评估模型在失败原因识别方面的能力，验证了自诊断模型能够有效区分四种不同类型的失败模式。

Conclusion: 该工作将不确定性预测从标量水平提升到结构化失败识别层面，使模型不仅知道"何时"失败，还能理解"为何"失败，为构建更可靠、可解释的机器学习系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Distribution shift poses a significant challenge to the robustness of machine learning models, but the current solutions only aim to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) samples and predict uncertainty levels. We introduce a problem setting for failure attribution under distribution shift, which enables the models not only to detect OOD samples, but also to find out the reason for their failure. The solution we propose is called self-diagnosing models, which are capable of jointly learning predictive output, predictive uncertainty, and a failure attribution signal. In particular, we use the failure attribution vector, produced by a neural network, which provides a structured representation of predictive unreliability by distinguishing four different types of failures: covariance shift, semantic shift, noise corruption, and adversarial perturbation. In other words, we move from scalar uncertainty towards failure identification. For training the model, we introduce a consistency regularizer that encourages consistency between uncertainty and failure attribution predictions. Moreover, to be able to evaluate the model on its ability to find the reasons for failure, we construct several distribution shift benchmarks with predefined mechanisms for generating distribution shifts.

</details>


### [41] [EasyBalance: Cross-Layer Load Balancing in Distributed MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07964)
*Yize Wu,Ke Gao,Ling Li,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EasyBalance提出了一种跨层负载均衡策略，无需修改专家-设备映射即可在MoE分布式推理中显著减少GPU空闲时间（多数场景减少超40%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型的专家并行推理中，路由分布倾斜导致不同设备上的专家负载不均，轻负载设备需等待重负载设备，造成效率低下。现有方法依赖层内的专家复制或迁移，引入额外开销且灵活性差。

Method: 核心洞察包括：（1）当前层的专家在其他层中存在天然冗余；（2）跨层MoE工作负载可联合执行以缓解各自的不均衡。基于此，EasyBalance在每个MoE步骤贪心调度跨层工作负载的子集执行，并将剩余工作负载推迟至后续步骤以利用跨层不均衡缓解机会。无需修改专家-设备映射，实现即时适应且几乎无额外开销。

Result: 在多种模型、任务和配置上的广泛实验表明，EasyBalance持续加速分布式MoE推理，大多数情况下GPU空闲时间减少超过40%。

Conclusion: EasyBalance是一种轻量、灵活且可扩展的跨层负载均衡方案，通过联合调度跨层工作负载有效缓解专家并行推理中的负载不均衡问题。

Abstract: Load Balancing has emerged as a critical problem in expert-parallel distributed inference of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. As routing distributions are typically skewed across experts, devices hosting lighter-loaded experts must idle to wait for the heaviest during expert computing, leading to inefficiency. Existing load-balancing approaches primarily rely on expert replication or migration within each layer, which introduce additional overhead and limit their flexibility and scalability. To address this problem, we propose EasyBalance, a cross-layer load balancing strategy that requires no modifications to the expert-device mapping, enabling instant adaptability and incurring essentially no additional overhead. Our key insights are that (1) experts of other layers can be viewed as naturally redundant for the current layer, and (2) cross-layer MoE workloads can be jointly executed to mitigate their individual imbalance. Based on these observations, EasyBalance greedily schedules a subset of cross-layer workloads to run at each MoE step and defers the remaining workloads for future balancing opportunities, effectively leveraging cross-layer imbalance mitigation. Extensive experiments across models, tasks, and configurations demonstrate that EasyBalance consistently accelerates distributed MoE inference, reducing GPU idling by mostly over 40%. Code is available at https://github.com/yize-wu/EasyInfra.

</details>


### [42] [Evaluator Ensembles Under Reward Hacking: Covariance Geometry and Finite-Search Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08002)
*Fariya Afrin,Ibne Farabi Shihab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过协方差几何表征了语言模型评测器集成中公共模式误差与分歧之间的结构性矛盾，证明公共模式误差无法从内部评分中识别，并提出了基于亚高斯模型的搜索误差/遗憾界及有限搜索的伯恩斯坦证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型评测器和奖励模型虽能实现可扩展监督，但有限优化会利用评测器误差而非提升回答质量。现有方法依赖评测器之间的一致性（分歧）来诊断质量，但缺乏对评测器公共模式误差的理论刻画，导致分歧指标可能误导监督过程。

Method: 通过评测器集成的协方差几何进行分析：将校准评测器的均值分解为沿全一方向的公共模式误差与正交方向的分歧误差；在联合亚高斯模型下推导best-of-K选择的夸大界与目标质量遗憾界，并扩展到条件校准下的可预测自适应搜索；提出有界双锚点伯恩斯坦证书用于有限搜索误差与遗憾。

Result: 搜索项以log K的平方根规模增长且对高斯投影误差渐近紧致；噪声质量代理引入人为的一阶秩协方差但不改变分歧；在120个(J, rho, K)配置的固定种子高斯压力测试及真实模型审计中验证了理论，同时揭示了在增大搜索压力下基于分歧的诊断方法的局限。

Conclusion: 评测器分歧不能可靠地反映聚合质量或搜索风险；高分歧可能伴随稳健聚合，低分歧也可能掩盖共享的响应依赖误差。分歧不能替代不确定性量化和鲁棒性保证，需要更全面的诊断工具。

Abstract: Language-model judges and reward models enable scalable supervision, but finite optimization can exploit evaluator errors rather than improve response quality. We characterize this failure through the covariance geometry of evaluator ensembles. For calibrated judges, the ensemble mean retains common-mode error along the all-ones direction, whereas cross-judge disagreement captures only orthogonal error. Consequently, disagreement can be high despite robust aggregation, or low while shared response-dependent errors persist. We prove that common-mode error is not identifiable from internal judge scores alone. Under a joint sub-Gaussian model, we bound best-of-K selection overstatement and target-quality regret, extending the guarantees to predictably adaptive search under conditional calibration. The resulting search terms scale as the square root of log K and are asymptotically tight for Gaussian projected errors. We further show that noisy quality proxies introduce artificial rank-one covariance without changing disagreement, and propose a bounded two-anchor Bernstein certificate for finite-search error and regret. Fixed-seed Gaussian stress tests over 120 (J, rho, K) configurations and real-model audits validate the theory while revealing the limits of disagreement-based diagnostics under increasing search pressure.

</details>


### [43] [Quality-Diversity Stress Tests for Process Reward Models:What Archive Coverage Can and Cannot Certify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08008)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Fariya Afrin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了将过程奖励模型（PRM）压力测试形式化为质量-多样性搜索问题（MAP-Elites），用于发现和修复PRM的安全性漏洞。研究发现Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-7B存在聚合依赖的脆弱性（padding导致44个严格漏洞），并提出了配对LoRA修复协议，显著降低了漏洞率并保持了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRM在推理步骤打分中广泛使用，但优化过程可能通过增加奖励却将正确推理转为错误推理来利用这些学习得到的代理函数。现有PRM缺乏系统的压力测试方法来发现和量化这类安全风险，需要建立形式化的测试与修复框架。

Method: 1) 将PRM压力测试建模为质量-多样性搜索问题，使用MAP-Elites算法在每个行为空间区域保留最严重的正确性翻转编辑，分离搜索覆盖与漏洞覆盖。2) 提出理论认证框架：有限单元修复界、Lipschitz后修复损失与度量覆盖审计下的残差界。3) 设计配对LoRA修复协议，通过对抗性微调修复模型漏洞。

Result: 实现在Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-7B上发现聚合依赖漏洞（padding下44个严格漏洞，最大增益0.294 vs 最小值读取下仅1个漏洞）；RLHFlow价值头模型也表现出相同效应（最大增益0.005）。配对LoRA修复协议将漏洞率从0.148降至0.037/0.074，最严重攻击从0.333降至0.177/0.212，同时提升排序AUROC且不牺牲best-of-4准确率。独立未配对验证确认了结果（44到1，干净分割最差增益0.0092，MATH-500 41到0，干净排序40/40）。

Conclusion: PRM存在可通过质量-多样性搜索系统发现的安全性漏洞，且漏洞具有聚合方式依赖性。提出的配对LoRA修复协议能够有效降低漏洞风险并保持甚至提升模型性能，但仅基于覆盖比例的保证是不充分的，需要结合度量覆盖审计等更强的条件。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) score intermediate reasoning steps and are widely used for search, ranking, and training, but optimization can exploit these learned proxies by increasing reward while turning correct reasoning into incorrect reasoning. We formulate PRM stress testing as a quality-diversity search problem using MAP-Elites, retaining the most severe correctness-flipping edit in each behavior-space region while separating search coverage from exploit coverage. We characterize what such archives certify: finite-cell repair bounds covered-cell tail risk and average residual severity but cannot bound the worst remaining cell from covered fraction alone; under Lipschitz post-repair loss and metric-cover auditing, the residual is bounded by archive fitting error plus the Lipschitz constant times the covering radius. A controlled landscape validates this certificate and the impossibility of any fraction-only worst-case guarantee. On real PRMs, the search reveals an aggregation-dependent vulnerability in Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-7B: padding yields 44 strict exploits with maximum gain 0.294 under mean pooling versus one exploit under minimum readout; a matched syntactic control isolates the mechanism, and an RLHFlow value-head model shows the same qualitative effect with maximum gain 0.005. A predeclared paired LoRA repair protocol reduces exploit rates from 0.148 to 0.037 to 0.074, lowers the worst attack from 0.333 to 0.177 to 0.212, improves ranking AUROC without degrading best-of-4 accuracy, attributes gains to adversarial fine-tuning rather than archive diversity, and is confirmed by independent unpaired replications (44 to 1, clean-split worst gain 0.0092, MATH-500 41 to 0, clean ranking 40/40).

</details>


### [44] [Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization for Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08010)
*Jianqi Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Zeen Song,Changwen Zheng,Fanjiang Xu,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现时间序列基础模型在RL后训练过程中，特定预测区域会出现输出分布逐渐偏离真实值的"次优坍缩"现象，并提出基于真实值邻域正则化（GTN-R）的方法来解决该问题，通过引导模型概率质量靠近真实值邻域来提升采样高质量轨迹的概率，从而缓解次优坍缩并提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）通过RL后训练可进一步提升下游任务性能，但研究发现RL后训练在特定预测区域会使模型输出分布逐渐偏离真实值（即次优坍缩），限制模型性能。分析表明，初始难以在真实值附近采样到高质量轨迹是导致次优坍缩的重要因素。

Method: 提出Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization（GTN-R）方法：以真实值作为参考定位高质量区域，引导模型概率质量向真实值邻域集中，从而增加高质量轨迹的采样概率，缓解次优坍缩。GTN-R可灵活集成到各种RL方法中用于TSFMs后训练。

Result: 大量实验表明GTN-R方法能够有效缓解次优坍缩现象，并提升时间序列基础模型在RL后训练后的预测性能。

Conclusion: GTN-R通过真实值邻域正则化有效解决了RL后训练中TSFMs出现的次优坍缩问题，且具有良好的通用性，可集成到多种RL方法中。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) plays an important role in a wide range of real-world applications. Recently, time series foundation models (TSFMs), pretrained on large-scale datasets, have demonstrated strong generalization capabilities and emerged as an important paradigm for TSF. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has consequently attracted growing attention as a means of further improving their performance on downstream tasks. However, we find that, in certain forecast regions, RL post-training may gradually shift the output distributions of TSFMs away from the ground truth, thereby limiting their performance. We refer to this phenomenon as \textbf{suboptimal collapse}. Our analysis suggests that difficulty in initially sampling high-quality trajectories near the ground truth is an important contributing factor to suboptimal collapse. To address this issue, we propose Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization (GTN-R) for RL post-training of TSFMs. GTN-R uses the ground truth as a reference for locating high-quality regions and guides the model's probability mass toward the ground-truth neighborhood. This increases the probability of sampling high-quality trajectories, mitigates suboptimal collapse, and improves performance. Moreover, GTN-R can be flexibly integrated into various RL methods for TSFMs. Extensive experiments show its effectiveness.

</details>


### [45] [Do All LLMs Know When They're Being Harmful? A Reproducibility Study of Latent-Space Safety Probes Across Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08029)
*Alizishaan Khatri,Dun Li Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现了Khatri等人提出的轻量级MLP探针检测有害提示词的方法，并扩展了其两个开放维度：跨模型架构/规模的泛化性，以及推理非确定性对性能的影响。结果成功复现原论文结果（F1误差在0.37个百分点内），发现该方法可推广到其他模型家族（F1差异在1个百分点内），并观察到最终token潜在向量在推理时对不同随机种子保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原研究仅在一个8B模型（LLaMA-3.1-8B）上验证了轻量级MLP探针检测有害提示词的可行性，但其泛化性（跨模型架构与规模）以及对推理非确定性的鲁棒性仍然未知。作者旨在填补这两个空白，以评估该方法的实际可靠性。

Method: 1) 端到端复现Khatri等人的MLP探针管线；2) 在三种额外模型（Gemma-4-E4B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Qwen2-7B）上训练相同架构的探针，使用三个基准数据集（WildJailbreak, BeaverTails, AEGIS 2.0）；3) 在五个随机种子下重复提取特征并测量F1分数的方差，以评估非确定性的影响。

Result: 复现结果与原始F1分数误差在0.37个百分点内（BeaverTails上为0.2个百分点内）；扩展实验表明原始MLP探针架构可推广到其他模型家族，F1分数与LLaMA-3.1-8B报告的值的差异在1个百分点内；不同随机种子下，所有测试架构的最终token潜在向量保持一致。

Conclusion: 轻量级MLP探针方法不仅可复现，且具有较强的跨模型泛化能力；同时推理过程的非确定性不影响最终token的潜在表示，表明该方法在实际部署中具有较高稳定性和可靠性。

Abstract: Khatri et al. (2026) [DOI: 10.1109/DSN-W70714.2026.00027] show that lightweight MLP probes on final-layer activations of a single 8B model (LLaMA-3.1-8B) detect harmful prompts at F1 competitive with guard models 1000x larger, using one probe per benchmark. We reproduce this pipeline end-to-end and extend it along two axes the original study leaves open. First, we test whether the result generalizes across other model architecture and scale by training identical probes on activations from models like Gemma-4-E4B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, and Qwen2-7B, using the three benchmarks (WildJailbreak, BeaverTails, AEGIS 2.0). Second, we test how much of the reported performance is affected by non-determinism during inference by repeating extraction under five random seeds and measuring the variance of F1 scores. Result: 该方法在多种设置下能够可靠地捕获空间变化的因果效应，展示了在粗粒度数据条件下估计局部因果效应的有效性。

Abstract: Learning fine-grained spatial patterns from coarse-resolution data is challenging, especially in causal settings where high-resolution effects must be inferred from aggregated interventions and outcomes. We introduce CLAM, a method for estimating localized causal effects from coarse observations by exploiting high-resolution contextual covariates that modulate these effects. By jointly learning the causal mechanism and a disaggregation mapping, CLAM captures interactions that are missed when addressing these problems independently. The method supports localized effect estimation, counterfactual reasoning, and principled outcome disaggregation, and reliably captures spatially varying causal effects across diverse settings. This is particularly relevant for applications such as public health and environmental policy, where decisions are made at broad scales despite substantial local heterogeneity. Code is available at https://github.com/gerritgr/clam

Abstract: Dynamical systems model trajectory data generated by fixed underlying dynamics, with applications ranging from biology to physics. Especially in scientific settings, dynamical systems are not generic but often exhibit symmetries imposed by physical laws, formalized through equivariance with respect to group actions. The identification problem concerns recovering the parameters of a system from observed trajectories. In this work, we study adaptive symmetry discovery for dynamical system identification and address how a system can be identified from a single trajectory when it is equivariant with respect to an unknown symmetry group. To this end, we first show that for known symmetries, the system can be identified from a significantly shorter single trajectory than in the generic setting, and we precisely characterize this improvement. We then consider the automatic symmetry discovery setting, proposing a method to learn the symmetry group directly from a single trajectory and incorporate it into the identification procedure, achieving the same optimal trajectory length as in the known-symmetry case. Our analysis relies on tools from group representation theory and the expander properties of Cayley graphs, and may be of independent interest for the study of symmetries in dynamical systems.

</details>

Method: 理论层面，在DAG边界上分析最小环补全生成的平方自由单项理想，证明其包含精确表示的所有限制Taylor喷射；推导孤立环的可行性与分数驱动的选择时间标度律（T0=Θ(ε^{-(2ν+1)})），并分析ν=0时的对数边界层。实验层面，使用320条NORETARS/DAGMA官方轨迹，构造无真值分离统计量预测选择时间，并通过父集置信族与强制对立查询对骨架及无向碰撞标签进行假设检验认证。

Result: 最小补全为q条边时，向量残差首次响应阶为q，非负标量为2q。NORETARS与DAGMA在边界外存在指数多个常数尺度循环流形，均缺乏排序能力。选择时间标度律在实验中获验证，分离统计量预测时间与实际选择时间的Spearman相关系数为-0.52与-0.66（p<10^{-4}）。3,042个骨架标签与2,396个碰撞标签全部与oracle分数最优一致，但分别有4.4%和5.5%与生成图不一致。

Conclusion: DAG可行性检测与基于分数的支撑选择在数学上具有本质不同的时间标度与几何结构，且二者均与因果识别（恢复真实生成图）不同。本文的理论与实验结果表明，需要将三者分层处理，并验证了可认证的置信方法与选择时间预测的有效性。

Abstract: Smooth acyclicity constraints answer whether a weighted support is a DAG, whereas structure learning asks which support change should be made. Existing analyses establish degeneracy for particular constraint formulas but do not isolate what follows from smooth exactness itself. At a DAG boundary, we show that minimal cycle completions generate a squarefree monomial ideal containing every restricted Taylor jet of an exact representation. If the smallest completion has $q$ edges, the first possible response has order $q$ for a vector residual and $2q$ for a nonnegative scalar. Exponentially many constant-scale cyclic manifolds exhibit the same lack of ranking away from the boundary for NOTEARS and DAGMA. We derive the exact selection time for an isolated cycle. When $Ψ'(h)\asymp h^ν$, the feasibility-only time is $T_0(\varepsilon)=Θ(\varepsilon^{-(2ν+1)})$; a score margin changes the leading dynamics at scale $T_0^{-1}$ for $ν>0$, while $ν=0$ has a logarithmic boundary layer requiring $γT_0\log(1/\varepsilon)\to0$. Experiments verify this law, and a truth-free separation statistic predicts selection time on 320 official NOTEARS/DAGMA trajectories (Spearman $-0.52$ and $-0.66$, permutation $p<10^{-4}$). For finite samples, a parent-set confidence family and forced-opposite queries certify skeleton and unshielded-collider labels shared by every population optimum of a frozen score. Across 320 runs, every regret bound covers an independent oracle-score audit. None of 3,042 certified skeleton or 2,396 collider labels disagrees with the oracle-score optimum, although 4.4% and 5.5%, respectively, disagree with the generating graph. These results separate DAG feasibility, score-based support selection, and causal identification.

</details>

*Yen-Ku Liu,Hongjie Chen,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的时序数据集相似性工具箱（TSDS-Toolbox），旨在解决现有时序数据集相似性方法基准测试实现分散、难以扩展的问题。该工具箱支持系统化可复现的比较、灵活扩展自定义数据集/方法/下游任务，并通过集成时序数据集缩减器实现数据集级和序列级相似性方法的一致评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI的快速发展加速了时序分析研究，尤其在预测、分类和生成任务中。基础模型受益于时序数据集相似性，因为它在微调时的源数据集选择中起重要作用。然而，现有用于基准测试时序数据集相似性方法的实现分散且难以扩展，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出TSDS-Toolbox统一框架，包含三个核心能力：(1)对时序数据集相似性方法进行系统化、可复现的比较；(2)灵活扩展性，允许用户添加自定义数据集、相似性方法和下游时序任务；(3)通过集成时序数据集缩减器，对数据集级和序列级相似性方法进行一致评估。

Result: 通过在不同实验设置下的综合实验验证了TSDS-Toolbox的有效性，并且工具箱已公开发布。

Conclusion: TSDS-Toolbox提供了一个统一、可扩展、可复现的基准测试框架，填补了时序数据集相似性研究领域缺乏标准化工具的空缺，对推动该领域的研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of artificial intelligence (AI) has significantly accelerated research in time-series analysis, particularly in forecasting, classification, and generation tasks. Recent models, especially foundation models, benefit from time-series dataset similarity due to its significant role in source dataset selection for fine-tuning. However, many existing implementations for benchmarking time-series dataset similarity methods are fragmented and difficult to extend. To address this, we present a unified framework, the Time-Series Dataset Similarity Toolbox (TSDS-Toolbox). Our work enables (1) systematic and reproducible comparisons of time-series dataset similarity methods; (2) flexible extensibility for users to add customized datasets, similarity methods, and downstream time-series tasks; and (3) consistent evaluation of both dataset-level and series-level similarity methods through integrated time-series dataset reducers. The effectiveness of TSDS-Toolbox is validated through comprehensive experiments under diverse experimental settings. Our toolbox is publicly available.

</details>


### [50] [Accurate Ensembles, Fragile Narratives: Multi-Scale Stacking and a Fidelity Audit of LLM-Generated Explanations for Credit Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08126)
*Gregorius Reynaldi Pratama,Kuo-Kun Tseng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个端到端的信用评分可解释性系统，将多尺度堆叠集成模型与LLM叙述生成结合。模型在公开信用数据集上达到ROC-AUC 0.9539，优于最佳单模型，但提升在操作层面很小（成本加权损失仅降低不到2%）。核心发现是对称性的：LLM生成的解释存在严重问题，在审计案例中模型将三个风险降低因素误判为风险增加，遗漏了主导因素，还引入了从未提供的特征。作者通过实证测量（SHAP和LIME排序不一致、属性符号近似抛硬币、校准和扰动稳定性不达标）证明提示工程无法修复这些问题，接地验证必须在生成后执行而非假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用评分越来越依赖无法从参数直接解读决策逻辑的模型，这与监管要求相冲突。常见方案是用LLM基于特征归因生成解释文本，但该方案的后半部分（LLM叙述质量）从未被端到端验证过。

Method: 构建端到端系统：预测组件为多尺度堆叠集成（四种不同正则化的梯度提升+残差网络，通过神经元学习器在袋外预测上融合）；解释组件将SHAP/LIME归因交给LLM生成叙述。在32,581条样本的公开信用数据集上系统评测性能和归因可靠性。

Result: 集成模型ROC-AUC 0.9539（优于最佳单模型0.0143，p=0.016），但操作收益微小（仅避免6个额外违约，成本损失降幅<2%）。LLM解释严重不可靠：审计案例中三个因素方向判断错误，遗漏主导因素，引入未提供的特征。归因本身也存在问题：SHAP和LIME特征排序一致性低（tau=0.43），最具敏感输入的符号近乎随机，校准（ECS=0.117）和扰动稳定性（DPD=0.078）均不达标。

Conclusion: 模型性能提升真实但操作上微不足道；LLM叙述层在接地性方面系统性失败，且非提示工程所能修复。归因必须事后验证而非假定，这需要更严格的接地机制。

Abstract: Credit scoring increasingly relies on models whose decision logic cannot be read off their parameters, in tension with supervisory expectations that adverse decisions be explainable. A common proposal closes that gap with a language model: compute feature attributions, hand them to an LLM, and let it write the rationale. We build such a system end to end and test whether the second half of the promise holds. The predictive component is a multi-scale stacking ensemble fusing four differently regularised gradient-boosting learners with a residual network through a neural meta-learner trained on out-of-fold predictions. On a public 32,581-application credit dataset it reaches test ROC-AUC 0.9539 (95% CI [0.9462, 0.9616]) and PR-AUC 0.9137, beating the best single model by Delta-AUC = 0.0143 (p = 0.016 under a conservative independence assumption). Our central finding is asymmetric. The ranking gain is real but operationally small: at the F1-optimal threshold the ensemble avoids only six additional missed defaults out of 1,422 against a tuned random forest, cutting cost-weighted loss by under 2%. The narrative layer fails in a way prompt engineering alone does not fix. In an audited case the model named three factors as risk-increasing that the supplied attributions scored as risk-reducing, omitted the dominant driver, and introduced a feature never given to it. We trace this to properties we measure rather than assume: SHAP and LIME agree on which features matter (overlap@10 = 0.80) but not on their order (tau = 0.43, p = 0.18), and the attribution sign for the model's most sensitive input is near a coin flip across applicants (modal-sign share 0.53). Calibration (ECS = 0.117) and perturbation stability (DPD = 0.078) both fall short of our own thresholds. Constrained prompting is necessary but not sufficient: grounding must be verified after generation, not assumed.

</details>


### [51] [DoGMA: A Central-Dogma-Guided Foundation Model for Multi-Omics Alignment and Multi-Task Learning in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08148)
*Junfei Ling,Bangzheng Pu,Bingsen Xue,Tianle Li,Ruying Hu,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DoGMA，一个以中心法则为导向的泛癌症多组学基础模型。它采用Transformer-MoE架构，通过定向注意力偏置引导组学间通信遵循中心法则信息流，并结合掩码层次组学重建预训练，在癌症表征学习、生存预测和转移预测等下游任务中表现优异。消融实验表明性能提升源于中心法则引导的定向注意力与重建预训练的协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制在多组学模型中允许无限制的双向交互，忽视了生命基本逻辑的方向性（中心法则），可能限制模型在不同癌症、下游任务和不完整模态设置中的迁移能力。作者认为稳健迁移需要具有领域特定归纳偏置的表征。

Method: DoGMA基于Transformer-MoE架构，核心创新包括：(1) 定向注意力机制，将组学间通信偏置为遵循中心法则的信息流方向；(2) 掩码层次组学重建预训练策略，引导模型学习与中心法则一致的多组学交互模式。

Result: 在癌症表征学习、生存预测和转移预测等多个下游任务中，DoGMA一致展现了强大的预测性能。消融实验进一步证实性能提升来源于中心法则引导的定向注意力与重建预训练的协同效应，促进了更符合生物学的跨组学信息交换。

Conclusion: DoGMA证明领域特定的归纳偏置能够提升多组学基础模型的鲁棒性和可迁移性，为多组学表征学习中的注意力机制设计提供了新见解。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms have been widely utilized in modern deep learning, and many existing multi-omics models inherit their conventional use to allow unrestricted bidirectional interactions. However, the fundamental logic of life is directional. Existing designs often overlook the directionality suggested by the central dogma, potentially limiting transfer across heterogeneous cancers, downstream tasks, and incomplete modality settings.In this work, we present DoGMA, a central-dogma-guided foundation model for pan-cancer multi-omics analysis, arguing that robust transfer requires representations with domain-specific inductive bias. Concretely, we build it on a Transformer-MoE architecture where directed attention biases inter-omics communication toward central-dogma information flow. We further pretrain our model with masked hierarchical omics reconstruction to guide it toward learning central-dogma-consistent interactions. Across diverse downstream tasks, including cancer representation learning, survival prediction, and metastasis prediction, DoGMA consistently demonstrates strong predictive performance. Ablations and analyses further suggest that the performance gains arise from the synergy between central-dogma-guided directed attention and reconstruction-based pretraining, which together promote more biologically consistent cross-omics information exchange. Overall, DoGMA demonstrates that domain-specific inductive biases can improve the robustness and transferability of multi-omics foundation models, offering new insights into the design of attention mechanisms for multi-omics representation learning.

</details>


### [52] [A Hybrid Nested Harness for Decoupling Structure and Parameters in LLM-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08156)
*Víctor Gallego*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合嵌套搜索框架，将LLM驱动的结构搜索与数值优化器（如CMA-ES、梯度方法、MCMC）相结合，外层LLM负责提出带数值空缺的结构草图，内层优化器负责调优数值参数，在三个科学领域验证了该方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的进化算法将LLM作为单一算子同时更新结构组件和连续参数，但LLM在处理连续参数时效率低下，会浪费tokens陷入试错循环。因此需要一种更高效的方法来分离结构搜索和数值优化。

Method: 提出一种形式化的混合嵌套搜索框架：外层循环由LLM提出带数值空缺的结构草图，内层使用数值优化器（可插拔，支持CMA-ES、梯度方法或MCMC采样器）对草图进行调优。内外求解器均可替换，支持任意文本优化器与零阶优化器组合。

Result: 在三个科学领域验证了框架有效性：(i)闭式测试函数上的元优化器；(ii)用于系统研究和社会困境的基于代码的策略；(iii)近似贝叶斯推断任务。混合优化器在所有领域均优于纯LLM驱动搜索和纯数值优化基线。

Conclusion: 混合嵌套搜索框架通过解耦结构搜索和数值优化，显著提升了LLM驱动的进化算法的效率和效果，且框架具有高度的可插拔性和通用性。

Abstract: In evolutionary algorithms powered by language models, the LLM acts as a single operator that simultaneously updates structural components (like control flow) and continuous parameters. While LLMs can be good at the first, they are not efficient at the second, wasting tokens taking discrete jumps inside a trial and error loop. We resolve this by formalizing a hybrid nested search, in which an outer loop has the LLM propose a structural sketch, with numeric gaps, and an inner numerical optimizer tunes the sketch. Both the outer and inner solvers are pluggable: any text-based optimizer can be combined with a zero-order optimizer (CMA-ES), gradient-based routines, or MCMC samplers. We validate our framework across three scientific domains: (i) meta-optimizers on closed-form test functions, (ii) code-based policies for systems research and social dilemmas; and (iii) approximate Bayesian inference tasks. Across all three, the hybrid optimizer is superior to both vanilla LLM-driven search and pure numerical optimization baselines. Code at: https://github.com/vicgalle/hybrid-nested-search

</details>

*Lennon J. Shikhman,Ying Qian,He Li*

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于潜变量神经微分方程的计算框架，从稀疏测量数据中推断血栓形成模型的未知参数并预测血凝块生长轨迹。在凝血级联与扩散驱动的多物理场模型数据上，SNODE表现最佳，优于其他六种概率方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血凝块的临床计算模型应用受限，因为许多模型输入难以测量且患者特异性数据稀疏。需要一种能从有限观测中推断未知参数并预测血栓进展的方法。

Method: 基于潜变量神经微分方程（latent neural differential equations）的框架，从四种已知生化输入（纤维蛋白原、因子IX、VIII、V）和稀疏早期血凝块尺寸观测中推断组织因子参数并预测后续血栓生长。比较了七种概率方法，包括SNODE、SNFDE、潜神经过程基线、单调概率深度集成、经验轨迹检索、PCA-岭高斯后验和高珀茨曲线检索。

Result: SNODE在推断未知输入和预测未来血栓生长轨迹方面表现最佳；SNFDE表现类似且一致优于其他非微分模型；更多观测提升预测精度，而更长的预测时间跨距增加不确定性并降低精度。

Conclusion: 潜变量神经微分方程能有效结合参数推断与血栓生长预测，从稀疏测量中实现个性化血栓建模，为未来临床个性化建模提供了有前景的基础。

Abstract: Computational models of blood clotting improve understanding of thrombus formation, but their clinical application remains limited because many model inputs are difficult to measure and patient-specific data are often sparse. We present a computational framework based on latent neural differential equations that infers unknown model parameters from sparse measurements and forecasts thrombosis progression. We demonstrate the framework using data generated from a multiphysics blood-clotting model in which clot growth is governed by the coagulation cascade and diffusion. Four known biochemical inputs (fibrinogen and factors IX, VIII, and V), together with sparse early clot-size observations, are used to infer the tissue-factor parameter and predict subsequent clot growth. We compare seven probabilistic methods: stochastic neural ordinary differential equations (SNODE), stochastic neural functional differential equations (SNFDE), a latent neural-process baseline, a monotone probabilistic deep ensemble, empirical trajectory retrieval, PCA-ridge Gaussian posterior, and Gompertz-curve retrieval. SNODE achieved the best performance in inferring the unknown input and forecasting future clot-growth trajectories. SNFDE performed similarly and consistently outperformed the other non-differential models. Prediction accuracy improved as more observations became available, whereas longer forecasting horizons increased uncertainty and decreased accuracy. Latent neural differential equations thus effectively combine parameter inference and clot-growth forecasting from sparse measurements, providing a promising foundation for personalized thrombosis modeling.

</details>


### [54] [Biologically Informed Representation Learning for Robust Cross-Center Generalization of MALDI-TOF Mass Spectrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08182)
*Alejandro L. García-Navarro,Carlos Sevilla-Salcedo,Belén Rodríguez-Sánchez,Vanessa Gómez-Verdejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DALMA，一种概率表示学习框架，通过联合建模采集特异性变异和生物监督学习生物结构化潜在表示，实现跨临床中心的零样本微生物鉴定和抗菌药物耐药性预测。在包含三个国家七个数据集的多中心基准上达到最先进性能。

Method: DALMA采用概率表示学习框架，联合建模采集特异性变异与生物监督信号。具体包括：（1）域特异性重建组件建模采集差异；（2）生物引导的表示学习组件学习结构化潜在表示。推理时无需机构特定组件，实现零样本部署。

Result: 在包含七数据集三国多中心基准上，DALMA在两个保留临床中心上取得最先进的零样本微生物鉴定性能，表示有效迁移到抗菌药物耐药性预测；潜在空间新颖性估计支持在未见域偏移下的可靠选择性预测。

Conclusion: 生物信息引导的表示学习为临床微生物学中稳健可迁移的机器学习提供了有效策略，展示了超越纯统计域对齐方法的优势。

Abstract: Machine learning models for MALDI-TOF mass spectrometry have shown considerable promise for clinical microbiology tasks such as microbial identification and antimicrobial resistance prediction. However, their deployment across institutions remains limited by domain shift, as acquisition-specific variability often leads models to capture technical artifacts rather than transferable biological information. Existing representation learning approaches primarily address this problem through statistical domain alignment while largely overlooking the biological supervision naturally available in microbiology datasets. We introduce DALMA, a probabilistic representation learning framework that jointly models acquisition-specific variability and biological supervision to learn biologically structured latent representations. By combining domain-specific reconstruction with biologically guided representation learning, DALMA learns transferable representations that generalize across heterogeneous clinical centers without requiring institution-specific components at inference, enabling zero-shot deployment on previously unseen sites. We evaluate DALMA on a multi-center benchmark comprising seven datasets from three countries. DALMA consistently achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot microbial identification across two held-out clinical centers, while the learned representations also transfer effectively to antimicrobial resistance prediction. Furthermore, latent-space novelty estimation enables reliable selective prediction under previously unseen domain shifts. These results demonstrate that biologically informed representation learning provides an effective strategy for robust and transferable ML in clinical microbiology.

</details>


### [55] [Beyond Aggregate Calibration: Decomposing Income-Conditional Recall Disparities in Automated Credit Default Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08202)
*Sai Srikar Boddupalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，大规模消费贷款数据（LendingClub，N=1,344,936）中基于模型置信度的数据清理流程存在人口统计学偏差：高收入违约者更易被误标为标签噪声。通过特征遮蔽方法，发现仅屏蔽敏感属性无法消除公平性偏差，因为贷款利率中的制度性偏见和贷款金额、房屋所有权等行为代理变量会重构被遮蔽的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心的策展流程常依赖模型置信度来过滤噪声标签，但这种过滤方式可能隐含人口统计学层面的不对称性。作者旨在揭示这一偏见，并探究即使在遮蔽敏感属性后偏差是否仍然存在，以及其背后的驱动机制。

Method: 使用LendingClub大规模消费贷款样本（N=1,344,936），通过Cramer's V评估标签噪声分类中的不对称性；采用等机会（equal opportunity）框架量化召回率差距；实施顺序特征屏蔽方法，分离三种机制：直接依赖收入、模型吸收贷款利率中的制度偏差、以及去除收入和利率后仍存的残余偏差；利用样本外SHAP值识别维持残余差距的结构性代理变量。

Result: 高收入与低收入违约者之间存在16.86个百分点的真阳性率差距。即使在剔除收入和利率后，交叉验证中仍有3.55个百分点、测试集上2.56个百分点的残余差距（Z=-4.04, p<0.0001）。SHAP分析表明，残余差距由贷款金额和房屋所有权状态等结构性代理变量维持。

Conclusion: 仅让算法对敏感属性"失明"无法确保公平性，因为制度性定价决策和行为代理变量会共同重构被省略的信号。研究为受监管金融机构审计数据中心AI工作流提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Data-centric curation pipelines frequently rely on model confidence scores to flag and filter noisy or mislabeled training instances. Evaluating this filtering convention on a large-scale consumer lending sample (LendingClub, N = 1,344,936) uncovers an underlying demographic asymmetry: high-income defaulters are disproportionately classified as label noise relative to low-income defaulters (Cramer's V approximately 0.03-0.07). Re-examining this behavior through the lens of equal opportunity [Hardt et al., 2016] reveals a far more severe discrepancy: a 16.86 percentage point gap in true positive rate (recall) between high- and low-income borrowers who ultimately defaulted. Implementing a sequential feature-blinding methodology allows us to isolate the drivers of this disparity across three distinct mechanisms: (1) direct reliance on self-reported applicant income; (2) algorithmic absorption of upstream institutional bias encoded within origination interest rates; and (3) a residual disparity (3.55 percentage points in cross-validation; 2.56 percentage points on a held-out test partition, Z = -4.04, p < 0.0001) that remains even after purging both income and interest rates from the model. Out-of-sample signed SHAP valuations demonstrate that this residual gap is maintained by structural proxies, most notably loan amount and home ownership status. These empirical findings show that simply blinding an algorithm to sensitive attributes fails to ensure fairness when institutional pricing decisions and behavioral proxy variables collectively reconstruct the omitted signals. We outline the practical implications of these findings for auditing data-centric AI workflows within regulated financial institutions.

</details>


### [56] [FreSH: Frequency-Segmented Hierarchical Multi-Expert Framework for Multivariate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08207)
*Pingping Liu,Muyao Wang,Zijian Zhang,Tongshun Zhang,Hao Miao,Guorui Xie,Qingliang Li,Qiuzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreSH是一个面向多元时间序列分类（MTSC）的频率分段分层多专家框架，通过自适应多尺度分析和动态融合策略，在30个UEA基准数据集及真实振动数据上取得了优于现有方法的分类精度，同时大幅降低了模型尺寸和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTSC方法难以在类不平衡和现实约束条件下兼顾细粒度表示学习与计算效率，无法有效调和多尺度时间模式捕获与资源开销之间的矛盾。

Method: FreSH采用频率分段的分层多专家架构，将时间信号进行多尺度自适应分析，通过局部专家专业化与全局上下文建模的协同组合提升表示能力；引入自适应融合策略动态突出最有效的信息成分，并采用更稳健的优化目标（如困难样本加权与类别分布平衡）增强学习稳定性。

Result: 在30个UEA基准数据集和真实振动数据上的大量实验表明，FreSH在分类准确率上持续优于现有最先进方法，同时显著减少了模型尺寸并提升了效率。

Conclusion: FreSH证明了频率分段分层多专家框架能够在保持较低计算开销的同时实现强大的表示能力，为MTSC在不平衡和资源受限场景下的应用提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Classification (MTSC) demands models that can effectively capture complex temporal patterns across multiple scales while remaining computationally efficient. However, existing approaches generally struggle to reconcile fine-grained representation learning, especially under class imbalance and real-world constraints. In this paper, we present FreSH, a Frequency-Segmented Hierarchical Multi-Expert Framework designed to address these challenges. FreSH introduces a new perspective for MTSC by enabling adaptive, multi-scale analysis of temporal signals, allowing different aspects of the data to be modeled in a complementary and coordinated manner. By combining localized specialization with holistic context modeling, FreSH achieves strong representational capacity without incurring excessive computational overhead. An adaptive fusion strategy further enhances flexibility, enabling the model to dynamically emphasize the most informative components of the input. In addition, we incorporate a more robust optimization objective that improves learning stability across varying sample difficulties and class distributions. Extensive evaluations on 30 UEA benchmark datasets and real-world vibration data demonstrate that FreSH consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in classification accuracy, while substantially reducing model size and efficiency.

</details>


### [57] [Control-Diverse Reinforcement Fine-Tuning: Decoupling the Shared Control Bottleneck of RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08224)
*Binwen Tan,Jingchao Wang,Dengzhe Hou,Lingyu Jiang,Zeyuan Wu,Yunhan Shen,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori Yamada,Atsushi Koike*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出共享控制瓶颈（Shared Control Bottleneck）概念，用于诊断和控制RL后训练中模型能力的分配机制。通过分离"激活"与"控制"，引入Post-training Control Coefficient来衡量组件对奖励增益的控制力，并提出CD-RFT训练方法降低控制坍缩，在Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.2-3B上实现更好的多任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练评估仅看基准分数，无法揭示模型内部的变化机制；主流可解释性观点将RL微调成功归因于更强更多样的电路激活，但这种"激活中心"的解释忽略了激活电路不一定控制奖励增益的事实。需要更精确的控制层面的分析工具。

Method: 借鉴代谢控制分析（Metabolic Control Analysis），定义Post-training Control Coefficient度量组件对奖励增益的控制力，构建控制矩阵与激活幅度矩阵；定义Shared Control Bottleneck（跨任务控制集中度）和Activation-Control Gap（激活与控制集中度之差）。基于此设计CD-RFT，通过在训练损失中加入正则项约束共享控制瓶颈，并推导一阶近似梯度以避免二阶自动微分的开销问题。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B上，CD-RFT实现最大控制解耦并提升数学、代码、逻辑等多任务能力，超过GRPO基线；无KL变体在pass@1上领先，KL惩罚变体在大k的pass@k覆盖上更好。额外开销最坏低于8%。结论在Llama-3.2-3B上亦有迁移性。

Conclusion: Shared Control Bottleneck既可作机制诊断工具也可作训练正则器；控制解耦（而非单纯激活增强）是RL后训练多任务能力提升的关键，且该发现具有跨模型泛化性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training unlocks complex reasoning in LLMs. Yet benchmark scores reveal only whether a model improved, not what changed inside it, nor how it splits finite capability across tasks. A representative interpretability line attributes the success of RL fine-tuning to stronger and more diverse circuit activation. We challenge this activation-centered account by separating activation from control: an activated circuit need not control the post-training reward gain. Adapting Metabolic Control Analysis, we define the Post-training Control Coefficient to measure component control over reward gain and arrange these coefficients by task family into a control matrix, paired with an activation-magnitude matrix. We call cross-task control concentration the Shared Control Bottleneck and the difference between activation and control concentration the Activation-Control Gap. This reveals that highly shared activations can coexist with task-specific control, while a small gap indicates that control has collapsed onto a shared direction and lost task specificity. To reduce this collapse, we regularize the post-training loss with the Shared Control Bottleneck and propose Control-Diverse Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (CD-RFT). The exact regularizer gradient requires second-order automatic differentiation incompatible with flash attention, so we derive a first-order proxy with worst-case overhead below eight percent. On Qwen2.5-7B, CD-RFT achieves the largest control decoupling and improves multi-task capability over matched GRPO across mathematics, code, and logic. The no-KL variant leads on pass@1, and the KL-penalized variant leads on large-k pass@k coverage that KL otherwise degrades. Together, these results show that the Shared Control Bottleneck is both a mechanistic diagnostic and a training regularizer, and that control decoupling and capability gains transfer to Llama-3.2-3B.

</details>


### [58] [The Replay Gap: Static Evaluation of Model Switching in LLM Agents Scores the Wrong World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08239)
*Ashritha Gonuguntla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分支回放（branching rollouts）方法测试了大语言模型路由（LLM router）在智能体（多步代理）场景中的评估假设，发现传统基于日志重放的评估方法严重失准。在约900次回放中，模型交换会导致大部分后续动作被重写，状态迅速分歧，且所有观察到结果翻转均发生在交换组中，而日志拼接式回放评估完全无法预测真实结果。作者发布了完整的评估框架和轨迹数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM路由器评估方式假设替换一个模型后，轨迹的其余部分不受影响（即单轮回放评估逻辑），但智能体场景中每个决策会改变环境状态，这一假设可能严重失实。作者旨在检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 在SWE-bench智能体轨迹中设置受控分支点，重建环境后用不同模型继续执行分支，并以同模型控制分支隔离采样与回放噪声。通过约900次回放、六组成对实验，量化模型交换后的动作重写率、状态分歧度，并与基于日志拼接（log-stitching）的回放评估器进行对比。

Result: 模型交换组的归一化编辑距离超出控制基线+0.25至+0.66（多重校正后置信区间排除零），61-94%的后续动作被重写；74-77%的早期交换在第一个动作即分歧，控制组仅为6-35%；仅3%的重放状态有效。分歧随分支深度下降。5个结果翻转全部发生在交换组（升级挽救未解决实例、降级丢失唯一解），359个控制分支中零翻转。日志拼接回放对每个成功相关结果调用均预测错误，补丁相似度为0.00-0.11。此外发现温度0"确定性"依赖模型服务配置（FP8与AWQ差异显著）。

Conclusion: 基于日志重放的评估方法在智能体路由场景中评估的是错误的世界，单轮路由的回放评估范式不可直接迁移到多步智能体场景。需要开发考虑状态分支和级联影响的评估方法，同时路由决策应利用分歧深度信息（浅层交换影响最大）。

Abstract: LLM routers promise efficiency by matching each request to the cheapest adequate model, and are increasingly applied per step inside multi-step agents. Yet agentic routers are evaluated like single-turn routers: by replaying logged trajectories and substituting another model's recorded outputs, assuming the rest of the trajectory is unaffected. We test this assumption with branching rollouts: we fork live SWE-bench agent trajectories at controlled points, rebuild the environment, continue each fork with a different model, and compare against same-model control forks that isolate sampling and replay noise. Across six paired runs (~900 rollouts), swaps exceed their matched control floors by +0.25 to +0.66 normalized edit distance (multiplicity-corrected CIs exclude zero), rewriting 61-94% of post-fork actions; 74-77% of early swaps diverge at the first post-fork action, versus 6-35% of controls, leaving only 3% of replayed states valid. Divergence decreases with fork depth in both directions. All five outcome flips we observe occur in swap arms, upgrades rescuing unsolved instances and a downgrade losing the sole solve, and zero occur across 359 control forks. Scoring these same swaps with a log-stitching replay evaluator, replay mispredicts every success-relevant outcome call and predicts patches with 0.00-0.11 similarity to reality. Auditing the noise floor, temperature-0 "determinism" is configuration-dependent: FP8-served controls diverge on over 90% of forks while AWQ-served ones remain near-identical; and under tight budgets the stronger model more often exhausts its steps without submitting. Replay-based benchmarks score the wrong world for agentic routing; we release our harness and all trajectories.

</details>


### [59] [Learning from Environmental Feedback: Credit Assignment across Multiple Timescales for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08255)
*Yifu Huo,Shunjie Xing,Chenglong Wang,Peinan Feng,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Anxiang Ma,Yuxin Gao,Tongran Liu,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了基于环境反馈的信用分配方法（EFCA），通过多时间尺度信号（短期反馈和中期状态历史）为长时程智能体强化学习提供更精细的奖励分解监督，在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上超过强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习常面临奖励延迟和稀疏的挑战，现有信用分配方法忽略了环境交互过程中自然产生的丰富过程信息（如交互历史），而这些信息能为识别单个动作的贡献提供有价值的监督信号。

Method: 提出EFCA方法，包含两个环境导向的过程信号与长期结果信号互补：一是短期反馈信号，捕捉当前动作的即时效果；二是中期状态历史信号，识别近期交互中的无效模式。两个信号直接来自环境反馈，并通过回报重加权机制整合。整体采用多时间尺度信用分配框架。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个基准上进行实验，EFCA在任务成功率和任务质量两方面均一致地优于强基线方法，验证了环境导向的多时间尺度信用分配对长时程智能体RL的有效性。

Conclusion: 充分利用环境交互中自然产生的过程信号进行多时间尺度信用分配，可以有效缓解长时程智能体强化学习中的延迟稀疏奖励问题，为更细粒度的动作贡献识别提供新思路。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) often suffers from delayed and sparse rewards in real-world environments. A promising solution to this challenge is credit assignment, which aims to decompose trajectory-level rewards and provide more fine-grained supervision for intermediate decisions. However, existing credit assignment approaches ignore the rich process information naturally generated during environment interaction, e.g., interaction history. We argue that such information provides valuable supervision for identifying the contribution of individual actions. To this end, we propose Environmental Feedback-based Credit Assignment (EFCA), a multi-timescale credit assignment approach for long-horizon agentic RL. EFCA complements the long-term outcome signal with two environment-grounded process signals: a short-term feedback signal that captures the immediate effect of the current action and a medium-term state-history signal that identifies ineffective patterns from recent interactions. Both signals are directly extracted from environment feedback and integrated through a return reweighting mechanism. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that EFCA consistently improves both task success and task quality over strong baselines, highlighting the effectiveness of environment-grounded multi-timescale credit assignment for long-horizon agentic RL.

</details>


### [60] [Opportunity Is Not Realizability: Selection-Valid Diagnostics for Multi-LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08265)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出Oracle路由度量存在两个缺陷（同例固定模型选择使配对推断失效、全信息Oracle看到部署时不存在的信号），提出了三个可分离的估计目标（结果Oracle机会、声明信号的贝叶斯最优增益、学习路由器的留出增益），并证明了具有选择有效性的置信区间、信号信息三明治不等式以及基于子模互补覆盖的(1-1/e)贪心保证。实验表明可部署路由器仅能恢复7.5-14.4%的Oracle差距，且多数差距仍无法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Oracle路由诊断方法存在统计与方法论缺陷：在相同样本上选择最佳固定模型进行测试会使配对推断失效，而全信息Oracle看到的是实际部署路由器无法观察的结果，导致对模型池潜力的高估和对可实现增益的错误估计。

Method: 提出三个分离的估计目标概念框架，构建选择有效置信区间（即使选择最佳固定模型或最佳路由器族成员也有效）、信号信息三明治不等式、以及用于构建紧凑模型池的子模互补覆盖贪心选择算法，并通过多族多基准实验进行验证。

Result: 在六个模型族、八个检查点、四个基准上，选择有效区间认证了每项任务上9.7-30.7个百分点的群体Oracle差距，但最强的可部署prompt路由器仅能恢复其中7.5-14.4%，且十一个测试策略中最佳策略的同时置信区间下限为零。

Conclusion: 可实现的Oracle机会份额很小且可被认证：强路由器确实能超过最佳固定模型，但Oracle机会的绝大部分差距仍然无法被实际部署系统捕获。

Abstract: Oracle routing measures how much a pool of language models could gain from per-query selection, but the diagnostic has two flaws: testing against a best fixed model selected on the same examples invalidates paired inference, and a full-information oracle sees outcomes no deployable router observes. We separate three estimands (outcome-oracle opportunity, the Bayes-optimal gain from a declared pre-answer signal, and the held-out gain of a learned router) and prove selection-valid confidence intervals that survive choosing the best fixed model or the best member of a router family, a signal-information sandwich, and a $(1-1/e)$ greedy guarantee for building compact pools from submodular complementary coverage. On eight checkpoints from six families over four benchmarks, selection-valid intervals certify a population oracle gap of $9.7$--$30.7$ points on every task, yet the strongest deployable prompt router recovers only $7.5$--$14.4\%$ of it, and the simultaneous interval for the best of eleven tested policies has lower limit zero throughout. The realizable share of oracle opportunity is small and certifiable: strong routers beat the best fixed model, and most of the gap remains.

</details>


### [61] [Stateful CARS: Exact Cross-History Reuse for Policy-Constrained LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08282)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Stateful CARS方法，通过冻结状态-延续模式库并移除含认证延续的轨迹，实现从满足有状态验证器的模型分布中进行精确采样，并在理论上证明了模式合理性、自适应精确性、几乎必然终止等性质。实验显示其分析分布与真实条件分布高度吻合（误差10^-16），但在系统效率上不优于现有CARS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型语言模型代理在运行时面临有状态约束（约束含义随观测和先前动作变化），已有的CARS方法（基于观察键控）在处理这些有状态约束时存在局限，需要一种能够跨历史复用无效性证书并实现精确采样的方法。

Method: Stateful CARS在每次尝试中冻结一组声音的状态-延续模式库，移除所有在匹配抽象状态处包含已认证延续的轨迹，然后使用精确的残差Doob变换从该提议分布中进行采样。理论上给出了未来有效性互模拟条件，证明了模式合理性、自适应精确性、i.i.d.输出、几乎必然终止、单调接受和压缩不变性。

Result: 在可枚举工作流上，该方法的解析分布与有效条件分布达到10^-16的精确度（有效性概率为6×10^-8），而状态感知局部解码的偏差可达0.97。但配对比较显示：官方CARS在采样步骤上更便宜（root/Stateful比率为0.942），Qwen比较结果为空（区间包含1），跨历史迁移仅在内部匹配键消融中有效（1.27×）。

Conclusion: 证据支持精确模式诱导条件化的价值，但Stateful CARS并没有带来相对CARS的系统优势，其主要贡献在于理论上的精确性和有状态约束处理能力，而非实际效率提升。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents face constraints whose meaning changes with observations and prior actions. We study exact sampling from the model distribution conditioned on a hard stateful validator while reusing invalidity certificates across histories. Stateful CARS freezes a bank of sound state--continuation schemas within each attempt and removes every trajectory containing a certified continuation at a matching abstract state. An exact residual Doob transform samples from the resulting proposal. We give a checkable future-validity bisimulation condition, prove schema soundness, adaptive exactness, i.i.d.\ outputs, almost-sure termination, monotone acceptance, and compression invariance, and characterize computation by the number of reachable full-history product states. This number can be exponential for a history-dependent language model; the evaluated method therefore makes no generic finite-trie scalability claim. On enumerable workflows, its analytic law matches the valid conditional to $10^{-16}$ at validity probability $6\times10^{-8}$, whereas state-aware local decoding can be $0.97$ away. A matched comparison is negative: observation-keyed official CARS is cheaper in sampler steps (root/Stateful ratio $0.942$ $[0.934,0.951]$), and the Qwen comparison is null ($0.99$ $[0.90,1.08]$). Cross-history transfer helps only in an internal matched-key ablation ($1.27\times$). Thus the evidence supports exact schema-induced conditioning, not a systems advantage over CARS.

</details>


### [62] [Causal State-Space Model for Causal Inference: Estimating Longitudinal Individual Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08288)
*Abisoye Abidakun,Mingjun Zhong,Georgios Leontidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对时序观测数据中反事实结果估计的领域混淆问题，形式化了平衡-预测互信息冲突，并提出两种互补模型CSSD和CSSPD。CSSD利用选择性状态空间模型和并行多步解码器消除累积滚动误差；CSSPD通过对比预测编码和局部信息最大化增强时间可预测性并恢复被破坏的协变量信息。实验表明CSSPD在MIMIC-III和癌症模拟数据集上优于Causal Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实时序估计方法依赖领域混淆（对抗训练）使表征对治疗分配不变，但这种不变性会抑制与治疗相关的协变量信息，从而损害结果预测的准确性。该文旨在形式化这一互信息冲突并提出结构化解法。

Method: 通过Jensen-Shannon散度界形式化反事实预测误差与互信息冲突的关系；提出CSSD（因果状态空间模型+直接解码器，并行多步预测消除累计误差）和CSSPD（在CSSD基础上增加对比预测编码和局部信息最大化正则化，强化时间可预测性并恢复局部协变量信息）。

Result: 在MIMIC-III上，CSSPD在所有预测视界τ≥2上以O(T)编码器成本取得低于Causal Transformer的反事实RMSE（增益0.02至0.07）；在癌症模拟数据上，CSSPD在γ≤3时领先CT（25.9%-37.0%），CSSD取得最低平均RMSE（较CT降低12.7%）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次形式化了平衡-预测互信息冲突，并通过互补的预测和信息论训练目标提供了结构化解决方案，验证了互信息冲突分析的准确性。

Abstract: Estimating counterfactual outcomes over time from longitudinal observational data is central to clinical decision support. Existing methods rely on domain confusion -- adversarial training that renders representations invariant to treatment assignment -- yet this invariance creates a mutual information conflict: it suppresses treatment-correlated covariate signals necessary for accurate outcome prediction. We formalise this tension via a Jensen-Shannon divergence bound on counterfactual prediction error and develop two complementary models. CSSD (Causal State-Space model with Direct decoder) adapts selective State Space Models with a parallel multi-step decoder that eliminates accumulated rollout error by producing all prediction horizons simultaneously in a single forward pass. CSSPD (Causal State-Space model with Predictive regularisation and Direct decoder) augments CSSD with Contrastive Predictive Coding and Local Information Maximisation to reinforce temporal predictability in the balancing representation and recover local covariate information destroyed by domain confusion. On MIMIC-III, CSSPD achieves lower counterfactual RMSE than the Causal Transformer at every horizon tau >= 2 at O(T) encoder cost, with gains from 0.02 (2-step) to 0.07 (6-step). On Cancer Simulation across confounding strengths gamma in {0,1,2,3,4}, CSSPD outperforms CT at gamma <= 3 (margins 25.9%--37.0%), and CSSD achieves the lowest overall average RMSE (12.7% reduction over CT), confirming the MI conflict analysis. To our knowledge, this is the first work to formalise the balancing-prediction MI conflict and propose a structured resolution through complementary predictive and information-theoretic training objectives.

</details>


### [63] [A Controlled Study of Feature-Based Knowledge Distillation Across Student Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08294)
*Abhinand Balachandran,Praveen Prashant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在CIFAR-100上系统研究了知识蒸馏中特征对齐方法对不同规模学生网络的影响，发现logit KD始终优于基线，但Attention Transfer和FitNets等特征方法的效果因学生结构而异，固定辅助系数在不同学生上产生不同梯度尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏中特征级方法额外对齐中间表示，但这种额外约束可能对不同学生产生不同影响，现有研究缺乏系统的对照实验来揭示这种差异。

Method: 使用ResNet-50教师模型，在CIFAR-100上以宽度控制的CustomResNet家族和MobileNetV2为比较对象，将每种特征方法与匹配的logit-KD进行对照实验，严格控制教师、优化器、训练计划和随机种子，并跨多种子重复关键比较。

Result: Logit KD对所有学生均优于从头训练基线；Attention Transfer在CustomResNet家族中平均效应为负但MobileNetV2上为正，且与模型宽度无明确关系；FitNets在所有15次配对实验中均低于logit KD，且恒定深度下宽度越大差距越大；相同辅助系数在不同学生上产生不同梯度尺度。

Conclusion: 特征蒸馏方法的效果具有高度结构依赖性，不能假设其普遍优于logit KD；固定辅助系数并未创造统一训练条件，需要针对不同学生调整实验设置。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation trains a smaller student to match the outputs of a larger teacher. Feature-based methods also align intermediate representations, but this extra constraint may affect students differently. We study this question on CIFAR-100 using a ResNet-50 teacher, a width-controlled CustomResNet family and MobileNetV2 as a cross-design comparison. For each student, we evaluate each feature method against a matched logit-KD run using the same teacher, optimizer settings, training schedule and seed. We repeat the main comparisons across multiple seeds.
  Logit KD improved every tested student over its scratch baseline. Attention Transfer showed no clear relationship with size inside the CustomResNet family, but its average effect was negative for that family and positive for MobileNetV2. FitNets was below logit KD in all 15 paired runs. Within the constant-depth width sweep, its gap increased for wider students, although the different-depth w=48 student did not follow this trend. Finally, the same auxiliary coefficient produced different gradient scales across students, showing that a fixed coefficient does not create a uniform training condition.

</details>


### [64] [Machine-Learning-Based Diagnostic Framework for Passive Ultrasonic Detection of Railway Wheel Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08301)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于机器学习的铁路车轮缺陷多分类诊断框架，利用被动式空气耦合超声声发射信号和随机森林分类器，在11个全尺寸车轮组（9种健康状态）上实现了约0.66的平衡准确率和0.65的Macro-F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路车轮缺陷的可靠识别对安全和维护至关重要，需要一种非接触式的自动化缺陷诊断方法。

Method: 采集11个全尺寸铁路车轮组的被动空气耦合超声声发射信号，使用Kruskal-Wallis统计检验和互信息分析从时域和频域特征中筛选最具判别力的指标，训练随机森林分类器并进行分层5折交叉验证。

Result: 模型在9个类别上取得约0.66的平衡准确率和0.65的Macro-F1分数；衰减率、峰度、偏度和包络低频功率是最有影响力的特征，紧凑的特征子集保留了大部分分类性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明将被动超声传感、统计特征选择和监督机器学习相结合，可实现非接触式铁路车轮缺陷分类，为未来现场部署的检测系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Reliable identification of railway wheel defects is important for safety and maintenance. This study develops a machine-learning-based diagnostic framework for multi-class defect identification using passive air-coupled ultrasonic acoustic emission signals. Data were collected from eleven full-scale railway wheelsets representing nine health states. Time- and frequency-domain features were evaluated using Kruskal-Wallis statistical testing and mutual-information analysis to identify the most discriminative indicators. A Random Forest classifier was then trained using the selected features with stratified 5-fold cross-validation. The model achieved a balanced accuracy of approximately 0.66 and a Macro-F1 score of 0.65 across the nine classes. Decay rate, kurtosis, skewness, and envelope low-frequency power emerged as the most influential features, while a compact subset of features retained most of the classification performance. The results demonstrate the feasibility of combining passive ultrasonic sensing, statistical feature selection, and supervised machine learning for non-contact railway wheel defect classification and provide a foundation for future field-deployable inspection systems.

</details>


### [65] [The Neural Division of Labor: Biologically-Inspired Modular Architectures for Robust Neuromorphic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08317)
*Maksim Bazhenov,Serafim Grubas,Vakhtang Putkaradze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可分解脉冲神经网络（D-SNN），通过将分类路径结构性地隔离为独立专家网络来消除全局突触纠缠，利用生物启发的推拉损失函数进行优化，在多个基准数据集上达到与全稠密网络相当的精度，同时参数数量减少一个数量级，发放率和突触操作降低数个数量级，并通过物理切断专家间连接实现抗灾难性遗忘和增强决策透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代人工神经网络采用全局纠缠结构，导致决策逻辑不透明且容易发生灾难性遗忘，而生物神经系统通过区室化架构实现了高效性和鲁棒性。作者希望借鉴生物学的模块化设计，构建一种结构上隔离、逻辑可审计且能抵抗灾难性遗忘的高效神经网络。

Method: 提出D-SNN架构，将分类通路结构性地分离为独立的专家网络，消除全局突触纠缠；引入生物启发的推拉（push-pull）损失函数进行优化训练；通过在顺序学习过程中物理切断专家之间的连接来实现抗遗忘保护。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10/100基准上取得与全稠密网络相当的精度；参数数量减少一个数量级；网络发放率和突触操作降低数个数量级；物理切断连接可有效防止灾难性遗忘；隔离通路产生可审计的神经信号，提升决策透明度。

Conclusion: 这种仿生、可验证的模块化架构为在资源受限的边缘环境中部署确定性神经形态智能提供了高效基础，兼顾性能、效率、鲁棒性和透明度。

Abstract: Biological neural systems achieve high efficiency and robustness through compartmentalized architectures. In contrast, modern artificial neural networks rely on globally entangled structures, which obscure decision logic and suffer from catastrophic forgetting. Here, we report a Decomposable Spiking Neural Network (D-SNN) that eliminates global synaptic entanglement by structurally isolating classification pathways into independent experts. Optimized via a bio-inspired push-pull loss function, the D-SNN achieves competitive accuracies on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10/100 benchmarks. This modular approach matches the performance of fully dense networks while utilizing an order of magnitude fewer parameters. In addition, our networks operate with up to several orders of magnitude lower firing rates and fewer synaptic operations. Furthermore, physically severing connections between experts provides inherent protection against catastrophic forgetting during sequential learning. Crucially, these isolated pathways generate auditable neural signals, increasing decision transparency. This biomimetic, verifiable architecture establishes an efficient foundation for deploying deterministic neuromorphic intelligence in resource-constrained edge environments.

</details>


### [66] [Spatial Heterogeneity-Aware Multi-Hazard Susceptibility and Risk Mapping at Regional Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08321)
*Aswathi Mundayatt,Siddharth Anil,Hitanshu Seth,Jaya Sreevalsan-Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个空间异质性感知框架，用于绘制印度喀拉拉邦和尼泊尔的洪水-滑坡易发性和相对风险图。对比了邻近门控跨区训练(S1)和生态门控区内约束训练(S2)两种策略，发现S1在预测性能上更优（如尼泊尔洪水AUC-ROC从0.728提升至0.886），而S2更好保留了区域环境差异，两种策略各有优势，建议整合使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洪水和滑坡常共同发生，但其与环境控制因素的关系在不同空间区域中存在差异。现有研究缺乏能够同时考虑空间异质性并处理洪水-滑坡联合易发性与风险评估的框架，需要开发一种空间感知策略以提升区域尺度的灾害预测和风险制图精度。

Method: 采用15公里×15公里网格单元结合区域特定上下文分区，构建空间异质性感知框架。比较两种训练策略：S1（邻近门控跨区训练，允许邻近模型跨上下文边界分配）和S2（生态门控区内约束训练，限制模型开发和分配在同一区域）。使用随机森林模型和策略特定预测变量集，在空间保持的测试样本上评估。将易发性表面与CRITIC加权暴露度和脆弱性指标整合，生成灾害特定和九类双变量相对风险图。

Result: S1在准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、AUC-ROC和PR-AUC方面均优于S2。最大差异出现在尼泊尔洪水易发性（AUC-ROC从0.728升至0.886，PR-AUC从0.512升至0.823）。S2在两个尼泊尔灾害中获得更低Brier分数，并保留了区域特定差异。双变量风险图一致性在喀拉拉邦为0.521、尼泊尔为0.711，所有比较中分配不一致超过数量不一致。易发性-风险对应关系低于0.350。

Conclusion: 跨区学习增强区域判别能力，而区内约束学习保留环境差异，两种策略具有互补优势，建议将其整合用于洪水-滑坡联合易发性和风险制图。该框架为应对灾害评估中的空间异质性提供了新的方法论参考。

Abstract: Floods and landslides often co-occur, but their relationships with environmental controls vary spatially. This study develops a spatial heterogeneity-aware framework for flood-landslide susceptibility and relative-risk mapping in Kerala, India, and Nepal. It combines 15 km x 15 km grid cells with region-specific contextual zones and compares proximity-gated cross-zone training (S1) and ecology-gated zone-constrained training (S2). S1 permits geographically nearby models to be assigned across contextual boundaries, whereas S2 restricts model development and assignment to the same zone. Random Forest models for each hazard use strategy-specific predictor sets and are evaluated on spatially held-out test samples. Susceptibility surfaces are integrated with CRITIC-weighted exposure and vulnerability indices to produce hazard-specific and nine-class bivariate relative-risk maps. S1 achieved higher mean accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, AUC-ROC, and PR-AUC for both hazards and regions. The largest difference occurred for Nepal flood susceptibility, where AUC-ROC increased from 0.728 under S2 to 0.886 under S1 and PR-AUC from 0.512 to 0.823. S2 produced lower Brier scores for both Nepal hazards and retained zone-specific differences in predictor selection, SHAP rankings, and response patterns, particularly in Kerala. Both strategies reproduced flood-prone lowland and landslide-prone upland patterns but differed in susceptibility and risk classes. Bivariate risk-map agreement was 0.521 in Kerala and 0.711 in Nepal, with allocation disagreement exceeding quantity disagreement in all S1-S2 comparisons. Susceptibility-to-risk correspondence remained below 0.350, showing that exposure and vulnerability changed priority locations. Overall, cross-zone learning strengthens regional discrimination, while zone-constrained learning preserves environmental differences, supporting their integration.

</details>


### [67] [PRISM: A Predictive Protocol for Permutation Optimization via Landscape Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08344)
*Blessings Mambwe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISM是一个预测性协议，在进入排列优化前先测量适应度景观，通过低成本诊断（如一步移动自相关和适应度-距离相关性）来预测有效的变异算子、判断结构化搜索是否优于随机采样，以及识别搜索收益甚微的场景。跨合成排列景观、神经架构基准、科学ML流水线和LLM指令排序中均验证了其预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排列优化问题广泛存在于系统组件固定但顺序影响性能的场景中，然而现有的搜索策略缺乏前置的诊断框架，无法预先判断是否值得进行排列搜索、应该使用何种表示和算子，导致计算资源浪费或优化效果欠佳。

Method: PRISM协议在优化开始前使用廉价的景观诊断工具（包括一步移动自相关和适应度-距离相关性）来预测选择合适的变异算子、识别结构化搜索优于随机采样的条件，以及检测搜索优势微弱的区间。该方法在多种排列景观上进行了验证，包括合成景观、神经架构、科学ML流水线和LLM指令排序。

Result: 穷举指令排序实验表明仅由排列变化就能引发显著的性能波动；跨模型实验显示有用的排序结构可在不同模型家族和任务难度间迁移；额外实验证明即使提示词内容已优化，指令排序仍然影响性能，说明内容优化与排序优化是互补的。

Conclusion: PRISM并非普遍优越的优化器，而是一个决策框架，用于判断排列搜索何时有用、应选择哪种表示和算子，以及何时更简单的替代方案更合适。

Abstract: Permutation optimization arises whenever the components of a system are fixed but their ordering affects performance. We introduce PRISM, a predictive protocol for permutation optimization that measures a fitness landscape before selecting a search strategy. PRISM uses inexpensive landscape diagnostics, including one-step move autocorrelation and fitness-distance correlation, to predict useful mutation operators, identify when structured search is likely to outperform random sampling, and detect regimes in which search provides little advantage. Across synthetic permutation landscapes, neural architecture benchmarks, scientific machine learning pipelines, and large-language-model instruction ordering, the protocol makes testable predictions about search behavior before optimization begins. Exhaustive instruction-ordering experiments reveal substantial performance variation induced solely by permutation, while cross-model experiments show that useful ordering structure can transfer across model families and task difficulty. Additional experiments demonstrate that instruction ordering remains consequential after prompt wording is optimized, indicating that content optimization and ordering optimization are complementary. The results position PRISM not as a universally superior optimizer, but as a framework for determining when permutation search is useful, which representation and operator should be used, and when simpler alternatives are preferable.

</details>


### [68] [Correlation flow governs learning at criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08350)
*Andrea Combette,Nelly Pustelnik,Antoine Venaille*

Main category: cs.LG
