Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers commonly use the same scores both to select experts and to weight their already-computed outputs. We study whether these two roles, dispatch and aggregation, should be coupled. On pretrained OLMoE-1B-7B, we keep selected Top-8 expert IDs, expert computation, and total selected router mass fixed and change only within-set aggregation. A structured oracle improves full-horizon cross-entropy by 0.0160 +/- 0.0039 across three seeds; the router's top-scored expert is the counterfactual-best vertex only 17.2% of the time, with router-utility Spearman 0.030. We therefore train Fixed-Dispatch Adaptive Aggregation (FDAA), a 301K-parameter post-compute head optimized directly with the language-modeling objective while freezing the backbone, router, and experts. On OLMoE, FDAA improves fresh WikiText-103 test by Delta CE = -0.1523 +/- 0.0031 across three seeds, and mixed-domain training gives robust gains on WikiText-103, C4, and held-out Penn Treebank under frozen confirmatory evaluation. We also replicate the fixed-dispatch audit on DeepSeek-V2-Lite, which uses Top-6 routed experts plus shared experts. Best-vertex headroom remains significant on WikiText and C4, while router Top1 identifies the best selected expert in only 12.5% and 16.7% of audited examples. In a one-seed mixed-domain replication, FDAA improves locked WikiText and PTB, while C4 is statistically neutral. These results support a cross-architecture distinction between expert selection and expert commitment.

</details>


### [103] [Agentic Anomaly Detection with ORCA-Style Dynamic Inductive Bias Adaptation in Multimodal Wearable Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08859)
*Anushka Roy,Jyotirmoy Singh,Shreea Bose,Chittaranjan Hota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCA是一个面向无线体域网（WBAN）异常检测的自适应时间感受野控制框架，通过轻量级信号统计量在推理时动态选择时间上下文，无需额外训练参数或学习策略。在自定义WBAN数据集上达到与最强固定上下文基线相当的AUROC=0.99，并在MIMIC-IV上展现了良好的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线体域网生成的高度非平稳生理时间序列在严格计算和内存约束下处理时，时间感受野的选择是一个关键但未充分探索的挑战。现有方法依赖固定时间上下文，在不同信号状态下表现不一致，且需要针对数据集进行调参。

Method: ORCA采用智能体控制架构，使用监督控制器在推理时基于轻量级信号统计量，自主选择离散的时间上下文集合，实现状态依赖的归纳偏置自适应，无需引入额外可训练参数或学习策略，也无需重新训练。

Result: 在自定义WBAN数据集上，ORCA达到AUROC=0.99，与最强固定上下文基线相当，同时免除了时间视野的预先调优。在MIMIC-IV分布外基准上呈现保守泛化行为，未出现性能崩溃。

Conclusion: 自适应时间归纳偏置控制是资源受限、非平稳生理时间序列异常检测中一种实用且稳健的设计原则。

Abstract: Wireless Body Area Networks (WBANs) generate multivariate physiological time series that are highly nonstationary and must often be processed under strict computational and memory constraints. A critical yet underexplored challenge in this setting is selecting an appropriate temporal receptive field, which serves as a strong inductive bias for anomaly detection models. Existing approaches typically rely on fixed temporal contexts, which can perform inconsistently across heterogeneous signal regimes and require dataset-specific tuning. We propose ORCA, an agentically controlled anomaly detection framework that dynamically adapts the temporal receptive field at inference time based on lightweight signal statistics. Rather than introducing additional trainable parameters or learned policies, ORCA employs a supervisory controller that autonomously selects among discrete temporal contexts, enabling state-dependent inductive bias adaptation without retraining. Across a custom WBAN dataset, ORCA achieves performance comparable to the strongest fixed-context baselines (AUROC = 0.99) while eliminating the need to tune temporal horizons in advance. We further evaluate ORCA on MIMIC-IV as a challenging out-of-distribution benchmark, observing conservative generalization behavior without performance collapse under heterogeneous clinical conditions. These results highlight adaptive temporal inductive bias control as a practical and robust design principle for anomaly detection in resource-constrained, nonstationary physiological time series.

</details>


### [104] [Approximation Rates for Metaplectic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08872)
*Ahmed Abdeljawad,Marcello Carioni,Elena Cordero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于metaplectic算子字典的浅层神经网络，扩展了Barron空间概念，建立嵌入关系并证明Monte-Carlo逼近界，最后通过深度神经网络架构在薛定谔方程求解中验证了该字典的有效性，性能优于经典物理信息神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统浅层神经网络在函数逼近中对字典的选择和理论保证有限，且经典Fourier变换在辛几何背景下存在局限性。作者希望借助metaplectic变换这一辛几何动机下的Fourier推广，构建更具表达力的神经网络字典，从而改进对复杂函数（如量子力学方程解）的逼近效果。

Method: 首先利用metaplectic变换（Fourier变换的辛几何扩展）推广Barron空间概念，定义metaplectic Barron空间；然后建立该空间与Sobolev空间之间的嵌入关系；接着构造神经metaplectic字典，并证明有限字典原子线性组合对metaplectic Barron函数的Monte-Carlo逼近界；最后设计以字典原子为构建模块的深度神经网络架构，用于求解含时薛定谔方程。

Result: 理论方面得到了metaplectic Barron函数的Monte-Carlo逼近误差界；实验方面，所提出的基于metaplectic字典的深度网络在逼近含时薛定谔方程解时，表现优于经典的物理信息神经网络架构。

Conclusion: metaplectic字典为浅层及深层神经网络提供了新的函数逼近框架，在量子力学方程等具有辛结构的问题中展现出显著优势，验证了辛几何思想与神经网络结合的潜力。

Abstract: In this paper we develop quantitative approximation results for shallow neural networks constructed using a dictionary based on metaplectic operators. First, we extend the concept of Barron spaces by considering a symplectically motivated extension of the Fourier transform, known as the metaplectic transform. Then, after establishing embedding between metaplectic Barron spaces and Sobolev spaces we consider a neural metaplectic dictionary and we prove Monte-Carlo approximation bounds for metaplectic Barron functions using finite linear combinations of atoms of the dictionary. Finally, we validate the introduction of the neural metaplectic dictionary by devising a deep neural network architecture that uses as building blocks the atoms of the dictionary. We test it to approximate solutions of time-dependent Schrödinger equations, demonstrating better performance compared to classical phyisics informed neural networks architectures.

</details>


### [105] [DistillCache: KL-Guided Adaptive KV-Cache Eviction for Memory-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08878)
*Asaad Althoubi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DistillCache是一个基于强化学习的KV缓存驱逐框架，将KV缓存驱逐建模为序列决策问题，通过轻量策略网络利用模型内部信号（注意力统计、值范数、熵和位置）来学习驱逐策略，在25%缓存预算下保留了LongBench上94.2%的全缓存准确率，性能优于H₂O、SnapKV等启发式方法以及ForesightKV、RLKV等并发RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer大模型的KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，造成长上下文推理的严重内存瓶颈。现有启发式驱逐方法（如H₂O、SnapKV）依赖静态注意力或位置信号，往往无法捕捉token未来的预测影响力，需要更智能的驱逐策略。

Method: 将KV缓存驱逐形式化为顺序决策问题，使用REINFORCE算法训练轻量策略网络（输入包含注意力统计、值范数、熵和位置等丰富内部信号），并采用逐步KL散度奖励来保持全缓存的输出分布。

Result: 在Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3上，DistillCache在25%缓存预算下保留LongBench 94.2%的全缓存准确率，比H₂O和SnapKV高最多2.7个百分点，比重实现的并发RL方法（ForesightKV、RLKV）在长上下文任务上高最多1.4个百分点；在推理基准上与最优并发方法相当，在激进压缩下更优；吞吐量最高达到全缓存的2.1倍。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了学习型、分布感知的策略对于内存高效的长上下文LLM推理的有效性，DistillCache在不同压缩率和任务类型上都展现了优越或具有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across many tasks, but their Key-Value (KV) cache grows linearly with sequence length, creating a severe memory bottleneck for long-context inference. Existing heuristic eviction methods (e.g., H$_2$O and SnapKV) rely on static attention or positional signals that often fail to capture a token's future predictive influence. We propose DistillCache, a reinforcement learning framework that formulates KV-cache eviction as a sequential decision problem. DistillCache learns a lightweight policy network using rich internal model signals (attention statistics, value norms, entropy, and position) and trains it with REINFORCE via a per-step KL-divergence reward to preserve the full-cache output distribution. On a 7B-parameter instruction-tuned Transformer (Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3), DistillCache retains 94.2% of full-cache accuracy on LongBench at a 25% cache budget, outperforming both strong heuristic baselines (H$_2$O, SnapKV) by up to 2.7 absolute points and, under our re-implementations, concurrent RL-based methods (ForesightKV, RLKV) by up to 1.4 points on long-context tasks. On reasoning benchmarks, DistillCache is competitive with the best concurrent method and surpasses it under aggressive compression. It also delivers up to 2.1x full-cache throughput while maintaining competitive practical efficiency. These results highlight the effectiveness of learned, distribution-aware policies for memory-efficient long-context LLM inference.

</details>


### [106] [Federated Attention Autoencoders with a Stochastic Aggregation Scheme for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08906)
*Mihailo Ilić,Miloš Savić,Vladimir Kurbalija,Mirjana Ivanović,Giancarlo Fortino,Dušan Jakovetić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种针对基于注意力机制的自动编码器的新型聚合函数，用于联邦学习中的异常检测，在KDDCUP10数据集上相比传统自动编码器在F1分数和AUC ROC上分别提升2.9%和5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化数据环境中，异常检测面临数据无法共享的挑战。虽然注意力机制能提升自动编码器效率，但在联邦学习中因缺乏合适的聚合函数而应用不足。

Method: 提出两种针对注意力自动编码器的新型聚合函数，能够更好地保留网络记忆模块中学习到的信息。

Result: 在KDDCUP10数据集上，相比传统自动编码器，F1分数提升2.9%，AUC ROC提升5.1%。

Conclusion: 所提出的聚合函数有效解决了注意力机制模型在联邦学习中的聚合问题，提升了异常检测性能。

Abstract: Outlier detection in decentralized data environments is a challenging task for many machine learning implementations, particularly in settings where data cannot be shared. Recently, there have been advances in federated outlier detection, some of which are based on the use of autoencoder networks. The introduction of attention mechanisms to autoencoders boosts their efficiency. However, the application of attention-based models in federated learning remains underdeveloped due to the absence of proper aggregation functions for these types of networks. In our work, we propose two novel aggregation functions tailored for attention-based autoencoders, which better preserve the learned information stored within the memory modules of these networks. We evaluated our approach on the KDDCUP10 dataset, and we showed that the proposed methods achieve up to 2.9\% and 5.1\% better results for F1 score and AUC ROC respectively when compared to traditional autoencoders.

</details>


### [107] [A Domain-Structured Ensemble Framework for Perioperative Outcome Prediction Using Electronic Health Record Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08920)
*Shikhar Shukla,Cristina Barboi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于领域结构化集成框架的围手术期结局预测模型，利用电子健康档案数据，将预测因子按患者、手术和麻醉三个领域组织，通过梯度提升模型独立估计风险，再由逻辑回归元学习器整合。在5,386例手术事件中验证术后谵妄预测，AUROC达0.899，校准性能优异，优于单一阶段模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有围手术期风险预测模型受限于狭窄的手术人群、不完整的术中数据、校准不佳和可解释性有限，亟需一种更具通用性和可解释性的预测框架。

Method: 采用领域结构化集成框架：将预测因子分为患者相关、手术相关和麻醉相关三个领域；每个领域训练独立的梯度提升模型生成风险估计；通过逻辑回归元学习器整合各领域风险估计；使用5,386例手术事件（2,693例谵妄，2,693例对照）进行案例对照研究验证。

Result: 堆叠元学习器AUROC为0.899，PR-AUC为0.881，Brier得分为0.126；优于最佳单阶段模型的AUROC 0.849；时间验证AUROC达0.915；校准截距-0.006，斜率1.035；决策曲线分析显示在临床合理阈值下具有正净获益。

Conclusion: 该模块化框架支持替代结局、预测领域扩展和动态风险更新，为可解释、校准感知的围手术期临床决策支持提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Perioperative risk prediction models are often limited by narrow surgical populations, incomplete intraoperative data, poor calibration, and limited interpretability. We present a domain-structured ensemble framework for perioperative outcome prediction using routinely collected electronic health record (EHR) data. Predictors are organized into patient-related, surgery-related, and anesthetics-related domains. Domain-specific gradient boosting models generate independent risk estimates that are integrated through a logistic regression meta-learner. We demonstrate the framework using postoperative delirium (POD) in a case-control sample of 5,386 surgical encounters (2,693 cases, 2,693 controls) from a statewide health information exchange. POD required both delirium-related ICD codes and a positive Confusion Assessment Method screening within seven postoperative days; patients with preexisting dementia were excluded. The stacked meta-learner achieved AUROC 0.899 (95% CI: 0.891-0.906), precision-recall AUC 0.881, and Brier score 0.126, compared with AUROC 0.849 for the best single-stage model. Domain ablation showed improved discrimination and calibration over a surgery-only model (AUROC 0.879, Brier 0.140). Temporal validation on held-out post-2017 data yielded AUROC 0.915. Calibration was excellent, with intercept -0.006 (95% CI: -0.083 to 0.070) and slope 1.035 (95% CI: 0.982 to 1.088). Decision curve analysis, corrected for case-control sampling, showed positive net benefit across clinically plausible thresholds. The modular framework supports alternative outcomes, extension of predictor domains, and dynamic risk updating, providing a scalable foundation for interpretable, calibration-aware perioperative clinical decision support.

</details>


### [108] [Idea Search: Guiding Tree Search with Ideas to Explore Diverse Scientific Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08958)
*Xuefei Julie Wang,Hao Cui,Michael P. Brenner,Subhashini Venugopalan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Idea Search框架，将动态"Idea Bank"融入树搜索中，以解决LLM树搜索在科学编码任务中容易陷入局部最优和无效率循环的问题。在scRNA-seq批次整合任务中，该方法将纯树搜索基线的平均分数从0.678提升至0.697，最佳分数达到0.728。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯树搜索方法在科学编码的广阔搜索空间中缺乏系统性探索，容易陷入局部最优或产生无效循环，需要一种机制来引导搜索方向并积累有效知识。

Method: Idea Search包含三个步骤：(1)将现有方法分解为原子化思想；(2)从Idea Bank中采样以指导代码变异分支；(3)通过执行结果动态更新Idea Bank，纳入新发现的思想。系统性地将知识库与树搜索相结合。

Result: 在scRNA-seq批次整合任务中，Idea Search将平均分数从0.678提升至0.697，最佳分数达到0.728，成功突破了纯树搜索基线的性能瓶颈。此外发现：bank增强对bandit采样有效但对随机采样无效；优先探索新思想的"Exploratory"提示能发现罕见的最佳解；而增加采样级探索反而适得其反。

Conclusion: Idea Search通过动态思想库的引入有效改善了树搜索的系统性探索能力，证明了知识积累与搜索结合的价值，并为采样策略和提示设计提供了关键的实证指导。

Abstract: Tree Search-based test-time scaling of LLMs is a powerful tool for automated scientific coding. However, pure Tree Search sometimes struggles with systematic exploration, becoming trapped in local optima, or unproductive loops, especially in the vast search space of scientific methods. To address this limitation, we propose Idea Search, a framework that systematically integrates a dynamic "Idea Bank" into Tree Search. Idea Search involves three steps: (1) decomposing existing methods into atomic ideas, (2) sampling from this bank of ideas to guide branches of code mutations, and (3) dynamically updating the bank with new ideas discovered through execution. On single-cell RNA-sequencing (scRNA-seq) batch integration, Idea Search reliably breaks the plateau of a strong pure Tree Search baseline, improving the mean score from 0.678 to 0.697 and reaching a best score of 0.728. We then characterize which design choices drive these gains: bank augmentation helps bandit sampling but not random sampling, "Exploratory" prompting that prioritizes new ideas surfaces the rare best-performing solutions, while increasing sampling-level exploration is counterproductive.

</details>


### [109] [Gradient Under Microscope: Benchmarking Resource Utilization of Memory-Efficient Gradient Computation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08961)
*Sarthak Mahapatra,Zihan Zhou,Khatoon Khedri,Mehdi Hosseinzadeh,Reza Rawassizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地评估了五种梯度优化器（SGD、Adam、Adadelta、Adadelta和共轭梯度下降）在三种内存策略（标准训练、梯度检查点和梯度累积）下对四种Transformer架构（ViT、ModernBERT、Llama 3.1 1B和NanoVLM）的训练效果。研究发现梯度累积是最可靠的内存策略，可在不增加GPU内存的情况下显著降低训练损失；Adam并非在所有场景下都最优，Adadelta和SGD在编码器和自回归架构上表现更佳；梯度检查点的效果高度依赖架构类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI训练日益增长的资源消耗正对电力供应和碳排放预算构成压力，亟需系统研究在资源受限硬件上进行内存高效训练的方法。

Method: 在四种Transformer架构上，对五种梯度优化器在三种内存策略下进行系统基准测试，测量训练损失、GPU利用率、训练时间和内存使用四个关键指标。

Result: 梯度累积是最可靠策略，在视觉-语言模型上训练损失降低约一个数量级，在语言模型上降低约四倍且无需额外GPU内存；Adam并非普遍最优，Adadelta和SGD在编码器和自回归架构上表现更优；GPU利用率主要受架构决定，范围从内存受限语言模型的8-15%到计算受限视觉模型的96-99%。

Conclusion: 研究为资源高效模型训练和部署中的优化器与梯度策略选择提供了实用指南，强调策略选择应基于具体架构特征而非通用惯例。

Abstract: AI training's rising resource intensity is straining electricity supplies and carbon budgets, motivating systematic study of memory-efficient training on constrained hardware. We benchmark five gradient optimizers (SGD, Adam, Adagrad, Adadelta, and Conjugate Gradient Descent) under three memory strategies (standard training, gradient checkpointing, and gradient accumulation) across four transformer architectures (ViT, ModernBERT, Llama 3.1 1B, and NanoVLM), measuring training loss, GPU utilization, training time, and memory usage. Gradient accumulation emerges as the most reliable strategy, cutting training loss by roughly an order of magnitude on the vision-language model and about four-fold on the language model without additional GPU memory. Contrary to common practice, Adam is not universally superior: Adadelta and SGD outperform it on the encoder and autoregressive architectures. Gradient checkpointing's effect is strongly architecture-dependent, improving vision transformer loss while severely degrading the encoder model, and it increases training time by up to 60% on memory-bound models. GPU utilization is governed primarily by architecture, ranging from 8-15% for the memory-bound language model to 96-99% for compute-bound vision models. These findings provide practical guidelines for optimizer and gradient-strategy selection in resource-efficient model training and deployment.

</details>


### [110] [Math-Vision Diagrams: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Mathematical Diagram Generation Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08964)
*Harish Kashyap,Kiran Byadarhaly,Sriram Chakaravarthy,Sanyukta Tuti,Aryan Mistry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Math-Vision Diagrams，这是首个专门评估大型语言模型数学图表生成能力的基准，统一了text-to-code和text-to-image两种范式，包含2920个从数学竞赛题目中精选的提示-图像对，并通过多LLM集成与专家人工筛选构建，实验表明现有模型在此任务上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学图表生成对于课程准备、题目自动排序和科学出版等场景至关重要，但现有基准如MathVision、MathVista主要关注数学推理，DiagramGenBenchmark和MermaidSeqBench则面向通用图表生成，缺乏专门用于评估LLM数学图表生成能力的标准化提示-图像对数据集。

Method: 基于Math-Vision基准，从3040张竞赛题图像中筛选出2920张具有必要视觉上下文的高质量样本；提出一种结合多个LLM集成与领域专家（SME）人工筛选的新颖数据构建流程；同时设计了一套评估指标套件，用于统一评估text-to-code和text-to-image两种生成范式。

Result: 在多个主流LLM上测试该基准后，实验结果表明LLMs在数学图表生成任务上表现困难，揭示了这一领域的显著挑战。

Conclusion: 本文填补了数学图表生成评估基准的空白，首次在同一统一框架下评估text-to-code和text-to-image两种范式，并承诺开源全部代码、数据、筛选流程和评估脚本，为后续研究提供了标准化测试平台。

Abstract: The generation of mathematically precise diagrams from tex- tual prompts has emerged as a critical yet underexplored capability of Large Language Models (LLMs). This has been of interest to researchers in the areas of curriculum preparation, automated ranking of problem sets, and scientific publishing. For LLMs to achieve this, it requires per- fect coordination between Spatial Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning, and Rendering systems. While existing benchmarks such as MathVision, MathVista are built for Math Reasoning or DiagramGenBenchmark, Mer- maidSeqBench on general purpose diagram generation, no prior work provides a standardized set of prompt, image pairs that can be used to evaluate the LLMs specifically on math diagram generation. This includes fields that span both both text-to-code and text-to-image paradigms. We introduce Math-Vision Diagrams, the first benchmark specifically designed to evaluate LLMs on mathematical diagram generation, and the first to assess text-to-code and text-to-image generation paradigms together in a single unified setting, agnostic of the underlying coding lan- guage or model type. Building on the Math-Vision benchmark, we select a subset of 2920 images out of 3040 from high-quality competition problems with essential visual context. A novel pipeline combining an ensemble of LLMs with Subject Matter Expert (SME) curation is presented, together with a suite of evaluation metrics. Testing several leading models against this benchmark, we demonstrate that LLMs struggle with math diagram generation. All code, data, curation pipeline, and evaluation scripts will be fully open-sourced.

</details>


### [111] [Label-Free Parkinson's Disease Screening from Face and Voice through Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08976)
*Jiaheng Su,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全无标签的帕金森病（PD）面部+语音筛查方法，仅使用冻结的预训练编码器（人脸表情Vision Transformer和HuBERT），无需任何PD标签参与训练。语音模态通过合成构音障碍对比激活添加（CAA）方向实现，面部模态使用k近邻异常分数。在YouTubePD基准上，融合后AUROC达0.802，NPV为0.95，支持排除性分诊应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病是第二大常见神经退行性疾病，但传统机器学习筛查需要大量PD标注数据，而这类数据受隐私限制且需要专家标注，获取困难。因此需要一种不依赖PD标签的筛查方法。

Method: 方法包括三个核心部分：(1) 语音模态：通过对健康语音进行时间拉伸和气息退化处理生成合成构音障碍样本，构建合成退化方向的对比激活添加（CAA）方向作为检测器；(2) 面部模态：使用k近邻异常分数衡量面部与对照组嵌入聚类的偏离程度；(3) 引入"对齐原则"（alignment principle）作为事后分析工具，判断合成退化CAA检测器是否有效——当合成疾病方向与真实疾病方向的余弦相似度大于零时有效。最终采用等权重晚期融合。

Result: 在YouTubePD基准上，语音模态的合成与真实疾病方向余弦相似度为+0.37（CAA有效，AUROC 0.765），面部模态为-0.48（CAA失效，异常检测有效，AUROC 0.751）。等权重晚期融合达到AUROC 0.802（95% CI [0.70,0.89]），NPV为0.95。过度拟合审计显示语音检测器迁移良好，但面部及融合AUROC可能过于乐观，需外部验证。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了无需任何PD标签即可构建有效筛查系统的可行性，通过合成退化方向和异常检测两种互补策略分别处理语音和面部模态。融合系统的高NPV支持其作为排除性分诊工具的潜力，但面部模态的结果仍需外部数据集的进一步验证。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) is the second most common neurodegenerative disorder. Typical machine learning screening methods require PD labels, but the available data is limited by privacy concerns and the need for expert annotation. We propose a label-free face-plus-voice PD screen built entirely on frozen pretrained encoders--a face-expression Vision Transformer and HuBERT--in which no PD label touches any fit; the reference is training controls only. The voice modality uses a synthetic-dysarthria contrastive activation addition (CAA) direction built from time-stretch and breathy degradation of healthy speech; the face modality uses a k-nearest-neighbor anomaly score to the control embedding cluster. We introduce the alignment principle, a post-hoc analysis showing that a synthetic-degradation CAA detector works when the cosine similarity between the synthetic and real disease directions exceeds zero. Measured on the YouTubePD benchmark, this cosine is +0.37 for voice (CAA works, AUROC 0.765) and -0.48 for face (CAA fails; anomaly succeeds, AUROC 0.751). Equal-weight late fusion reaches AUROC 0.802 (95% CI [0.70,0.89]) with NPV 0.95, supporting a rule-out triage interpretation. An overfitting audit shows the voice detector transfers cleanly, while the face-side--and thus fused--AUROC is potentially optimistic pending external validation.

</details>


### [112] [Twin Rollouts: Noise-Coupled Counterfactual Branching in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08982)
*Yu Ma,Hongli Shi,Xinran Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在交互式视频世界模型中生成反事实轨迹的框架，通过"噪声耦合孪生展开"（noise-coupled twin rollouts）实现精确的溯因步骤，从而绕过了编辑式管线中的近似反演难题，并将最小变更原则转化为可逐样本验证的时空局部性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互式视频世界模型虽然在动作流条件下生成展开，但训练和评估几乎完全基于事实预测。作者希望研究反事实生成：给定模型自身生成的轨迹，如果从某一步起采用不同动作，会发生什么？这需要形式化的框架来定义和验证反事实生成的正确性。

Method: 提出噪声耦合孪生展开框架：事实分支与反事实分支共享生成的prefix和未来外生噪声序列，仅干预点之后的动作流不同。由于事实分支是自生成的，其外生噪声精确可知，Pearl反事实程序中的溯因步骤可以精确完成。噪声耦合将最小变更原则转化为逐样本可验证的时空局部性度量，通过分割模拟器状态获取反事实真值渲染作为可验证奖励用于后训练。

Result: 本文目前仅建立了形式化框架、度量定义和定位，实验部分尚未完成（forthcoming）。因此尚无实证结果呈现。

Conclusion: 该框架的贡献在于：1）通过自生成轨迹实现精确溯因，避免近似反演问题；2）将最小变更原则变成可验证的逐样本属性；3）利用模拟器真值作为奖励进行后训练。属于理论/方法笔记性质的工作。

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate rollouts autoregressively under an action stream, yet they are trained and evaluated almost exclusively on factual prediction. We study counterfactual generation inside the rollout: given a trajectory the model has itself generated, what would have happened had the actions differed from step t* onward? We formalize noise-coupled twin rollouts --- a factual and a counterfactual branch sharing the generated prefix and the future exogenous noise sequence, diverging only in the action stream at an intervention point. Because the factual branch is self-generated, its exogenous noise is known exactly: the abduction step of Pearl's counterfactual procedure is exact by construction, sidestepping the approximate-inversion problem faced by editing-based pipelines. Noise coupling further turns the minimal-change principle into a per-sample verifiable property: we define a spatiotemporal locality metric that penalizes divergence outside the causal descendants of the intervention, computable against simulator ground truth without a learned judge. Forking the simulator state at t* yields ground-truth counterfactual re-renders, which we use as verifiable rewards for post-training. This note establishes the formal framework, metric definitions, and positioning; experiments are forthcoming.

</details>


### [113] [SoftMCC: An MCC-Brier Calibration Bridge for Threshold-Free Model Selection under Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08984)
*Özkan Canay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftMCC，一种基于后训练概率混淆计数的MCC验证框架，通过协方差归一化的概率-标签关联分数进行模型选择，在18个设置中展现了最优的排序稳定性，但效用优势有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不平衡二分类的模型选择常使用MCC，但硬阈值化使得验证排序依赖阈值选择，缺乏对概率输出的直接利用，需要一种阈值无关且稳定的MCC族验证指标。

Method: SoftMCC将传统的硬预测混淆计数扩展为概率值的混淆计数，设计了MCC特定的校准恒等式，并采用平局感知的共享池选择协议。核心分数是协方差归一化的概率-标签关联量，对硬预测时精确退化为MCC，且受Pearson界约束。

Result: 在18个设置、12次去重分组的重复实验中，SoftMCC获得最佳稳定性平均排名(2.31)和最高的平局修正Kendall W(0.659)，Friedman检验显著(p=0.007)。但在所选模型的效用上无显著优势：三个预设比较中的六个存在负的平均测试MCC差值，仅F1@best通过Holm校正(p=0.014)，数据集级检验不显著(p=0.117)。标签置换将平均W降至0.092；温度缩放会改变SoftMCC排序（平均Spearman=0.851），而基于排名和阈值优化的指标保持不变。

Conclusion: SoftMCC是一个对校准敏感的MCC族模型选择器，具有有界的稳定性证据和有限的效用证据，适合作为阈值依赖MCC的替代方案，但需注意其对概率校准的敏感性。

Abstract: Model selection for imbalanced binary classification often uses the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC), but thresholding makes validation rankings threshold-dependent. SoftMCC is a post-training MCC validation framework on established probability-valued confusion counts, coupling an MCC-specific calibrated identity with a tie-aware, shared-pool selection protocol. Its core score is a covariance-normalized probability-label association, reduces exactly to MCC for hard predictions, and is Pearson-bounded. Under perfect population calibration it equals the Brier skill score with identical candidate ordering; outside that regime the gap does not identify calibration error. Across 18 settings with 12 duplicate-safe grouped repeats, SoftMCC attains the best stability mean rank (2.31) and highest mean tie-corrected Kendall's W (0.659), with a significant Friedman test (p=0.007); Nemenyi analysis separates it from AUPRC and MCC@0.5, while 14-source-family sensitivity retains only the latter. Selected-model utility shows no advantage. Three of six prespecified comparisons have negative mean test-MCC differences, only F1@best survives Holm correction (p=0.014), and the dataset-level test is not significant (p=0.117). Label permutation lowers mean W to 0.092; temperature scaling shifts SoftMCC rankings (mean Spearman 0.851) whereas rank-based and threshold-optimized metrics remain invariant. SoftMCC is a calibration-sensitive MCC-family selector with bounded stability and utility evidence.

</details>


### [114] [Dynamic Distribution-Aware Uncertainty Tracking in Vision-Language Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09011)
*Ao Zhou,Zhiwei Jiang,Zifeng Cheng,Cong Wang,Shufan Yang,Haoru Chen,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DDA-UQ框架，通过从静态映射转向动态分布感知过程，利用高斯混合模型建模VLM嵌入空间来动态估计不确定性，有效解决了后验方法局限于源域失败模式拟合的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后验不确定性量化方法仅能拟合源域的失败模式，忽略了测试分布动态变化的特性，在安全关键场景中难以保证可靠性。

Method: 训练阶段使用高斯混合模型对VLM的嵌入空间进行建模，提取分布证据以动态推导不确定性估计；推理阶段动态响应数据分布的变化。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DDA-UQ通过动态分布感知的不确定性量化框架，成功弥补了后验方法的静态局限性，为VLM在安全关键场景中的部署提供了更可靠的不确定性保障。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) aims to measure the reliability of model predictions, serving as a critical safeguard for deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in safety-critical scenarios. Post-hoc approaches are widely adopted due to their lightweight nature, mapping the outputs of VLMs to uncertainty measures through learnable modules or inductive summarization. However, Post-hoc approaches remain inherently confined to fitting the failure patterns of the source domain, ignoring the dynamic nature of test distributions. To address this challenge, we propose a Dynamic Distribution-Aware Uncertainty Quantification framework (DDA-UQ) that shifts the paradigm from static mapping to a dynamic distribution-aware process. During training, we leverage a Gaussian Mixture Model to model the VVLMs'embedding space and extract distributional evidence, thereby dynamically deriving uncertainty estimates. During inference, the design dynamically responds to changes in the data distribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [115] [HOPPER: Learnable Hop Extraction for Linearized Graph Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09031)
*Isuru Herath,Arin Gopakumar,Sharan Sahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HOPPER，一种端到端可学习的线性化图序列模型（LGSM）扩展框架，通过学习如何提取跳数序列，再由现代状态空间模型处理，实现自适应传播机制，在长程图表示学习任务上取得最优或竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络通过重复消息传递层传播信息，导致信息传播距离与非线性变换次数耦合，引发过平滑、过压缩和长程信息丢失等问题。现有LGSM虽然通过分离信息深度与处理深度来解决这一问题，但使用固定的图算子构建序列，无法根据输入图和节点特征自适应调整传播方式。

Method: HOPPER是一个可学习的序列提取器，在LGSM框架中学习跳数序列的提取方式，支持特征条件化、结构感知、图和跳数自适应的传播机制，同时保持排列等变性。标准的基于邻接矩阵和非回溯的LGSM序列是该提取器族的特例。该框架还允许调整消息回溯消除的最大邻域大小（结构记忆窗口）。

Result: 在ECHO-Synth基准上取得最先进或具有竞争力的性能；在LRIM物理长程依赖基准上，通过改变结构记忆窗口大小可以优化准确率，证明了可学习序列提取的有效性。

Conclusion: 可学习的序列提取为长程图表示学习提供了一种灵活且有效的途径，HOPPER在多个基准上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Graph neural networks typically propagate information through repeated message-passing layers, coupling the distance over which information travels with the number of nonlinear transformations applied. This coupling can make deep architectures difficult to optimize and can lead to over-smoothing, over-squashing, and the loss of long-range information. Linearized Graph Sequence Models (LGSMs) address this issue by separating information depth from processing depth and treating the successive propagation states of each node as a sequence. However, existing LGSMs construct these sequences using fixed graph operators, limiting their ability to adapt propagation to the input graph, node features, and downstream task. We introduce HOPPER, an end-to-end learnable extension of LGSM that learns how hop sequences should be extracted before they are processed by a modern state-space model. Our framework supports feature-conditioned, structure-aware, graph- and hop-adaptive propagation mechanisms while preserving permutation equivariance. Standard adjacency-based and non-backtracking LGSM sequences arise as special cases of our proposed extractor family. We show that HOPPER is state-of-the-art or competitive across the ECHO-Synth benchmark, and that varying the maximum neighborhood size of message backtracking cancellation (i.e. structural memory window) can optimize accuracy on the LRIM physics-based long-range dependency benchmark. These results demonstrate that learnable sequence extraction provides a flexible and effective approach to long-range graph representation learning.

</details>


### [116] [F2STNet: Fair and Federated Spectral-Temporal Modeling for Graph Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09082)
*Jiayi Zhang,Jinfeng Xu,Hewei Wang,Siyuan Cen,Haidong Huang,Yiyao Zhan,Zheyu Chen,Jinjiang You,Ai Jian,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出F²STNet，一个联邦时空预测框架，结合截断图傅里叶特征、轻量对角状态空间时序编码器、图卷积和公平感知联邦聚合（FFA），在PeMS04、HZMetro和KnowAir数据集上取得良好预测精度，并在联邦实验中改善了最差客户端性能和客户端分散度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构数据的时空预测（如交通预测、环境监测）面临两个核心挑战：一是去中心化且异质的客户端数据使得序列建模和协作训练变得复杂；二是现有方法在处理长时序依赖和联邦学习中的客户端公平性问题时存在不足。

Method: F²STNet包含三个关键组件：(1) 截断图-傅里叶特征提取光谱分支，揭示图频率结构；(2) 轻量对角状态空间时序编码器，以序列长度线性复杂度建模长时序依赖；(3) 图卷积模块；(4) 公平感知联邦聚合（FFA），利用客户端验证损失和递增的公平性调度来调整FedAvg先验。

Result: 在PeMS04、HZMetro和KnowAir三个数据集上，与评估的基线方法相比取得了更优的预测精度；在PeMS04上的联邦实验中，进一步改善了最差客户端性能以及客户端间性能分散度指标。

Conclusion: F²STNet通过融合光谱特征与状态空间建模，并结合公平感知的联邦聚合策略，有效解决了去中心化异质数据下的时空预测与联邦协作训练问题，兼顾了预测精度与客户端公平性。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal prediction on graph-structured data is central to traffic forecasting and environmental monitoring, yet decentralized and heterogeneous data complicate both sequence modeling and collaborative training. We propose F$^2$STNet, a federated forecasting framework that combines truncated graph-Fourier features, a lightweight diagonal state-space temporal encoder, graph convolution, and Fairness-aware Federated Aggregation (FFA). The spectral branch exposes graph-frequency structure, while the state-space layer models long temporal dependencies with linear complexity in the sequence length. FFA adjusts the FedAvg prior using client validation losses and an increasing fairness schedule. Experiments on PeMS04, HZMetro, and KnowAir show favorable forecasting accuracy relative to the evaluated baselines; federated experiments on PeMS04 additionally improve worst-client and client-dispersion metrics.

</details>


### [117] [RAVEN: Frozen Random Graph Reservoirs with Physics-Informed Interaction Fingerprints for Protein-Ligand Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09099)
*Qingyang Zou,Jiaye Huang,Hangbo Xie,Jiayue Yin,Youyi Song,Jinfeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAVEN利用多头随机初始化的冻结原子图编码器生成多样化的结构投影，结合物理化学相互作用指纹，通过异质监督回归器（包括神经网络和树模型）进行验证驱动的非负融合，在PDBbind 2020R1和CASF-2016数据集上实现了强大的蛋白-配体结合亲和力预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白-配体结合亲和力的定量预测是结构计算化学和分子建模中的基本任务，但现有数据有限、实验异质性高、构象依赖性强且对数据集划分敏感，导致可靠预测仍具挑战性。

Method: RAVEN方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 多head reservoir——多个独立初始化且完全冻结的原子级图编码器生成多样化结构投影，无需端到端优化图表示；(2) 确定性物理化学相互作用指纹作为显式描述符；(3) 异质监督读者（神经网络和基于树的回归器）集成，通过基于验证集的非负融合合并输出。

Result: 在基于GEMS相似性资源重建的相似性隔离PDBbind 2020R1划分以及受保护的CASF-2016子集上的评估均展现了强大的预测性能。

Conclusion: 冻结的多视图图表示、显式物理化学统计和异质模型融合为蛋白-配体结合亲和力预测提供了一个鲁棒且灵活的框架。

Abstract: Quantitative estimation of protein-ligand binding affinity from three-dimensional complex structures is a fundamental task in structure-based computational chemistry and molecular modeling. Reliable prediction remains challenging because available structure-affinity data are limited, experimentally heterogeneous, conformation-dependent, and sensitive to dataset partitioning. RAVEN (Randomized Atomistic Views with Ensemble Neural Reservoirs) utilizes a multihead reservoir of independently initialized and fully frozen atomistic graph encoders to generate diverse structural projections without end-to-end optimization of the graph representation. These projections are integrated with a deterministic physicochemical interaction fingerprint and processed by heterogeneous supervised readers, including neural and tree-based regressors, whose outputs are combined through validation-based nonnegative fusion. The random reservoir expands structural feature coverage across independent encoder realizations, whereas the explicit physicochemical descriptors and heterogeneous readers contribute complementary information and distinct inductive biases. Evaluation on a similarity-isolated PDBbind 2020R1 split reconstructed using GEMS similarity resources, together with the protected CASF-2016 subset, demonstrated strong predictive performance. The results indicate that frozen multi-view graph representations, explicit physicochemical statistics, and heterogeneous model fusion provide a robust and flexible framework for protein-ligand binding-affinity prediction.

</details>


### [118] [Real Data Closes Synthetic-to-Real Gap in Optical Chemical Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09100)
*Yani Guan,Dengpan Dong,Zi Wei,Shuang Luo,Dan Hannah,Yumin Zhang,Kang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了光学化学结构识别（OCSR）在实际文档上的表现，发现标注真实训练数据是提升效果的最关键因素，而视觉塔适配（如LoRA）的价值因基础模型而异。最佳配置在干净渲染图上达到0.96的精确匹配率，在多个真实基准上显著提升，并指出基础模型与真实数据配比必须一同选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数百万化学结构仅以图形形式出现在专利和论文中，大规模利用这些信息需要读取图纸。尽管OCSR在合成图像上看似已解决，但在真实文档上表现仍差（如Qwen2.5-VL在合成图达91%以上，真实基准却低于16%），需要找出提升的主要来源。

Method: 对21个识别器进行微调，在合成渲染结构和标注真实图（专利、期刊图形、手绘集合）的混合数据上进行训练，变化视觉语言模型（VLM）基础、真实训练数据比例和视觉塔适配策略。另外进行了手写图像到LaTeX识别和图表到表格转换的小规模实验。

Result: 标注真实图像贡献最大：Qwen2.5-VL的ACS精确匹配从0.15（无真实数据）提升至0.37（9.5%）和0.46（50.2%）。视觉塔LoRA对Qwen无帮助（+0.00），对InternVL3-8B帮助显著（+22.8至+34.6pt），对GLM-4.1V-9B帮助温和（+1.0至+9.6pt）。最佳配置达到0.96（干净渲染）、0.49/0.65/0.84/0.76（ACS/CLEF-IP/UOB/USPTO）。基础模型差距从无真实数据时的0.21缩小至70%真实数据时的0.06。

Conclusion: 视觉结构识别的模型和适配选择应在目标任务上进行评估。基础模型与真实数据配比必须协同选择，因为不同基础模型对真实数据和视觉塔适配策略的响应差异很大，且这种排名变化也延伸到化学领域之外。

Abstract: Millions of chemical structures appear in patents and papers only as drawings, and using that information at scale requires reading the drawings. OCSR appears nearly solved on synthetic images yet remains difficult on real documents: the starting recognizer, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, exceeds 91% accuracy on synthetic renders but falls below 16% on three real-world benchmarks (ACS, CLEF-IP, USPTO). To identify the main source of improvement, 21 recognizers were fine-tuned on mixtures of synthetically rendered structures and labeled real depictions from patents, journal figures, and hand-drawn collections, varying the vision language model (VLM) base, the fraction of real training data, and the vision-tower adaptation strategy. Labeled real training images make the largest difference. For Qwen2.5-VL, ACS exact match rises from 0.15 with no real data to 0.37 at 9.5% and 0.46 at 50.2%; a controlled experiment across three base models reproduces the trend. A vision-tower LoRA, in contrast, does nothing for Qwen (+0.00, paired p=1.00), substantially helps InternVL3-8B (+22.8 to +34.6 pt), and modestly helps GLM-4.1V-9B (+1.0 to +9.6 pt), so its value depends on the base model. The best configuration reaches 0.96 exact match on clean renders and 0.49, 0.65, 0.84, and 0.76 on ACS, CLEF-IP, UOB, and USPTO, respectively. Gaps between base models are largest without real data (0.21), shrink to 0.06 at 70% real data, and reorder the ranking; base model and real-data mixture must therefore be selected together. Small-scale experiments on handwritten image-to-LaTeX recognition and chart-to-table conversion show that base-model rankings also vary beyond chemistry. More generally, model and adaptation choices for visual structure recognition should be evaluated on the target task.

</details>


### [119] [A Probabilistic Circuit-Induced Pseudo-Metric for Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09117)
*Bhumika K,Vidhya S,Narayanan C Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率电路（PC）层次结构的新型OOD检测方法。通过引入层次似然向量（HLV）和层次似然距离（HLD），利用PC内部节点的概率摘要信息进行分布外检测，无需额外的留出数据，并可通过假设检验实现无监督OOD检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于PC的OOD检测方法忽略了PC内部节点编码的层次概率摘要信息，仅将整个电路简化为根节点处的标量似然（或其不确定性）进行计算，未能充分利用PC的层次结构优势。

Method: 提出HLV表示：将选定PC节点的似然值组成向量；定义HLD伪度量：通过HLV期望比较概率分布；证明HLD是PC自然诱导函数类上的积分概率度量；构建基于拟合优度的无监督OOD检测假设检验；所有所需量可从训练好的PC中精确计算，得到近似解析决策阈值。

Result: 在表格数据集和MNIST数据集上的实验表明，利用PC编码的层次概率摘要比基于根似然、不确定性、典型性和核方法等基线方法显著提升了OOD检测性能，并能自然地将分布偏移定位到导致偏移的PC节点上。

Conclusion: 利用PC的层次结构可以显著提高OOD检测的效果，且只需训练好的PC本身即可作为分布内数据的表示，无需在部署时使用留出的分布内数据，同时具备分布偏移定位能力。

Abstract: Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) are tractable generative models whose internal nodes encode a hierarchy of probabilistic sum- maries over different variable scopes. Existing PC-based out- of-distribution (OOD) detection methods ignore this hierar- chy, reducing the entire circuit to the scalar likelihood (or its uncertainty) computed at the root. We introduce Hierar- chical Likelihood Vector (HLV), a representation whose en- tries are the likelihoods associated with selected PC nodes and define the Hierarchical Likelihood Distance (HLD), a PC-induced pseudo-metric that compares the probability dis- tributions through the expectations of their HLVs. We show that HLD is an integral probability metric over a function class naturally induced by the PC and develop a principled goodness-of-fit hypothesis test for unsupervised OOD detec- tion. Unlike existing approaches, the trained PC alone serves as the representation of the in-distribution: no held-out in- distribution data are required at deployment. We further show that the quantities required by the hypothesis test can be com- puted exactly, directly from the trained circuit, yielding an ap- proximate analytic decision threshold. Experiments on tabular and MNIST datasets demonstrate that exploiting the hierarchi- cal probabilistic summaries encoded through the PC improve OOD detection over root-likelihood, uncertainty-, typicality- and kernel-based baselines, while naturally localizing distri- bution shifts to the PC nodes responsible for the shift.

</details>


### [120] [MARA: Flow-Matching-Guided Multi-Agent Resource Allocation for Computational Resource Efficient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09130)
*Hanye Zhao,Muning Wen,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARA框架，在计算资源由离散节点提供、任务带截止时间的实际场景中，通过条件流匹配预测损失轨迹、多智能体自回归策略协调节点，实现计算资源的自适应调度。相比基线LARA，任务完成率提升8.54个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在并发学习任务中，每个任务需在截止时间前达到目标损失，但训练所需算力未知。现有方法将计算视为连续可分的吞吐量，忽略了实际场景中计算由离散节点提供、任务随时间到达的特点，导致决策面临需求不确定和顺序约束的双重挑战。

Method: 提出MARA框架：1）利用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）预测未来损失轨迹，替代传统的加权最小二乘；2）通过基于势能的进度奖励（potential-based progress reward）提供中间训练反馈，同时保持无折扣的任务完成目标；3）采用协同多智能体自回归策略协调离散计算节点的分配。

Result: 在分布内、强化学习和视觉任务负载上，流匹配相较加权最小二乘显著降低了剩余资源预测误差。在调度器训练负载下，MARA平均完成63.46%的任务，比强基线LARA高8.54个百分点；在未见过的更重负载下仍保持领先。

Conclusion: MARA在更贴近实际的离散节点计算调度场景中，通过更优的损失轨迹预测和多智能体协同调度，实现了超越现有方法的任务完成率，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Allocating limited computation among concurrent learning tasks is difficult when each task must reach a target loss before a deadline but its required training effort is unknown. Existing approaches combine online loss prediction with adaptive resource allocation, yet commonly treat computation as continuously divisible throughput. We instead study a practical setting in which tasks arrive over time and computation is provided by discrete nodes. This setting introduces both uncertain demand and constrained sequential decisions. We propose MARA, which predicts future loss trajectories with conditional flow matching and coordinates compute nodes through a cooperative multi-agent autoregressive policy. A potential-based progress reward supplies intermediate training feedback while preserving the undiscounted task-completion objective. Across in-distribution, reinforcement-learning, and vision workloads, flow matching reduces remaining-resource prediction error relative to weighted least squares. At the scheduler's training load, MARA completes 63.46% of tasks on average, 8.54 percentage points above strong baseline Learning with Adaptive Resource Allocation (LARA), and remains ahead under unseen heavier workloads.

</details>


### [121] [Tabular Numeric Stretch Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09162)
*Zihao Ye,Juyong Kim,Johnna Sundberg,Burak Varici,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"stretch变换"框架，将表格数据中数值特征的预处理形式化为优化问题，通过使目标函数更平滑来提升深度学习的可学习性，包含无监督和监督两种变体，并在38个数据集的TALENT基准上验证了监督stretch的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中数值特征具有异质性（分布、尺度、统计性质多样），现有工作主要聚焦于模型架构的改进，但对数值特征如何变换为模型友好的表示这一关键环节研究不足。

Method: 提出stretch变换框架：(1) 无监督stretch，通过minimax优化均匀重分配特征密度；(2) 监督stretch，通过最小化变换空间中目标函数的Dirichlet能量来优化目标感知的数值特征变换。理论分析将该框架与分段线性编码、经验CDF变换以及目标编码建立联系。

Result: 在TALENT基准的38个数据集上，监督stretch一致性地优于所有基线方法。理论分析表明无监督stretch与分段线性编码共享分段线性几何结构、在bins增长时趋近于经验CDF变换；监督stretch在细粒度分箱极限下与目标编码密切相关。

Conclusion: 显式优化目标函数的平滑性是表格深度学习中一个强大且尚未充分探索的策略，stretch变换框架为其提供了系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular data presents unique challenges for deep learning due to its heterogeneous nature, where numeric features exhibit diverse distributions, scales, and statistical properties. Although recent advances have improved how models learn from tabular data, how numeric data are transformed into model-friendly representations remains comparatively underexplored. We introduce the stretch transformation framework, which formulates numeric feature preprocessing as an optimization problem to make the target function smoother and thus more learnable. Our framework has two variants: (1) unsupervised stretch, which uniformly redistributes feature density via minimax optimization, and (2) supervised stretch, which optimizes target-aware numeric feature transformations from the perspective of target-function smoothness by minimizing the target function's Dirichlet energy in the transformed space. Our theoretical analysis further connects this framework to several popular transformations: unsupervised stretch is closely related to Piecewise Linear Encoding through a shared piecewise-linear geometry and approaches the empirical CDF transformation as the number of bins grows, while supervised stretch becomes closely related to target encoding in the fine-binning limit. Comprehensive experiments on 38 datasets from the TALENT benchmark demonstrate that supervised stretch consistently outperforms all baselines. These results show that explicitly optimizing for target function smoothness is a powerful and underexplored strategy for tabular deep learning.

</details>


### [122] [A Time-Frequency Dual-Domain Multi-Scale Convolutional Neural Network for Bearing Fault Diagnosis under Strong Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09174)
*Yanxi Ding,Tingyue Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时频双域多尺度卷积神经网络用于强噪声下的轴承故障诊断，通过时域多尺度卷积核和频域FFT特征提取进行双分支特征融合，在CWRU数据集上实现99.75%清洁精度和92.50%（-4dB SNR）精度，优于多种对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强噪声环境下轴承故障诊断精度严重退化，现有单域方法在噪声条件下的鲁棒性不足，需要设计能够同时捕获时域冲击特征和频域抗噪结构信息的方法。

Method: 构建时频双域多尺度卷积神经网络：时域分支使用三个并行卷积核提取多尺度冲击特征，频域分支通过快速傅里叶变换（FFT）提取频谱结构信息，然后将两个分支特征融合进行分类。

Result: 模型参数量仅110,122；在CWRU数据集上，清洁条件下精度99.75%，-4dB SNR下为92.50%，相比单域基线提升7.25个百分点，且噪声越强增益越大；消融实验和对比实验（WDCNN、DRSN-CW、MCNN、1D-LeNet）验证了方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 时频双域多尺度卷积神经网络能够有效应对强噪声下的轴承故障诊断挑战，双分支结构各自贡献独立性能，且模型紧凑、参数量少，具有工程应用潜力。

Abstract: To address the degradation of bearing fault diagnosis accuracy under strong noise, this paper proposes a time-frequency dual-domain multi-scale convolutional neural network. The time-domain branch employs three parallel convolutional kernels to capture multi-scale impulse features, while the frequency-domain branch applies the Fast Fourier Transform to extract noise-robust spectral structure information. Features from both branches are fused for fault classification, yielding a compact model of 110,122 parameters. Experiments on the CWRU bearing dataset across seven signal-to-noise ratio levels demonstrate that the proposed method achieves 99.75% accuracy under clean conditions and maintains 92.50% at -4 dB SNR, representing a 7.25 percentage-point improvement over the single-domain baseline with monotonically increasing gains under stronger noise. Ablation experiments validate the independent performance contributions of the time-domain multi-scale branch and the frequency-domain branch. Comparative experiments against WDCNN, DRSN-CW, MCNN, and 1D-LeNet confirm the superiority of the proposed method under strong noise conditions.

</details>


### [123] [Beyond Solvability: Task Learnability as a Static Prior for LLM RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09217)
*Ting Zhou,Zhenqing Ling,Daoyuan Chen,Qianli Shen,Yilun Huang,Ying Shen,Yaliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajVal，一种基于轨迹的轻量级任务可学习性估计器，通过短探针运行和两个端点评估来预测任务对后续强化学习训练的响应程度，从而改进任务采样策略，提升数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练中任务均匀采样忽视了不同任务对优化响应的差异，而现有任务估值方法仅基于当前策略下的快照信号（如通过率或奖励），无法区分当前可解性相似但后续训练响应差异显著的任务，因此需要一种能提前预测任务可学习性的方法。

Method: 首先通过分析逐任务奖励轨迹，定义任务可学习性为在固定RL训练体制下对持续训练的正向响应的期望；然后提出TrajVal，一种基于探针的轻量级估计器，从短探针运行和两个端点评估（起点和终点）近似估计每个任务的可学习性；TrajVal可用作独立静态先验或作为乘法先验集成到现有在线调度器中。

Result: 在数学和逻辑推理基准上跨多个模型规模的实验表明，TrajVal相对于均匀采样提高了数据效率，与在线调度方法结合时提供了互补增益；可学习性在独立采样的训练上下文中具有可重现性，并能预测下游效用。

Conclusion: 任务可学习性是一个独立于当前可解性的重要维度，TrajVal能够以轻量方式有效估计该信号，显著改善RL训练的任务采样效率和最终性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central post-training paradigm for eliciting reasoning capabilities in large language models, yet uniform task sampling allocates compute without regard to differences in how tasks respond to optimization. Existing task-valuation methods mostly rely on snapshot-based signals such as current pass rate or reward, which estimate how solvable a task is under the current policy. However, tasks with similar current solvability can still differ substantially in how positively they respond to further training. We study this residual axis as task learnability: a regime-conditional measure of expected positive response to continued training under a fixed RL post-training regime. By analyzing per-task reward trajectories, we find that learnability is reproducible across independently sampled training contexts and predictive of downstream utility. To make this signal practical before training begins, we propose TrajVal, a lightweight probe-based estimator that approximates per-task learnability from a short probe run and two endpoint evaluations. TrajVal can be used either as a standalone static prior for task sampling or as a multiplicative prior for existing online schedulers. Experiments on mathematical and logical reasoning benchmarks across multiple model scales show that TrajVal improves data efficiency over uniform sampling and provides complementary gains when combined with online scheduling methods.

</details>


### [124] [Online Learning of Scale Parameters in Score-Driven Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09218)
*Fabrizio Lillo,Giulia Livieri,Gianluca Palmari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了得分驱动滤波器中增益参数的在线学习问题，将增益视为决策变量，通过KL散度目标进行条件预测决策，并建立了动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 得分驱动滤波器中的增益参数传统上是预先设定的常数或通过网格搜索选取，但最优增益可能随时间变化且依赖于当前状态。作者希望将增益选择形式化为在线学习问题，从而动态调整以提升预测性能。

Method: 将增益视为决策变量，条件于当前状态、观测、得分和缩放规则，每个可行增益诱导一个可达的下一个状态和一阶超前预测密度。标量增益控制沿一条线的距离，对角增益控制坐标方向传输。将增益选择视为KL目标下的条件预测决策问题。对于标量未缩放增益，连续得分的负原始乘积是损失的随机梯度；aGAS正缩放仅重新缩放有效步长。单调可微增益链接在有限增益域上诱导镜像下降几何，而持久性产生Bregman向参考增益的拉动。在凸性、紧性和正则性条件下，建立投影和折扣镜像更新的动态遗憾界。

Result: 模拟实验展示了缩放、链接几何、持久性和坐标方向传输率的作用。实证中，使用有界镜像增益的股票指数波动率面板预测，在大多数情况下匹配或优于常数增益，同时避免了名义上无界指数链接的极端尖峰，在多危机市场中改善最为显著。

Conclusion: 动态学习增益比固定常数增益更具稳健性，尤其在多危机市场中表现突出；有界镜像链接优于无界链接，提供了理论保障和实证验证。

Abstract: Score-driven filters multiply a scaled log-likelihood score by a gain that controls the update magnitude. We treat this gain as a decision variable and study its online learning. Conditional on the current state, observation, score, and scaling rule, each admissible gain induces a reachable next state and a one-step-ahead predictive density: scalar gains govern distance along a line, while diagonal gains govern coordinatewise transmission. Gain selection is therefore a conditional predictive decision problem with a Kullback-Leibler objective. For a scalar unscaled gain, the negative raw product of consecutive scores is the stochastic gradient of this loss; positive aGAS scaling only rescales the effective step. Monotone differentiable gain links induce mirror-descent geometries on bounded gain domains, while persistence yields a Bregman pull towards a reference gain. Under convexity, compactness, and regularity conditions, we establish dynamic-regret bounds for projected and discounted mirror updates relative to time-varying, current-information comparators. Simulations illustrate the roles of scaling, link geometry, persistence, and coordinatewise transmission rates. An out-of-sample panel of equity-index volatilities shows that the bounded mirror gain generally matches or outperforms a constant gain while avoiding the extreme spikes of a nominally unbounded exponential link, with the strongest improvements observed in multi-crisis markets.

</details>


### [125] [Privileged Solutions or Context-Induced Teacher Behavior? Dissecting On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09228)
*Yuki Ichihara,Naoto Iwase,Mohammad Atif Quamar,Junpei Komiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）的传统解释，提出教师监督学生时不仅传递了参考解信息，还改变了教师提供标记级监督的上下文。作者提出OP²SD方法，用其他问题的参考解替换配对的参考解，在三个模型和三个数学基准上验证了其与OPSD竞争力相当，说明OPSD的提升不一定来自参考解本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为OPSD的成功源于特权信息的传递（教师看到正确答案后指导学生），但作者指出这一解释混淆了两个效应：参考解不仅揭示了当前实例的答案，还改变了教师进行标记级监督的上下文。需要解耦这两个因素来理解OPSD真正的工作机制。

Method: 作者设计了OP²SD（On-Policy Self-Distillation from Other Problems）方法，将配对的参考解替换为来自不同示例的问题和解，同时保持学生rollout、教师和蒸馏目标不变，从而隔离参考解内容本身的影响，只保留教师上下文变化的影响。

Result: 在三个模型和三个数学基准上，OP²SD相比基线模型有提升，且与OPSD表现相当。这表明即使参考解来自其他问题，蒸馏仍然有效。

Conclusion: OPSD的提升并非必然来自参考解的访问权，教师的上下文诱导行为（context-induced behavior）才是重要因素。这推翻了对OPSD机制的简单理解，为后续蒸馏方法的设计提供了新的视角。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) is commonly interpreted as the transfer of privileged information: a teacher observes the verified solution to the target problem and supervises the student's trajectory. However, this interpretation conflates two effects. The reference solution not only reveals the answer to the current instance but also changes the context under which the teacher provides token-level supervision. We investigate the role of target-specific privilege with $\mathrm{OP}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$ (On-Policy Self-Distillation from Other Problems), which replaces the paired reference with a problem and solution from a different example, while preserving the student rollout, teacher, and distillation objective. Across three models and three mathematics benchmarks, $\mathrm{OP}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$ improves over the base model, remains competitive with OPSD. The success of $\mathrm{OP}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$ implies that OPSD gains do not necessarily come from access to the reference solution, and that the teacher's context-induced behavior is an important factor.

</details>


### [126] [DreOPD: Degraded-Reference Extrapolative On-Policy Distillation for Flow-matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09233)
*Mingfeng Lin,Chengfei Cai,Lin Xu,Yuxiang Wei,Liang Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DreOPD方法，通过将隐式奖励外推转化为闭式速度回归，实现流匹配模型的外推式后期训练，兼顾强化学习的直接奖励优化与OPD的密集稳定监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型在下游场景适配中存在挑战：后期训练可能引发多任务目标冲突，轨迹级强化学习存在高方差梯度和跨任务干扰，而传统OPD的教师匹配仍属模仿式学习，缺乏外推能力。

Method: DreOPD将隐式奖励外推转化为闭式速度回归，利用轻度退化参考增强教师-参考对比度，取得更清晰的外推方向，从而在稳定监督下实现外推式后期训练。

Result: 在单教师和多教师设置下的实验中，DreOPD在平均性能上优于OPD和多任务RL基线，并在大多数指标上超越专用教师模型。

Conclusion: DreOPD成功桥接了强化学习直接奖励优化与OPD稳定监督两大范式，为流匹配模型的外推式后期训练提供了有效且稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Flow-matching models are now a mainstream method to image generation, but its adaptation to diverse downstream scenarios typically relies on post-training, which may cause conflicts among task-specific optimization objectives. Reinforcement learning enables direct optimization of task-specific rewards beyond the original models, yet trajectory-level optimization may incur high-variance gradients and cross-task interference. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers dense and stable supervision on student rollouts, but conventional teacher matching remains imitation-based. We propose DreOPD, a Degraded-reference extrapolative OPD method for flow-matching models that bridges these two paradigms. Our DreOPD converts implicit reward extrapolation into closed-form velocity regression, enabling extrapolative post-training with the stability of OPD. It further uses a mildly degraded reference to strengthen the teacher-reference contrast, yielding a clearer extrapolation direction. Experiments on single- and multi-teacher settings show that DreOPD outperforms OPD and multi-task RL baselines in average performance, while surpassing specialized teachers on most metrics.

</details>


### [127] [Label Granularity Skew in Federated Learning with Hierarchical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09236)
*Jaeheon Kim,Hokeun Kim,Bong Jun Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的联邦学习统计异质性形式——标签粒度偏斜（label granularity skew），并提出了分支级解耦微调（BDFT）及其联邦版本FedBDFT，通过条件softmax分类器和分支级微调来应对客户端在共享类层次结构中以不同细节级别提供标签的情况。在CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet和ImageNet上的实验表明，FedBDFT在严重标签粒度偏斜下显著提升鲁棒性，平均提升27.9%和56.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习研究主要关注数据分布异质性，但忽略了客户端在领域知识和标注能力上的差异。实际场景中，客户端可能在同一类层次结构的不同细节级别提供标签，这种标签粒度偏斜会严重影响层级分类模型的性能。现有强耦合层次模型对不完全监督敏感，因此需要新的方法来解决这一挑战。

Method: 本文首先利用概率关系邻居分类器生成客户端特定的局部标签层次，并通过基于轮廓系数的粗化构建WordNet引导的层次结构来建模标签粒度偏斜。基于分析发现条件softmax分类器比强耦合层次模型更鲁棒的洞察，提出Branch-wise Decoupled Fine-Tuning (BDFT)方法，分别微调分支级分类器并通过联邦优化进行聚合，形成联邦版本FedBDFT。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet和ImageNet上，FedBDFT在偏斜水平0.6和0.9下分别平均提升27.9%和56.4%的鲁棒性。零样本结果还表明FedBDFT更好地保留了对未见细粒度类别的层次表征。

Conclusion: FedBDFT有效解决了联邦层次分类中的标签粒度异质性挑战，通过解耦分支级微调显著提升了模型鲁棒性和层次表征质量，为异构标注能力的联邦学习场景提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Federated learning enables privacy-preserving collaboration across distributed devices without centralizing local data. However, clients may differ not only in data distributions but also in domain knowledge and annotation capabilities. In this paper, we introduce label granularity skew, a new form of statistical heterogeneity in federated hierarchical classification, in which clients provide taxonomy-consistent labels at different levels of detail within a shared class hierarchy. To model this heterogeneity, we generate client-specific local label hierarchies using a probabilistic relational neighbor classifier and construct a WordNet-guided hierarchy via silhouette score-based coarsening. Our analysis shows that strongly coupled hierarchical models are sensitive to incomplete supervision, while the conditional softmax classifier is more robust. Based on this insight, we propose Branch-wise Decoupled Fine-Tuning (BDFT) and its federated version, FedBDFT, which fine-tune branch-wise classifiers and aggregate them through federated optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and ImageNet show that FedBDFT substantially improves robustness under severe label granularity skew, with average gains of 27.9% and 56.4% at skewness levels of 0.6 and 0.9, respectively. Zero-shot results further indicate that FedBDFT better preserves hierarchical representations for unseen fine-grained classes. These findings demonstrate its effectiveness for federated hierarchical classification with heterogeneous label granularities.

</details>


### [128] [Multimodal Federated Learning under Dual-Axis Modality Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09240)
*Adiba Orzikulova,Jaehyun Kwak,Jaemin Shin,Yunqi Guo,Xiaomin Ouyang,Guoliang Xing,Steven Euijong Whang,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flux，一个多模态联邦学习框架，通过模态感知置信度调节和梯度解耦的私有适应两个互补组件，统一解决双重维度的模态缺失问题，在四个多模态数据集上取得最优平均宏F1成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态联邦学习方法通常分别处理"客户端维度"和"样本维度"的模态缺失，缺乏统一解决方案。此外，基于置信度的自适应温度调节若直接应用于共享模型，会引入置信度相关的梯度扰动，影响共享表示学习的稳定性。

Method: Flux包含两个组件：1）模态感知置信度调节（通过掩码感知的单模态监督学习样本级置信度，并将观测模态的置信度融合为样本自适应温度）；2）梯度解耦的私有适应（仅将温度应用于客户端私有预测路径，共享联邦模型使用标准无调节目标训练，实现客户端本地置信度适应而不干扰共享表示学习）。

Result: 在四个多模态数据集上，Flux在每项数据集的平均宏F1上均取得最高成绩，比最强数据集特定基线高0.8~2.2个百分点，平均高1.6个百分点。额外分析显示其校准性能良好，温度对模态缺失和输入损坏均具敏感性，且私有化温度调节下共享优化更稳定。

Conclusion: Flux通过统一处理双轴模态缺失并解耦置信度适应与共享表示学习，为隐私敏感的多模态联邦学习提供了更灵活、稳定且高性能的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal federated learning (FL) supports collaborative modeling in privacy-sensitive health-sensing and medical settings, but realistic deployments often exhibit dual-axis modality missingness: clients have different modality sets, and individual samples may contain only subsets of the modalities available locally. Existing methods typically address these two axes separately. We propose Flux, a multimodal federated learning framework built around two complementary components. First, modality-aware confidence tempering learns sample-specific confidence for each modality through mask-aware unimodal supervision and fuses the confidence estimates from observed modalities into a sample-adaptive temperature that adjusts predictive sharpness according to evidence quality and completeness. Second, gradient-decoupled private adaptation applies this temperature only to a client-private prediction pathway, while training the shared federated model with a standard, untempered objective. This enables sample-specific, client-local confidence adaptation without allowing confidence-dependent gradients to perturb shared representation learning. Across four multimodal datasets, Flux achieves the highest average macro-F1 on every dataset, outperforming the strongest dataset-specific baseline by 0.8~2.2 points and by 1.6 points on average. Additional analyses demonstrate favorable calibration, temperature sensitivity to both modality missingness and input corruption, and more stable shared optimization under private-only tempering. Our code is available at https://github.com/AdibaOrz/Flux.

</details>


### [129] [Full-Feature versus Limited-Input Machine Learning for Residential Energy Estimation: A Comparative Analysis of RECS and ResStock Under Realistic Input Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09255)
*Aditya Ramnarayan,Fatih Evren,Patti Gunderson,Samuel Rosenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于RECS和ResStock两个美国住宅能源数据集，比较了CatBoost、XGBoost、LightGBM、随机森林和神经网络等模型在全特征与十个低负担变量条件下的住宅总能耗估计性能。全特征下CatBoost最优（RESC R²=0.73，ResStock R²=0.90）；特征受限后性能收敛至R²≈0.61-0.62，但针对同质化住宅群体的简化模型可达R²=0.85。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅能耗评估常常在缺乏详细建筑围护结构、设备效率及传感器数据的情况下就需要进行，因此需要量化预测精度与输入数据可获取性之间的权衡关系。

Method: 使用RECS（基于调查）和ResStock（基于仿真）两个全国代表性数据集，先建立全特征模型作为性能基准，再限制为十个可从住户、行政记录或位置天气数据获取的低负担变量，比较CatBoost、XGBoost、LightGBM、随机森林和神经网络五种算法的表现，并针对同质化住宅群体进行专门建模。

Result: 全特征下CatBoost表现最优（RECS R²=0.73，ResStock R²=0.90）；受限特征下各模型性能收敛至R²≈0.61-0.62；针对单户独立住宅、天然气供暖、气候区6A、2000-2010年建成的同质化群体，简化输入模型精度提升至R²=0.85。

Conclusion: 基于树的集成模型可作为国家尺度住宅能耗数据集的高保真替代模型，但需充分考虑特征可获取性、数据集来源（经验vs.合成）及适用场景。算法复杂度无法完全弥补物理和行为信息的缺失，针对同质化人群的目标建模可显著提升精度。

Abstract: Residential energy estimates are often needed before detailed envelope characteristics, equipment efficiencies, infiltration, sensor, or billing data are available. This study quantifies the trade-off between predictive accuracy and input accessibility using two nationally representative U.S. residential-energy datasets: the survey-based Residential Energy Consumption Survey (RECS) and the simulation-based ResStock dataset. Full-feature models were first used to establish dataset-specific performance benchmarks. For total-energy estimation, the models were subsequently restricted to ten low-burden variables obtainable from occupants, administrative records, or location-based weather data without an on-site energy audit. Among CatBoost, XGBoost, LightGBM, Random Forest, and Neural Networks, CatBoost consistently achieved the highest predictive performance for the full-feature analysis, reaching R2 = 0.90 for ResStock and R2 = 0.73 for RECS. When the feature set was restricted to ten homeowner-accessible inputs to simulate realistic deployment conditions, model performance converged to R2 = 0.61 for RECS and R2 = 0.62 for ResStock, showing that algorithmic complexity cannot fully compensate for missing physical and behavioral information. However, for a more homogeneous ResStock cohort consisting of single-family detached, natural-gas-heated homes in Climate Zone 6A constructed between 2000 and 2010, a reduced-input model improved accuracy to R2 = 0.85, demonstrating the value of targeted modeling for homogeneous populations. The results indicate that tree-based ensemble models can serve as high-fidelity emulators of national-scale residential energy datasets. However, careful consideration of feature availability, dataset origin (empirical vs. synthetic), and applicable use cases are also important.

</details>


### [130] [SoftmaxGRPO: Learning to Reason using Softmax Advantage Group Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09271)
*Jefferson Hernandez,Jaywon Koo,Zilin Xiao,Chen Wei,Vicente Ordonez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftmaxGRPO，用温度缩放的softmax优势替代z-score归一化的组优势，解决GRPO在二元奖励下对简单提示分配学习信号不当的问题。该方法在DeepMath上达到51.8%，并将1.5B模型在Poetry任务上的性能从35.0%提升至68.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于组的强化学习目标（如GRPO）在二元奖励下，组归一化会导致对简单提示产生发散性加权，使得学习信号在不同难度提示间的分配不均衡，简单提示获得了过大的梯度权重。

Method: 提出Softmax Advantage Group Estimation（SoftmaxGRPO），作为GRPO的即插即用替代方案。用温度缩放的softmax优势替代z-score归一化的组优势，确保权重在有界范围内。推导了二元奖励下的精确有限组群体目标，识别MaxRL为其低温极限；对任意有界标量奖励，证明大组更新精确优化对数矩生成函数目标。

Result: SoftmaxGRPO将梯度预算从接近解决的提示重新分配出去，在相同奖励条件下一致优于GRPO。在DeepMath（可验证奖励）上达到51.8%，并通过轻量文本相似度奖励将1.5B指令微调模型在Poetry上的性能从35.0%提升至68.0%。

Conclusion: SoftmaxGRPO作为一种简单有效的替代方案，通过有界权重的softmax优势机制改善了组内学习信号分配，在多种奖励设置下均表现出性能提升，且理论分析表明其目标与GRPO相比具有更优的性质。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning objectives such as GRPO can allocate learning signal poorly across prompt difficulty: under binary rewards, group normalization induces a divergent weighting on easy prompts. We introduce Softmax Advantage Group Estimation (SoftmaxGRPO), a drop-in alternative that replaces z-score-normalized group advantages with temperature-scaled softmax advantages, keeping weights bounded regardless of prompt difficulty. For binary rewards, we derive the exact finite-group population objective and identify MaxRL as its low-temperature limit. For bounded scalar rewards, we show that the large-group update exactly optimizes a log-moment-generating-function objective, while a universal finite-group scalar objective cannot exist without additional assumptions on the reward distribution. Empirically, SoftmaxGRPO reallocates measured gradient budget away from near-solved prompts and consistently improves over GRPO under identical rewards. It reaches 51.8% on DeepMath with verifiable rewards and improves a 1.5B instruction-tuned model from 35.0% to 68.0% on Poetry using only lightweight text-similarity rewards.

</details>


### [131] [VeinCast: Physics-Guided Dynamic Field Graphs with Graph-Conditioned Fusion for Global Medium-Range Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09286)
*Zhisheng Chen,Jinhan Li,Yuxuan Li,Yuan Gao,Hao Wu,Zheng Lu,Jinlong Du,Kun Wang,Bo An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeinCast是一个物理引导的动态场图与图条件融合框架，用于全球中期天气预报，在1.5° ERA5基准上实现了69个气象场长达14天的领先预报性能，超越FuXi、Pangu-Weather、GraphCast等代表性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动天气预报模型大多隐式学习大气场之间的交互关系，而方程级物理约束可能引入近似和模型形式偏差。因此需要一种既能显式建模结构化、状态依赖的大气场交互，又避免过度依赖方程假设的方法。

Method: 提出VeinCast框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) Physics-Guided Dynamic Field Graph，在每个局部窗口内结合预定义大气关系与状态依赖的Top-K残差边，并利用图上下文自适应地球窗口注意力；(2) Graph-Conditioned Latent Fusion，利用图上下文和源节点中心性引导场到潜在空间的聚合，并通过有界反馈保留场特定信息。

Result: 在1.5° ERA5基准上，VeinCast在多达14天的预报时效内对全部69个气象场表现出有竞争力的预报性能，与FuXi、Pangu-Weather、GraphCast、FengWu和ARROW等代表性全球天气预报模型相比具有优势。消融实验证实两个模块提供互补增益。

Conclusion: 关系层面的物理引导对数据驱动天气预报有效，VeinCast通过动态场图显式建模大气场间的结构化交互，验证了物理先验知识与数据驱动方法结合的价值。

Abstract: Global medium-range weather forecasting requires modeling structured yet state-dependent interactions among heterogeneous atmospheric fields. Existing data-driven models largely learn these interactions implicitly, whereas equation-level physical constraints may inherit approximation and model-form biases. We present VeinCast, a physics-guided dynamic field graph and graph-conditioned fusion framework that jointly forecasts 69 surface and upper-air fields. Within each local window, its Physics-Guided Dynamic Field Graph combines predefined atmospheric relations with state-dependent Top-K residual edges and adapts Earth-window attention using the resulting graph context. Graph-Conditioned Latent Fusion further employs graph context and source-node centrality to guide field-to-latent aggregation, while bounded feedback preserves field-specific information. On the $1.5^\circ$ ERA5 benchmark, VeinCast demonstrates competitive forecasting performance across all 69 meteorological fields at lead times of up to 14 days, compared with representative global weather forecasting models including FuXi, Pangu-Weather, GraphCast, FengWu, and ARROW. Ablations confirm that the two modules provide complementary gains, demonstrating the effectiveness of relational-level physical guidance for data-driven weather forecasting.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond the Capability Boundary: Zeroth-Order Optimization for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09292)
*Bingzhen Liu,Xiaomeng Fan,Yuwei Wu,Zhi Gao,Mingyang Gao,Chuanhao Li,Yunde Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种零阶自进化框架（ZOForLLMAgents），通过对LLM的LoRA参数进行扰动来估计梯度并更新参数，使智能体能够学习超出其固有能力边界的困难样例，结合并行扰动推理和自适应查找机制降低时间开销，在多个深度研究基准上取得显著优于强基线方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自进化方法虽然能通过采样LLM的轨迹并从中学习来提升智能体能力，但存在一个关键局限：智能体无法在困难样例上采样到正确轨迹，因此难以学习超越其内在能力边界的内容。这限制了自进化方法在更具挑战性任务上的表现。

Method: 核心方法是零阶自进化框架：1）对LLM的LoRA参数进行扰动，在扰动和原始参数下分别运行智能体并计算损失，利用损失差值估计梯度并更新LoRA参数；2）使用更新后的LLM采样轨迹进行监督微调，形成闭环自进化循环；3）引入并行扰动推理机制和自适应查找机制来减少零阶优化的时间消耗；4）设计答案困惑度损失以提供平滑稳定的零阶损失值。

Result: 在多个深度研究基准上的实验表明，该方法能够获得显著更多的成功轨迹，并在整体性能上持续优于强基线方法，尤其在困难样例上的优势更为明显。

Conclusion: 通过零阶优化扰动参数的方式，智能体可以在无轨迹标注的情况下适应困难样例，从而突破其固有能力边界，实现更有效的自我进化。代码和资源已公开。

Abstract: Self-evolving methods improve the capabilities of LLM agents by sampling trajectories from the underlying LLMs and learning from these trajectories. However, these methods struggle to learn beyond the inherent capability boundary of the agents, since the agents cannot sample correct trajectories on difficult examples for further improvements. In this paper, we propose a zeroth-order self-evolution framework that enables agents to learn beyond their capability boundary by perturbing LLM parameters to adapt to difficult examples without any trajectory annotations. Specifically, we perturb LoRA parameters of LLMs, run the agent, compute the losses under the perturbed and original parameters, and use the loss difference to estimate gradients and further update the LoRA parameters. We sample trajectories using the updated LLMs for supervised fine-tuning to break through the capability boundary of the agents, forming a closed self-evolution loop. We introduce a parallel perturbation inference mechanism and an adaptive lookup mechanism to reduce time consumption in zeroth-order optimization, with an answer perplexity loss that provides smooth and stable zeroth-order loss values. Experiments on multiple deep research benchmarks show that our method obtains substantially more successful trajectories and consistently outperforms strong baselines, especially on difficult examples. The code and released artifacts are available at https://github.com/hidk1911/ZOForLLMAgents.

</details>


### [133] [Targeted Label-Flipping and Oversampling Attacks on Federated Conditional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09314)
*Panav Shah,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦生成对抗网络（GAN）中的标签翻转攻击及其过采样变体，通过理论分析和实验评估证明该攻击的有效性与隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦GAN框架中恶意客户端可能通过篡改标签信息攻击全局生成器，已有研究缺乏对该攻击的系统性理论分析和定量评估。

Method: 提出标签翻转攻击及其过采样变体（恶意客户端在本地训练时对中毒样本过加权），通过计算干净与中毒类条件分布之间的KL散度来量化分布偏移，并进行理论分析与FEMNIST、MNIST、CIFAR10上的实证评估。

Result: 语义损害随有效投毒强度线性增长，而与真实目标分布的偏差仅呈二次增长，使攻击既有效又难被与标签无关的指标检测。

Conclusion: 标签翻转攻击在联邦GAN中高效且隐蔽，需要设计更有效的防御机制来检测此类攻击。

Abstract: In a federated learning setup for GANs, several adversarial attacks are possible. One such attack is label flipping, in which malicious clients deliberately alter label information during local training in order to manipulate the global generator. The objective of this attack is to skew the learned generation distribution so that samples conditioned on a target label are instead mapped to a source class. In this work, we investigate the effectiveness of label flipping attacks in federated GANs through both theoretical analysis and empirical evaluation. We further consider an oversampling based variant, in which malicious clients upweight poisoned samples during local training to amplify their influence on the aggregated global model. We quantify the resulting distributional shift by computing the Kullback Leibler divergence between the clean and poisoned class conditional distributions, and show both analytically and on FEMNIST, MNIST, and CIFAR10 that the semantic damage of the attack grows linearly in the effective poisoning strength while deviation from the true target distribution grows only quadratically, making the attack effective yet difficult to detect from label agnostic metrics.

</details>


### [134] [MaxModShift: Model Privacy via Designed Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09328)
*Nomaan A. Kherani,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习中的窃听者模型学习问题，将其视为估计问题。通过信号设计将窃听者的Fisher信息矩阵驱动至奇异，使其无法学习模型。提出两种模型偏移方案，其中MaxModShift方案优于先前的ModShift设计，且相较于噪声注入方案性能更优、带宽需求更低、平均功耗更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习环境中，窃听者可能通过截获通信来学习全局模型，构成隐私威胁。现有方法未能有效平衡防窃听效果与传输功率、带宽等资源开销，亟需更优的信号设计方案。

Method: 将窃听者的模型学习形式化为估计问题，通过信号设计使窃听者对应的Fisher信息矩阵趋于奇异。设计模型偏移（model shift）以最大化窃听者与中心服务器之间学习模型的差异，同时满足智能体的传输功率约束，提出两种偏移方案并进行了性能对比。

Result: MaxModShift方案在性能上优于先前的ModShift设计，且所需传输功率更低；与噪声注入方案相比，MaxModShift在性能更优的同时，只需要更低的保密信道带宽和更少的平均功率消耗。

Conclusion: 所提出的MaxModShift方案在联邦学习防窃听方面具有显著优势，在模型保护效果、功耗和带宽开销等多个维度均优于现有方案，为联邦学习中的隐私防护提供了更有效的信号设计思路。

Abstract: Model learning by an eavesdropper is treated as an estimation problem in a federated environment. The Fisher Information Matrix for the eavesdropper's estimation problem is driven to singularity through a signaling design; this ensures that the eavesdropper cannot learn the model. Herein, the innovation of prior designs is that model shifts are designed to maximize the difference in the model learned by Eve and the central server while satisfying a transmission power constraint for the agents. Two shift schemes are provided. MaxModShift outperforms a prior ModShift design while requiring lesser transmission power. Compared to a noise injection scheme, MaxModShift performs better while requiring a lower bandwidth secret channel and a reduced average power consumption.

</details>


### [135] [Hallucinations and Constraints : Regulating surgical workflow recognition beyond accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09332)
*John S. H. Baxter,Pierre Jannin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了医学图像处理中AI幻觉问题，提出拓扑错误可作为可测量的幻觉形式，并将其表述为线性时序逻辑谓词，通过概率图模型显式约束。在机器人辅助子宫切除手术的自动手术阶段识别中，该方法将准确率提升约10%，并消除了绝大多数拓扑错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉是AI医学应用中的重大关切，但在医学图像处理领域研究较少。与自然语言理解不同，生物医学图像和信号预测是否正确并不直观，需要一种可测量和可监管的幻觉定义方式。

Method: 提出将拓扑错误视为幻觉形式，将生物医学信号分割中的某些拓扑性质重述为线性时序逻辑（LTL）谓词，并利用概率图模型显式地强制执行这些谓词约束。

Result: 在机器人辅助子宫切除手术的自动手术阶段识别任务中，显式约束谓词使准确率提升约10%，并消除了绝大多数拓扑错误。

Conclusion: 数学上的正确性保证可以补充机器学习在医学图像计算和计算机辅助干预中的其他经验性监管形式，为医学AI的幻觉控制和可靠性提供新途径。

Abstract: Hallucinations are a major concern for the integration of artificial intelligence into medicine, although less explored in the realm of medical image processing. Unlike problems in natural text understanding and reasoning therewith, determining whether or not predictions derived from biomedical images and signals is less intuitively clear. This article suggests that topological errors could constitute hallucinations in a way that can be more readily measured and thus regulated. Certain of these properties for certain types of problems, such as biomedical signal segmentation, can be rephrased as linear temporal logic predicates, a number of which can be explicitly enforced using probabilistic graphical models. Our simulations show the potential of these explicitly constrained predicates for the case of automatic surgical phase recognition in robot-assisted hysterectomy, improving accuracy by approximately 10% while removing the vast majority of topological errors, suggesting that mathematical guarantees of correctness can supplement other empirical forms of regulating machine learning in medical image computing and computer-assisted interventions.

</details>


### [136] [In-Context Density Estimation for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09348)
*Patryk Marszałek,Jacek Tabor,Marek Śmieja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ICED是一个基于Transformer的上下文能量密度估计器，通过合成先验预训练，单次前向传播即可从未标记数据集中估计log-density，无需每数据集训练或调参，统一驱动密度估计、OOD检测、异常检测和数据增强四项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据的密度估计支撑多项无监督任务，但这些任务通常需要各自训练独立模型并单独调参，存在较高的每数据集成本。作者希望构建一个通用的、零训练的密度估计器来统一这些任务。

Method: ICED是基于Transformer的模型，在专门为密度估计设计的合成先验上预训练，优化目标为在信息丰富处拟合log-density并保持其排序不变。推理时以数据集作为上下文，单次前向传播返回任意查询点的未归一化log-density，无需拟合、采样或超参数选择。

Result: 单个冻结的ICED模型在密度估计、OOD检测、无监督异常检测和生成式增强四项任务上与最强任务特定方法竞争，且是唯一无需重训练、无需调参、无需标签即可跨任务迁移的方法。

Conclusion: ICED证明了上下文学习可以泛化到密度估计领域，一个预训练模型即可替代多个专门化流水线，大幅降低表格数据无监督任务的计算和调参成本，代码已开源。

Abstract: Density estimation underlies many unsupervised tasks on tabular data such as anomaly detection, out-of-distribution detection, and data augmentation. Although all these problems reduce to questions about where probability mass lies, they are typically solved individually by fitting a separate model to each dataset, with its own hyperparameters and tuning budget. We introduce ICED, an in-context, energy-based density estimator that removes this per-dataset cost. ICED is a transformer-based model pretrained once on a synthetic prior built specifically for density estimation under an objective that fits log-density where it is informative and preserves its ordering elsewhere. In the inference, it reads a dataset as context and returns an unnormalized log-density for any query point in a single forward pass, with no fitting, sampling, or hyperparameter selection. A single frozen ICED model then drives four tasks usually handled by four specialized pipelines: density estimation, out-of-distribution detection, unsupervised anomaly detection, and generative augmentation. Across all four, it is competitive with the strongest task-specific method, while being the only approach that needs no retraining, no tuning, and no labels to move between them. The code is available at https://github.com/gmum/iced.

</details>


### [137] [Test-Time Augmentation for LLMs: When Input Diversity Beats Output Diversity at Matched Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09351)
*Nikita Kozodoi,Zainab Afolabi,Jack Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了测试时增强（TTA）方法，通过扰动输入而非仅采样推理路径来提升大语言模型（LLM）的推理准确性。在匹配计算量的对比下，语义改写策略在成本效益上比自一致性（self-consistency）方法高出约1.8倍准确率/美元，在六个任务中的五个上表现更优，尤其对中端模型最为划算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（Test-time scaling）虽然能提升LLM准确性，但推理成本随之成倍增加。自一致性方法仅从输出侧采样多次推理路径，但输入侧的多样性是否能更高效地将计算转化为准确率，这一问题尚未被系统性研究。

Method: 作者在匹配计算量的条件下进行系统对比：评估三种输入侧策略（语义改写、词汇扰动、视觉变换），跨越六个数据集（涵盖通用/多语言知识、数学推理、多模态问答和情感分类），与思维链提示及自一致性方法进行公平比较。

Result: 语义改写策略在准确率提升上一致且统计显著，在成本效益上帕累托支配自一致性方法，每美元获得的准确率提升约1.8倍，在六个任务中五个优于自一致性。TTA在中端模型中成本效益最高，尤其当更强模型不可用或太贵时。

Conclusion: 对于当前的中端LLM，变换输入比仅变换推理路径能更高效地将推理计算转化为准确性。作者提供了开源实现供社区使用。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves LLM accuracy but multiplies inference cost, making the accuracy gained per unit of compute the metric that matters in deployment. Self-consistency is one of the established approaches, which spends this budget entirely on the output side by sampling repeated reasoning paths. We study Test-Time Augmentation (TTA), which extends self-consistency by also perturbing the input, aggregating predictions across transformed versions of the input, and ask whether input-side diversity converts compute into accuracy more efficiently than output-side diversity. We perform a systematic, matched-compute comparison: we evaluate three simple input-side strategies (semantic rephrasing, lexical perturbations, and visual transformations) across six datasets covering general and multilingual knowledge, mathematical reasoning, multi-modal question answering, and sentiment classification, against chain-of-thought prompting and self-consistency. Semantic rephrasing delivers consistent and statistically significant accuracy gains while Pareto-dominating self-consistency on cost-effectiveness, delivering roughly 1.8X more accuracy per dollar and outperforming it on five of six tasks. We further analyze the number of augmentations, multi-modal strategies, and base model scaling, finding that TTA is most cost-effective for mid-tier models where a stronger model is unavailable or too expensive. Our findings indicate that for current mid-tier LLMs, varying the input converts inference compute into accuracy more efficiently than varying the reasoning path alone. The TTA implementation is available at https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-genai-reflection-for-bedrock.

</details>


### [138] [Beyond Binary: Continuous State Optimization with Graph-Structured Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09366)
*Corinna Cortes,Yishay Mansour,Mehryar Mohri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lazy Graph-LinUCB算法，将大规模学习系统中多目标优化的博弈框架从二元状态扩展到连续状态空间，通过依赖图（因子图）捕获目标间的局部结构，结合懒惰更新、异步调度、自适应图学习和联合估计器等机制，在最小化移动成本（系统不稳定惩罚）的同时保持接近最优的遗憾值，实验表明可将异构系统中的移动成本降低三倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
