The authority state the system trusts matters, and so does the path available after a block.

</details>


### [172] [Deep Multimodal Wearable Sensor Fusion for Detection of Body-Focused Repetitive Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09830)
*Samaneh Rezaeimanesh,Mohsen Behradfar,Mohammad Fili,Guiping Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于腕戴式传感器数据的多模态深度学习框架，用于自动检测和分类身体聚焦重复行为（如拔毛、抓皮），在二元检测中达到F1分数0.985，九类分类中达到宏观平均F1分数0.700，并通过可解释性分析揭示了时间飞行和惯性模态的关键贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身体聚焦重复行为（如拔毛、抓皮）常见于强迫症和焦虑障碍患者，但早期客观检测困难，因为这些动作细微且与普通非病理性手势重叠。现有方法缺乏准确、客观、连续的自动检测手段，亟需一种利用多模态传感器数据实现自动识别的解决方案。

Method: 利用Child Mind Institute的Helios腕戴设备采集多模态传感器数据（惯性测量单元、热电堆传感器和时间飞行传感器），构建融合卷积神经网络和门控循环单元的深度学习框架，并结合模态特异的自编码器和晚期融合分类器来捕捉时序和空间动态。

Result: 二元检测任务达到F1分数0.985和AUC 0.997；九类分类（将每种行为与单一分组非目标类别区分）达到宏观平均F1分数0.700和AUC 0.963，优于单模态基线。Shapley可解释性分析显示时间飞行和惯性模态贡献最大，层次聚类揭示误分类主要由手势的解剖区域驱动。

Conclusion: 多模态传感器融合能够实现准确、客观、连续的行为监测，为实时可穿戴辅助心理健康诊断和个性化干预奠定了基础，在生物医学研究和临床护理中具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Body-focused repetitive behaviors, such as hair pulling and skin picking, are compulsive motor actions commonly associated with obsessive-compulsive and anxiety disorders. Their early, objective detection remains difficult because the movements are subtle and overlap with ordinary, non-pathological gestures. We developed and evaluated a multimodal deep learning framework to detect and classify these behaviors from wrist-worn sensor data. The data, collected by the Child Mind Institute using the Helios wrist-worn device, combine inertial measurement units, thermopile sensors, and time-of-flight sensors, capturing kinematic, thermal, and proximity information. The framework combined a convolutional neural network with a gated recurrent unit, alongside modality-specific autoencoders and a late-fusion classifier, to exploit temporal and spatial dynamics. It achieved an F1 score of 0.985 and an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.997 for binary detection, distinguishing these behaviors from other activities, and a macro-averaged F1 score of 0.700 with an area under the curve of 0.963 across a nine-class scheme that distinguished each individual behavior from a single grouped Non-Target class, improving over single-modality baselines. Post-hoc interpretability based on Shapley additive explanations showed that the time-of-flight and inertial modalities dominated discriminative power by capturing spatial proximity and dynamic movement, while hierarchical clustering indicated that misclassifications were driven primarily by the anatomical region of the gesture. These findings demonstrate that multimodal sensor fusion enables accurate, objective, and continuous behavioral monitoring. This work establishes a foundation for real-time, wearable-assisted mental health diagnostics and personalized interventions in biomedical research and clinical care.

</details>


### [173] [Real-Time Climate Risk Assessment for Supply Chain Resilience: A Data-Driven Nowcasting Framework for Colombian Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09846)
*Hernan J. Silva-Sosa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据驱动即时预报（nowcasting）技术的气候风险评估方法框架，用于增强哥伦比亚农业供应链的韧性。该框架将短期气候预报与供应链风险建模相结合，构建了早期预警系统架构，并通过原型实现验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哥伦比亚气候变率大，降雨不规律、温度波动频繁且极端事件多发，对农业生产和物流（尤其是时效性强的作物）造成直接影响，亟需实时气候风险评估方法来提升供应链韧性。

Method: 该方法整合了基于历史气象观测的短期气候预报与供应链风险建模，构建概念性早期预警系统架构。通过显式风险映射、阈值分类和面向利益相关者的风险信号，将气候即时预报与供应链决策相衔接。原型实现采用官方统计与再分析产品中的历史气象和农业时间序列数据，不依赖卫星影像或计算机视觉组件。

Result: 合成数据和历史数据实验表明，短期降水即时预报可转化为农业供应链的可行动风险指标，支持库存、采购和运输等方面的前瞻性决策，验证了框架的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将气候即时预报与供应链决策整合的可行性，为哥伦比亚农业供应链提供了实用的早期预警与风险信号生成方法，支持前瞻性风险管理决策。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodological framework for real-time climate risk assessment using data-driven nowcasting techniques to enhance supply chain resilience in Colombian agricultural contexts. Climate variability in Colombia, characterized by irregular rainfall, temperature fluctuations, and recurrent extreme events, has a direct impact on agricultural production and logistics, particularly for time sensitive crops. The proposed approach integrates short term climate forecasting based on historical meteorological observations with supply chain risk modeling to establish a conceptual early warning system architecture. A prototype implementation developed in a controlled computational environment demonstrates the feasibility of the framework using historical meteorological and agricultural time series derived from official statistics and reanalysis products, without reliance on satellite imagery or computer vision components. The methodology addresses the integration of climate nowcasting with supply chain decision making through explicit risk mapping, threshold-based categorization, and stakeholder-oriented risk signals. Results from synthetic and historical data experiments indicate that short term precipitation nowcasts can be translated into actionable risk indicators for agricultural supply chains, supporting anticipatory decisions related to inventory, sourcing, and transport.

</details>


### [174] [Fairness in Link Prediction Beyond Demographic Parity: A Reproducibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09899)
*Valentijn Oldenburg,Floris de Kam,Stef de Wildt,Jarno Nilson Balk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究复现并验证了Mattos等人(2025)关于公平排名链接预测中ΔDP指标无法检测曝光偏差的论断，同时验证了MORAL后处理方法在改善曝光公平性的有效性，并通过合成同质性场景、分类敏感属性及多种公平性/效用指标进行了扩展的鲁棒性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在公平排名链接预测中，常用的公平性指标ΔDP由于忽略链接在排名中的位置而无法检测曝光偏差。作者旨在复现这一论断并评估更精细的排名感知指标的鲁棒性。

Method: 通过实证证明ΔDP在存在系统性排名歧视时仍可能显示整体公平性；采用排名感知的NDKL指标检测此类偏见；复现MORAL后处理方法；并使用合成同质性设置、分类敏感属性、AWRF等多个额外指标进行鲁棒性评估。

Result: 结果证实曝光类指标能够揭示ΔDP隐藏的偏见，MORAL方法在多种设置和数据集上均能以极小效用损失减少这些偏见，表明该后处理方法具有良好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究确认了单纯依赖ΔDP评估公平排名链接预测的局限性，曝光感知的公平性指标和后处理方法是必要的。同时提供了一份修正后的可复现实现代码。

Abstract: In fair ranked link prediction, demographic parity ($Δ_\mathrm{DP}$) is a common fairness metric. Yet, Mattos et al. (2025) argue that it fails to detect exposure bias because it ignores where links appear in the ranking. In this study, we reproduce this claim by showing that $Δ_\mathrm{DP}$ can indicate aggregate parity even when some subgroup-pair links are systematically ranked lower than others. The proposed rank-aware Normalized Discounted KL-divergence (NDKL), however, does detect such disparities. We also reproduce the effectiveness of MORAL, a post-processing method that improves exposure-based fairness while maintaining competitive utility. Beyond reproduction, we assess robustness using synthetic homophily settings, categorical sensitive attributes, and additional fairness and utility metrics, including subgroup-pair-adapted Attention-Weighted Rank Fairness (AWRF). Overall, our results show that exposure-based metrics uncover biases hidden by $Δ_\mathrm{DP}$ and that MORAL reduces these biases with minimal utility loss across diverse settings and datasets. We release a corrected, reproducible implementation at https://github.com/Floris93100/reproducing-MORAL.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [DevIntent: How Much Does LLM-Generated Code Violate Developer Intent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07614)
*Susana Haing,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出意图违反率（IVR）指标和基于HumanEval+的49个问题基准，用于衡量LLM生成的代码在通过可见测试的同时违反开发者隐含意图的比例。实验发现Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT 4.1虽然各自通过92%以上的可见测试，但在超过一半的问题上违反意图（54.5%和63.5%），表明传统通过率高估了代码对开发者意图的反映程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅衡量代码是否通过给定的测试，而LLM在模糊提示下生成的代码可能违反开发者的隐含意图。作者希望通过新指标揭示通过率所掩盖的意图偏差问题。

Method: 从HumanEval+衍生出49个问题的试点基准，每个问题去除澄清后的隐含约束并编码为隐藏约束测试。引入IVR指标，衡量通过可见测试但未通过隐藏意图约束测试的代码比例，并评估Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT 4.1两个模型。

Result: 两个模型通过率均超过92%，但IVR分别高达54.5%和63.5%，且违反意图的模式呈系统性双峰分布，在两个模型间保持一致。

Conclusion: 传统代码通过率显著高估了LLM生成代码与开发者意图的一致性，IVR提供了更全面的评测维度，提示需要将隐含意图约束纳入代码生成评估。

Abstract: Code generated by LLMs can violate a developer's implicit intentions when given an ambiguous prompt, yet standard benchmarks measure only whether code passes its stated test. We introduce the Intent Violation Rate (IVR) and a 49-problem pilot benchmark derived from HumanEval+. Each problem strips implicit constraints from a clarified prompt and encodes them as hidden constraint tests. IVR measures the fraction of LLM-generated solutions that pass the stated (visible) tests yet fail hidden constraint tests that capture unstated intent. Evaluating Claude Sonnet 4.6 and OpenAI GPT 4.1, we find both pass over 92\% of stated tests yet violate intent in over half of problems (54.5\% and 63.5\%), following a systematic, bimodal pattern consistent across both models. Out findings indicate that pass rates overstate how well generated code reflects developer intent.

</details>


### [176] [Refining LLM-based Directed Test Input Generation via Runtime Value Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07926)
*Narin Han,Shin Hong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReDig是一个运行时反馈引导的细化框架，在基于LLM的定向测试输入生成技术周围添加控制循环，利用先前未达目标测试执行中观测到的运行时值来细化输入生成，提升目标代码行覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的定向输入生成技术虽有效，但因可用代码信息受限和LLM推理的固有不可预测性，可靠生成需要将过程锚定在观测到的运行时行为上，当前缺乏这种反馈机制的保障。

Method: ReDig在LLM驱动的定向测试输入生成技术外围增加一个控制循环，从先前未命中目标代码行的测试执行中提取运行时值反馈，利用这些反馈来诊断失败原因并指导后续测试脚本的细化生成。

Result: 在Poppler和Libsndfile的案例研究中，ReDig能够有效提取运行时值反馈以诊断测试脚本未能到达目标行的原因，并能有效利用这些反馈在后续步骤中细化测试脚本，提升定向到达目标代码的能力。

Conclusion: 运行时反馈引导的细化机制可以显著增强LLM在定向测试输入生成中的可靠性和有效性，为LLM测试生成技术与程序运行时行为之间建立闭环反馈。

Abstract: LLM-based directed input generation techniques have shown promising effectiveness at producing target-reaching test inputs. However, due to the constraint of available code information and inherent unpredictability of LLM inference, reliable directed input generation requires mechanisms to ground the process in observed runtime behavior. We propose ReDig, a runtime feedback-guided refinement framework which adds a control loop around an LLM-based directed test input generation technique to refine the directed input generation with runtime values observed in prior target-missing test executions. In the case studies with Poppler and Libsndfile, we found that ReDig effectively derive runtime value feedback to diagnose why the previous test script failed to reach the target lines, and also effectively leverage given runtime value feedback to refine the test scripts in subsequent steps.

</details>


### [177] [Verication-driven closed-loop multi-agent large language modelframework for code-compliant structural design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07978)
*Jianbin Luo,Weibin Lin,Yiran Lin,Qing Wei,Wei Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将外部物理验证器反馈注入闭环修复循环的多智能体LLM结构设计框架，通过双节点循环（硬约束+软约束）将代码合规率从56.8%提升至98.6%，综合评分从63.8提升至71.4，并减少约5.8%的材料用量，且性能归因于外部验证器而非LLM本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体大语言模型系统虽已应用于结构设计，但大多数采用一次性生成方式，无法验证其输出，不适合安全关键任务。作者不信任LLM自我纠错能力，因此引入外部基于物理的验证器来提供可靠反馈。

Method: 框架将三层有限元验证系统与双节点闭环循环耦合：节点1将代码违规转化为硬性修复约束，节点2将四维质量评分转化为安全优先的软约束，并通过检索增强的规范库使每条违规均可追溯到具体条款。

Result: 在五种结构类型和44个案例上，代码合规率从56.8%提升至98.6%，综合评分从63.8提升至71.4（p<0.000001），材料用量减少约5.8%。移除任一节点性能均下降，且两个骨干LLM的合规性无显著差异。

Conclusion: 性能提升主要归因于外部验证器而非LLM模型本身，表明在安全关键的结构设计任务中，外部物理验证反馈比模型自纠错更可靠。框架、44案例基准及实验脚本均已开源以供复现。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model(LLM)systems are applied to structural design,yet most use one-shot generation and cannot verify their output,leaving themill-suited to safety-critical tasks.Rather than trusting LLM self-correction,thisframework injects feedback from an external physics-based verier into a closedrepair loop.The framework couples a three-layernite-element verication systemwith a dual-node loop.Node 1 turns code violations into hard repair constraints,Node 2 turns a four-dimensional quality score into safety-rst soft constraints,and a retrieval-augmented code base makes every violation traceable to a clause.Overve structure types and 44 cases,code compliance rises from 56.8%to 98.6%and the composite score from 63.8 to 71.4(p<0.000001),using about 5.8%lessmaterial.Removing either node degrades performance,and compliance does notchange detectably across the two backbone LLMs tested,indicating that it ishere attributed to the external verier rather than the model.The framework,the 44-case benchmark and all experiment scripts are released as open source forreplicability.

</details>


### [178] [Stateful Multi-Agent LLMs for Cross-View Interface Alignment in Automotive Model-Based Systems Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08038)
*Aleksei Velsh,Nenad Petrovic,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有状态的多智能体验证流水线，用于解决LLM在MBSE中对软件定义车辆生成架构时产生的"架构漂移"问题。该方法通过顺序生成矩阵和基于VSS的RAG，结合独立AI验证代理的审计与回溯循环，在ADAS场景中实现了97%的实体可追溯性、87%的信号守恒和85%的F1分数，相较标准RAG的0%实体可追溯性有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能加速软件定义车辆的MBSE建模，但其概率性本质会导致"架构漂移"，即在行为视图中生成缺乏结构基础的虚构接口，需要一种确定性接口对齐机制来确保架构一致性。

Method: 提出有状态的多智能体验证流水线：采用顺序生成矩阵（类图→活动图→序列图），结合基于VSS的RAG进行知识检索增强；引入独立的AI验证代理，依据严格错误分类法动态审计输出，并触发保持状态的回溯循环以解决不兼容性问题。

Result: 在ADAS场景中，标准RAG的实体可追溯性为0%；而多智能体工作流消除了跨阶段幻觉，实现了97%的实体可追溯性、87%的信号守恒和85%的F1分数。

Conclusion: 对抗性审计机制能够使LLM可靠地综合出零错误的MBSE架构，证明多智能体验证体系是确保LLM生成架构确定性的有效方案。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) can accelerate Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) for software-defined vehicles, their probabilistic nature causes "architectural drift", fabricating interfaces in behavioral views that lack structural foundations. To enforce deterministic interface alignment, we propose a stateful, multi-agent validation pipeline. The framework utilizes a sequential generation matrix (Class->Activity->Sequence) and Vehicle Signal Specification (VSS)-grounded Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). An independent AI Validator Agent dynamically audits outputs against a strict error taxonomy, triggering state-preserving backtracking loops to resolve incompatibilities. Evaluated on an Advanced Driver Assistance System (ADAS) scenario, standard RAG yielded 0% Entity Traceability. Conversely, our multi-agent workflow eradicated cross-phase hallucinations, achieving 97% Entity Traceability, 87% Signal Conservation, and an 85% F1-score. This proves adversarial auditing enables LLMs to reliably synthesize zero-error MBSE architectures.

</details>


### [179] [HugSelect: An Explainable Multi-Criteria Decision-Support Framework for foundation-model selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08069)
*Alireza Joonbakhsh,Arda Canser Adalı,Slinger Jansen,Farshad Khunjush,Siamak Farshidi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出HugSelect，一个用于基础模型（Foundation Model）选择的可解释决策支持框架。它通过构建包含71,274个模型的知识库，结合仓库元数据、功能能力和社区感知质量，使用加权加性模型对候选模型进行排序，并通过多种评估证明了其推荐质量与商业系统相当，同时提供可追溯和可检查的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型越来越多地被复用作软件组件，模型选择成为关键的软件工程决策。然而，当前模型中心主要通过流行度指标进行发现，忽视了功能能力、操作约束和社区感知质量。作者主张应将基础模型选择视为明确的、可审计的软件组件选择任务，而非关键词搜索或流行度排名。

Method: HugSelect框架包含三个核心部分：(1) 构建包含71,274个模型的知识库，通过统一管道整合仓库元数据、提取的功能能力和社区讨论中衍生的质量属性；(2) 使用加权加性模型对候选模型进行排名；(3) 提供标准级别得分分解，实现可解释的决策支持。评估包括管道验证、与四个商业LLM推荐系统的对比案例研究（44个场景）、细粒度消融实验和探索性用户研究（n=10）。

Result: 提取管道在功能特征上达到0.801的F1分数，质量属性映射准确率达0.84。HugSelect在模型级别的Coverage@10达到0.61，家族级别Coverage@10达到0.91，推荐质量与商业系统相当且排名质量无显著差异，同时提供稳定、可追溯、可检查的推理。消融实验证实功能特征是检索准确性的主要驱动力，用户初步反馈表明框架有用且直观。

Conclusion: HugSelect证明了将基础模型选择作为显式软件组件选择任务来处理是可行且有效的。它提供了与商业系统相当的推荐质量，同时保证了推理的透明性和可审计性。功能特征在模型检索中起主导作用，这为未来基础模型选择工具的设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly reused as software components, making model selection a critical software-engineering decision. Current model hubs primarily support discovery through popularity metrics, often neglecting functional capabilities, operational constraints, and community-perceived quality. We argue that foundation-model selection should be treated as an explicit, auditable software-component selection task rather than as keyword search, popularity ranking, or opaque conversational advice.
  This paper proposes HugSelect, an explainable decision-support framework for foundation-model selection. HugSelect builds a knowledge base of 71,274 models by combining repository metadata, extracted functional capabilities, and perceived quality attributes derived from community discussions into a unified pipeline. It ranks candidate models using a weighted additive model that exposes criterion-level score decompositions.
  We evaluated HugSelect through pipeline validation, comparative case studies against four commercial LLM-based recommendation systems (44 scenarios), fine-grained ablation, and an exploratory user study (n = 10). Extraction pipelines achieved an F1 score of 0.801 for functional features and an accuracy of 0.84 for quality-attribute mapping. HugSelect achieved a model-level Coverage@10 of 0.61 and family-level Coverage@10 of 0.91, showing recommendation quality comparable to that of the evaluated commercial systems, with no significant overall differences in ranking quality, while providing stable, traceable, and inspectable reasoning. Ablation confirmed that functional features were the main driver of retrieval accuracy, and preliminary user feedback suggests that the framework is useful and intuitive.

</details>


### [180] [Synthesizing Behavioural Models of CPS Using Automata Learning and Statistical Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08214)
*Negin Ayoughi,Baharin A. Jodat,Armina Faghihi,Patricio Saavedra,Shiva Nejati,Mehrdad Sabetzadeh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MELA，一种结合统计机器学习与自动机学习的被动式行为模型学习方法，用于从CPS的数值时间序列数据中自动合成行为模型。MELA通过信息论变量选择和基于决策树的范围抽象，将数值信号转换为与系统状态强相关的可解释符号区间，进而进行自动机学习。在两个CPS（商业入侵检测系统和工业自动驾驶仪基准）上的评估表明，相比基于专家经验的数值抽象方法，MELA平均减少49.20%的状态和转移数量，同时平均提高41.71%的准确率，且学到的状态机支持系统级需求验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS的输入输出通常是数值时间序列数据，而自动机学习算法要求有限符号字母表上的输入，因此需要对原始数值数据进行符号抽象。现存的抽象方法往往依赖专家经验，缺乏系统性，需要一种自动化的机器学习增强方案来解决数值数据到符号数据的抽象问题。

Method: MELA采用两阶段方法：首先使用信息论变量选择来识别与系统状态强相关的关键变量，然后使用基于决策树的范围抽象将数值信号划分为可解释的符号区间，从而将数值时间序列转换为适合自动机学习的符号表示，最终通过被动自动机学习合成行为模型。

Result: 在两个CPS（商业网络入侵检测系统和航空航天领域的工业自动驾驶仪基准）上评估，相比基于专家经验的数值数据抽象，MELA平均减少49.20%的状态和转移数量，平均提高41.71%的准确率。学到的状态机支持系统级需求验证，并帮助从业者探索系统需求中未明确的行为。

Conclusion: MELA验证了将统计机器学习与自动机学习系统化结合的有效性，能够从CPS数值时间序列数据中自动生成紧凑、准确且可解释的行为模型，为复杂异构CPS的正式验证和分析提供有力支撑。

Abstract: Inferring behavioural models from system executions is essential for supporting formal verification and analysis of complex, heterogeneous cyber-physical systems (CPS). Automata learning provides an effective way to infer state machine models from system executions. However, CPS inputs and outputs often consist of numeric time-series data, while automata learning algorithms assume inputs over a finite symbolic alphabet. As a result, raw numeric data must first be abstracted into a finite set of symbols. In this article, we present MELA, a passive automata learning approach enhanced with machine learning to synthesize behavioural models from numeric time-series data generated by CPS. MELA systematically combines statistical machine learning with automata learning to automatically abstract raw numeric signals into interpretable intervals that are strongly correlated with system states. Specifically, MELA uses information-theoretic variable selection and decision-tree-based range abstraction to transform numeric traces into symbolic representations suitable for automata learning. We evaluate MELA on two CPS: a commercial network intrusion detection system developed by our industry partner, RabbitRun Technologies, and a publicly available industrial autopilot benchmark from the aerospace domain. Compared with expertise-based numeric data abstraction, MELA reduces the number of states and transitions in the learned state machines by 49.20% on average, while improving accuracy by 41.71% on average. Furthermore, the learned state machines support system-level requirement verification and help practitioners explore behaviours that are not explicit in the system requirements. We make our implementation and experimental data available online.
  Keywords: Automata learning, Cyber-physical systems, Behavioural model synthesis, Decision trees, Model checking, Intrusion detection, Simulink.

</details>


### [181] [On the Robustness of LLMs' Internal Representation of Code Correctness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08266)
*Francisco Ribeiro,Sohaila Abdulsattar,Renata Gonzalez,Mahmoud Kassem,Sarah Nadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了从语言模型内部隐藏状态中提取代码正确性信号的稳健性问题，通过变化信号提取方式和构造仅含单一故障的程序对，发现不存在单一最优配置，且隔离故障并不能提升信号质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型生成的代码虽然自然流畅，但常常未能实现用户要求的功能，且模型的置信度信号与实际正确性校准不佳。先前工作通过对比正确和不正确程序的隐藏状态捕捉到了一种能评估代码正确性的内部信号，但这种信号仅在一种特定提取方式下获得，其是否反映模型的稳健属性还是提取方式的偶然产物尚不清楚。

Method: 论文系统性地变化从模型内部提取该信号的方式（多种配置），同时通过构造仅在故障类型上有所不同的程序对来考察数据质量对信号品质的制约，从而检验内部正确性信号的稳健性。

Result: 实验结果表明，没有任何单一配置在所有情况下最优，说明该内部信号的表现依赖于提取方式的选择；同时，隔离故障（使程序对仅在故障上不同）并不会改善信号的质量。

Conclusion: 模型内部代码正确性信号对提取配置敏感，并非一种完全稳健的模型属性；数据中隔离故障对信号质量无益，揭示了该类信号在应用中的局限性和需进一步探索的方向。

Abstract: Code generated by modern language models often reads naturally. Yet, it also often fails to implement what was asked. This should be no surprise, as research shows the models' own confidence signals are poorly calibrated with actual correctness. A promising way to assess correctness looks inside the model: by contrasting the hidden states of correct and incorrect programs, recent work captured an internal signal of code correctness that is able to judge candidate solutions better than the model's token-level or stated confidence, with no test execution. However, this signal was captured under one particular way, leaving open an important question: whether it reflects a robust property of the model or an artifact of that choice. We study this question systematically, varying how the signal is extracted from the model internals. Besides this, we also ask if the signal's quality is limited by the data used to extract it, by constructing program pairs that differ only in the fault that makes them incorrect. Our results show that no single configuration is best, and that isolating the fault does not help.

</details>


### [182] [Ouroboros: A Self-Developing Frontier Coding Agent with Reviewed Core Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08311)
*Anton Razzhigaev,Andrei Gritsaev,Andrei Kaznacheev,Nikita Dragunov,Roman Yampolskiy,Andrei Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Ouroboros，一个自我进化的智能体框架，其工具、提示词、上下文组装和核心实现在审阅式提交中不断改进，后续工作直接采用这些演进后的运行时。在Terminal-Bench 2.1、OSWorld-Verified和CL-Bench等基准测试上均取得SOTA成绩，并通过161天的Hope长期部署实验验证了进化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能体框架通常是静态的，工具、提示词和上下文组装方式在部署后很难自我改进。作者希望构建一个能够通过自身工作不断进化完善的自开发式智能体，使其在真实任务和社会交互中暴露问题并推动结构性改进。

Method: 核心进化分两种模式：(1)递归自由进化——改进本身作为任务，完成一个进化周期可调度下一个周期；(2)经验驱动核心进化——日常工作和社交互动暴露的bug、粗糙边缘和低效上下文构造推动审阅式结构变更。系统采用冻结快照用于基准测试，Hope则为独立谱系上的持续活体进化部署。

Result: Terminal-Bench 2.1上Opus 5运行得分86.74%（该基准最佳报告结果）；OSWorld-Verified上达90.69%，超越此前最佳分数；CL-Bench五次rollout活动获得归一化奖励0.2301，刷新SOTA。Hope是161天的长期活体进化实验，覆盖七个交互表面。

Conclusion: 自我进化的智能体在各项基准上均达到领先水平，证明了此范式的有效性。由于智能体可重写自身代码和选择新模型API，操作安全性成为首要设计问题，护栏必须在进化和公开社会压力下保持权威性；同时，基准测试使用冻结快照与活体进化分离并行的策略是可行且必要的。

Abstract: We present Ouroboros, a self-developing agent harness whose tools, prompts, context assembly, and core implementation improve through reviewed commits that become the runtime for later work. Core evolution proceeds in two modes. In recursive free evolution, improvement is itself a task, and completing one evolution cycle can schedule the next. In experience-driven core evolution, ordinary work and social interaction expose bugs, rough edges, and inefficient context construction that lead to reviewed structural changes.
  On Terminal-Bench 2.1, an Opus 5 run scores 86.74%, the best result reported on the benchmark. On OSWorld-Verified, an Opus 5 run reaches 90.69%, exceeding the best previously reported score. A five-rollout CL-Bench campaign achieves a normalized reward of 0.2301, setting a new state of the art.
  Hope is the longest-running publicly documented Ouroboros deployment. It is a 161-day living agent experiment in free evolution under governed human communication across seven surfaces. Human interaction surfaces faults and generates proposals, but the agent decides which changes to pursue. Because a self-developing agent may rewrite its own code and select new model APIs, operational safety becomes a primary design problem: guardrails must remain authoritative under evolutionary and public social pressure. Benchmark campaigns use frozen system snapshots, while Hope continues live evolution on a separate lineage.

</details>


### [183] [Do Stack Overflow Answer Edits Occur Beyond Java? A Replication on Python and JavaScript](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08361)
*Chaiyong Ragkhitwetsagul,In-on Wiratsin,Matheus Paixao,Denis De Sousa,Pongpop Lapvikai,Peter Haddawy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究在Python和JavaScript上复现了对Stack Overflow答案编辑行为及其对开源项目代码改进潜力的原始研究（原研究基于Java），发现两种语言的答案编辑率（41.25%和39.10%）约为Java（16.11%）的2.5倍，且编辑后答案的推荐改进在热门GitHub项目中的分布趋势与原研究一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始研究仅基于Java发现Stack Overflow答案编辑可为开源项目代码提供改进建议（16.11%的接受答案被编辑），但无法确定该行为是Stack Overflow的普遍属性还是Java社区特有的现象，因此需要进行跨语言复现研究来验证其泛化性。

Method: 采用与原研究相同的实验流程：使用SOTorrent数据提取管道处理840,132个Python接受答案和1,144,185个JavaScript接受答案，使用克隆搜索工具Siamese+，并在每种语言下选取100个GitHub项目按低、中、高流行度分类进行匹配分析。

Result: Python和JavaScript的答案编辑率分别为41.25%和39.10%（约为Java的2.5倍），每次编辑的平均修订次数跨语言几乎不变（2.78、2.68、2.82）。匹配答案编辑数从低到高流行度项目单调递增（Python: 80→156→977，JavaScript: 32→71→353），Python的差异具有统计显著性而JavaScript不显著。

Conclusion: 原始研究的核心发现可泛化到Java之外的语言，Python和JavaScript中候选改进的供给量远大于Java，表明该行为是Stack Overflow的普遍属性而非单一语言社区的特性。

Abstract: Stack Overflow answers are continually revised by the community, and the edits made to their code snippets are a potential source of improvements for code that has been reused in open-source projects. A recent empirical study established this for Java, reporting that 16.11% of accepted Java answers are edited and that the resulting recommendations concentrate in highly popular GitHub projects. Whether that behaviour is a property of Stack Overflow or a property of the Java community has remained an open question. We replicate the study on Python and JavaScript, the two most widely used languages alongside Java, applying the same SOTorrent-based extraction pipeline, the same clone search tool, Siamese+, and the same project popularity criteria. Analysing 840,132 accepted Python answers and 1,144,185 accepted JavaScript answers, we find that 41.25% and 39.10% respectively have been edited at least once, roughly two and a half times the Java rate, while the number of revisions per edited answer is almost invariant across the three languages at 2.78, 2.68 and 2.82. Searching 100 GitHub projects per language, we find that the number of matched answer edits increases monotonically from low- to medium- to high-popularity projects in both languages, from 80 to 156 to 977 for Python and from 32 to 71 to 353 for JavaScript. The difference is statistically significant for Python but not for JavaScript. The central findings of the original study therefore generalise beyond Java, with the supply of candidate improvements considerably larger in both replication languages than in the original.

</details>


### [184] [Tangent: An Empirical Study of Testing Practices for LLM-Based Agent Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08413)
*Rangeet Pan,Tyler Stennett,Divya Sankar,Bridget McGinn,Alessandro Orso,Raju Pavuluri,Saurabh Sinha,Maja Vukovic*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对大量开源项目中240个模块的2,572个测试方法进行人工标注，并结合10位资深行业从业者的访谈，首次对LLM智能体应用的测试实践进行了实证研究，提出了包含23种测试模式的分类体系，发现现有测试以窄范围的单元测试为主，缺乏对复杂交互、真实场景和非功能需求的覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的智能体应用在基准测试方面取得了快速进展，但很少有研究探讨这类系统在实际中是如何被测试的。测试层级、测试目标、数据模式、测试复杂度和验证策略等关键方面仍然缺乏探索。

Method: 研究者构建了一个大型开源项目语料库，从中挖掘智能体应用、工具和测试数据；对240个模块中的2,572个测试方法进行人工标注，推导出涵盖测试夹具、数据、目标和断言的23种测试模式分类体系；按测试层级（单元、模块、集成）进行表征，并辅以10位资深行业从业者的结构化访谈。

Result: 研究发现LLM智能体应用的测试以范围狭窄的单元测试为主导，对复杂交互、真实场景和非功能需求的覆盖有限。测试频繁依赖简单输入、重度mock和浅层验证，智能体相关测试的结构复杂度较低。行业实践比开源项目更重视非功能测试，但两者都暴露了共同缺口：缺乏正式的测试基础、测试目标不明确，以及高质量测试数据生成困难。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，本文提出了若干研究方向，包括智能体可测试性的基础理论、形式化的测试目标定义，以及基于故障的测试技术，旨在推动智能体应用测试走向更系统化、更严谨的方向。

Abstract: Agents built on large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to build applications that perform complex, multi-step tasks involving reasoning, tool use, and interaction with external environments. Despite rapid progress in benchmarking LLM-based agents, very few studies have attempted to understand how such systems are tested in practice. In particular, testing levels, objectives, data patterns, test complexity, and validation strategies for agent applications remain underexplored. In this paper, we present an empirical study of testing practices in LLM-based agent applications using a large corpus of mined open-source projects. We construct a large-scale dataset of agent applications, tools, and tests, and manually label 2,572 test methods from 240 modules. From this analysis, we derive a taxonomy of 23 testing patterns across test fixtures, data, objectives, and assertions, and characterize tests by level (unit, module, integration). We complement this with structured interviews of 10 senior industry practitioners building agentic systems. Our results show that testing of LLM-based agent applications is dominated by narrowly scoped unit tests, with limited coverage of complex interactions, realistic scenarios, and non-functional requirements. Tests frequently rely on simplistic inputs, heavy mocking, and shallow validation, and agent-related tests exhibit low structural complexity. While industry practice places greater emphasis on non-functional testing than open-source projects, both reveal common gaps, including the lack of formal testing foundations, unclear test objectives, and challenges in generating high-quality test data. Based on these findings, we outline research directions toward more systematic and rigorous testing of agent applications, including foundations for agent testability, formalized test objectives, and fault-based testing techniques.

</details>


### [185] [Generics-Aware Fuzz Target Generation for Rust Libraries via Structured API Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08637)
*Yiming Chen,Kaiwen Zhang,Guanjun Liu,Xiaofeng Li,Xiaogang Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GRAFT是一种面向Rust库API的模糊测试工具，通过从文档提取结构化API信息、构建递归泛型感知的API依赖图，并结合拓扑遍历与LLM编译器错误反馈，实现80.75%的API覆盖率和96.19%的编译成功率，显著优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust库API的模糊测试需要构造类型正确、可编译的调用序列，同时满足所有权规则、泛型参数和trait约束，而现有工具忽略这些约束或使用浅层启发式方法，导致覆盖率低。

Method: GRAFT包含三个核心步骤：从Rust文档中提取结构化API信息；通过递归泛型感知类型匹配构建API依赖图；采用拓扑引导遍历结合LLM生成和编译器错误反馈，生成可编译的模糊测试目标。

Result: 在13个来自crates.io的crate上，GRAFT达到80.75%的宏平均API覆盖率和96.19%的编译成功率，相比RULF和RPG分别提升4.76倍和2.43倍，在含有unsafe API的crate上达到deepSURF平均API覆盖率的1.41倍。

Conclusion: GRAFT通过结合结构化的API依赖图构建与LLM驱动的代码生成，有效解决了Rust模糊测试中类型复杂性和可编译性问题，为Rust生态的API模糊测试提供了更高效、更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Fuzzing Rust library APIs requires constructing well-typed, compilable call sequences that satisfy ownership rules, generic parameters, and trait bounds; existing tools ignore these constraints or use shallow heuristics, yielding low coverage. We present GRAFT, which extracts structured API information from Rust documentation, builds an API dependency graph via recursive generics-aware type matching, and uses topology-guided traversal plus LLM synthesis with compiler-error feedback to produce compilable fuzz targets. On 13 crates from crates.io, GRAFT achieves 80.75% macro-average API coverage at 96.19% compilation success, outperforming RULF and RPG by 4.76x and 2.43x, and reaching 1.41x the average API coverage of deepSURF on crates with unsafe-reaching APIs.

</details>


### [186] [Fusing UI Structure & Semantics for Feature-Oriented App Screen Retrieval & Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08880)
*Arun Krishna Vajjala,Yanfu Yan,Ajay Krishna Vajjala,Shrunal Pothagoni,Denys Poshyvanyk,Kevin Moran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出FRAME，一种多模态神经符号结合的UI屏幕嵌入技术，通过构建图结构表示UI组件关系，结合视觉语言模型和几何算法进行加权嵌入传播，在搜索和聚类任务上分别提升最高13% MRR和7.6个百分点的聚类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UI编程面临代码与图形界面之间的抽象鸿沟，现有工具依赖屏幕检索和聚类，但基于特征的相似度计算困难，因为功能相似的界面常常存在设计差异。

Method: FRAME采用多模态神经符号嵌入技术：1）构建基于图的符号表示来编码UI组件的关键关系；2）利用大型视觉语言模型进行视觉和词汇编码；3）提出新型UI专用计算几何算法实现加权嵌入传播。

Result: 在三个基准测试中，FRAME在搜索任务上比强基线最高提升13% MRR，在聚类准确率上提升7.6个百分点；消融研究验证了各组件均有效。

Conclusion: FRAME通过结合符号图表示与深度学习方法，有效克服了UI界面设计差异带来的相似度计算难题，展现出增强自动化UI设计和测试工具的潜力。

Abstract: User Interface (UI) programming is challenging due to the complex abstraction gap between code and graphical software representations. To bridge this gap, UI programming tools often rely on screen retrieval and clustering, which require accurate similarity measures based on overlapping features. However, computing feature-oriented similarity is difficult because screens with similar functionality often exhibit design variations.
  To address this, we propose FRAME (ReinForced UseR InterfAce Screen EMbedding with Graphical Structural ComprEhension), a multi-modal, neuro-symbolic embedding technique. FRAME constructs symbolic, graph-based representations of UI components to encode salient relationships and capture feature patterns across different screens. It leverages large vision-language models for visual and lexical encoding, alongside a novel UI-specific computational geometry algorithm that enables weighted embedding propagation. Across three benchmarks, FRAME outperforms strong baselines by up to 13% MRR in search and 7.6 percentage points in clustering accuracy. A comprehensive ablation study further confirms the benefit of each component, demonstrating FRAME's potential for enhancing automated UI design and testing tools.

</details>


### [187] [Biomedical Knowledge Composition: A Software Engineering Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08927)
*Natallia Kokash,Adam S. Z. Bellouma,Paola Grosso*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文从两个视角统一论述生物医学知识基础设施：一方面向软件工程师介绍生物医学领域的知识图谱（KG）核心概念、五大数据协调挑战及应用场景；另一方面分析为何生物医学知识工程仍然困难，提出软件工程实践（如包管理、类型化命名空间、可复现管线等）采纳不足是根本原因，并列出八大开放工程挑战，强调从静态KG实例转向可复现组装过程的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学研究积累了海量分子、临床和人群数据，但将这些数据转化为可操作的知识仍受技术和组织难题制约。本文致力于弥合生物医学领域与软件工程领域之间的鸿沟，促进两个社区之间的相互理解与协作。

Method: 采用统一的双视角论述框架：第一视角向软件工程师系统介绍生物医学知识图谱的基本概念、数据协调挑战、应用领域及六个代表性KG系统；第二视角从工程角度分析生物医学知识基础设施困难的根本原因，借鉴Web工程成熟实践提出对照，并列举八个开放工程挑战。

Result: 本文定义了五大数据协调挑战（标识符映射、实体解析、模式对齐、证据整合和溯源追踪），调查了从药物发现到数字孪生的应用领域，剖析了六个代表性KG系统，并识别了八个缺乏普遍采纳解决方案的开放工程挑战。

Conclusion: 文章呼吁将关注点从描述已部署的KG实例转向可复现的KG组装过程——包括可复用的构建管线、版本化依赖和软件工程实践，使研究者能够从源代码编译和定制KG而非消费静态制品。文中两个视角共同为新人提供领域基础，为软件工程师提供研究议程。

Abstract: Biomedical research has accumulated vast molecular, clinical, and population data, yet translating this wealth into actionable knowledge remains constrained by technical and organizational difficulties. This article presents a unified treatment of two perspectives on biomedical knowledge infrastructure. The first introduces the biomedical domain to software engineers: it explains why knowledge graphs (KGs) are the central integrative data structure in modern biomedicine, characterizes five data harmonization challenges (identifier mapping, entity resolution, schema alignment, evidence integration, and provenance tracking), surveys application domains from drug discovery to digital twins, and profiles six representative KG systems with contrasting choices. The second perspective asks why engineering biomedical knowledge infrastructure remains so difficult. We argue that a contributing root cause is limited adoption of software tooling and practices that make development in other mature domains - particularly web engineering - reliably composable and reproducible: package management, typed namespaces, canonical interchange formats, service composition protocols, reproducible pipelines, and lifecycle governance. Against this backdrop, eight open engineering challenges for biomedical data integration are catalogued, each with partial solutions but no universally adopted stack. Crucially, the article shifts emphasis from describing deployed KG instances toward the reproducible process of assembling them: reusable build pipelines, versioned dependencies, and engineering practices that let others compile and customize a KG from source rather than consuming a static artifact. Together, the two perspectives provide domain grounding for newcomers and a research agenda for software engineers seeking to make transformative contributions to biomedical knowledge infrastructure.

</details>


### [188] [Independent Patch Verification for Coding Agents with a Bidirectional Reconstruct-and-Verify Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08950)
*Chenglin Li,Yisen Xu,Zehao Wang,Shin Hwei Tan,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RETRACE是一个免训练的后生成验证框架，通过双向重建与对账机制为编码智能体生成的补丁提供独立验证信号，在SWE-bench Verified上显著提升了Pass@1指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主编码智能体虽然能直接从bug报告生成代码补丁，但缺乏独立的验证机制来确认补丁是否真正解决问题。已有的迭代自我精化和推理时扩展方法要么在产生补丁的同一解释下审查，要么仅扩大候选生成而不验证单个补丁，均未提供明确的补丁正确性验证信号。

Method: RETRACE包含三个核心阶段：前向重建从issue和智能体轨迹构建显式的修复理由；后向重建仅从补丁和轨迹（不访问原始issue）独立推断补丁试图解决的问题描述，并与原始issue比对产生对齐判定；对账阶段检查前向理由与补丁的一致性，诊断不对齐的根源，然后提交补丁或生成针对性的修订指导。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上使用两个骨干模型（GPT-5-mini和MiniMax-2.5）评估，RETRACE在mini-SWE-agent框架上分别将Pass@1提升了7.0%和3.6%，在OpenHands上无需修改即可获得相当的增益。消融实验表明前向和后向阶段均有贡献，且加入对账阶段可带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: RETRACE通过免训练的双向重建与对账机制，为编码智能体补丁提供了一种有效的独立验证信号，能显著提升补丁修复的准确率，且具有良好的可移植性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents powered by large language models can now generate code patches directly from bug reports, but a fundamental gap remains: once a patch is produced, no mechanism independently verifies whether it truly resolves the reported problem. Prior work has sought to address this through iterative self-refinement and inference-time scaling, but these approaches either review the patch under the same interpretation that produced it or broaden candidate generation without verifying individual patches, and neither provides an explicit verification signal for assessing patch correctness. We propose RETRACE, a training-free post-generation verification framework that derives such a signal through bidirectional reconstruction and reconciliation. When a coding agent generates a candidate patch for an issue, RETRACE performs forward reconstruction to build an explicit repair rationale from the issue and the agent's trajectory; backward reconstruction then independently infers, from the patch and its trajectory alone and without access to the original issue, a description of the problem the patch appears to address, and compares this reconstruction against the original issue to produce an alignment verdict; a reconciliation stage then checks the consistency between the forward rationale and the patch, diagnoses the source of any misalignment, and either submits the patch or produces targeted revision guidance. Evaluated on SWE-bench Verified with two backbones (GPT-5-mini and MiniMax-2.5), RETRACE lifts Pass@1 by 7.0% and 3.6% respectively on the mini-SWE-agent scaffold, and delivers comparable gains on OpenHands without modification. Ablation experiments show that both the forward and backward stages contribute to the overall improvement and that adding reconciliation yields further gains.

</details>


### [189] [GALA: Graph-Augmented LLM Agents for Root Cause Analysis and Incident Response in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08968)
*Yifang Tian,Yaming Liu,Zichun Chong,Zihang Huang,Yiran Li,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GALA+，一个基于图增强的LLM智能体框架，利用服务依赖图约束探索范围并通过局部多模态证据精化诊断，同时引入STRIX评分模块和SURE-Score评估框架，在微服务基准测试中AC@1指标超越最佳LLM基线超过25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微服务根因分析方法存在三大问题：单一遥测模态导致信息不全、LLM方法探索无约束且易产生幻觉、多数系统仅止步于故障排序而缺乏可操作的应急响应建议。

Method: GALA+采用图引导的调查机制，用服务依赖关系约束探索空间；通过STRIX（融合trace和图结构感知的评分模块）结合互补遥测信号生成初始假设；最终输出排序诊断、事件摘要和分层行动建议。同时提出与行业SRE专家共同开发的SURE-Score评估框架，用于衡量RCA输出质量。

Result: 在两个微服务基准数据集上，GALA+整体表现最优，AC@1指标超越最佳LLM基线超过25个百分点，并在SURE-Score和独立人类SRE评估中获得最高评分。

Conclusion: GALA+证明了图增强的LLM智能体框架在微服务根因分析中的有效性，不仅提升了诊断准确性，还提供了可操作的应急响应建议，且其提出的SURE-Score评估框架弥补了传统文本相似度指标在RCA场景中的不足。

Abstract: Microservice root cause analysis (RCA) requires correlating failures across heterogeneous telemetry within complex service dependency graphs. Existing methods often rely on a single telemetry modality; recent LLM-based approaches can suffer from unconstrained exploration and hallucination; and most systems stop at fault ranking without producing actionable incident response. We present GALA+, a graph-augmented LLM agentic framework centered on graph-guided investigation, which uses service dependencies to bound exploration and refine diagnosis through localized multi-modal evidence. For initial hypothesis generation, GALA+ combines complementary telemetry signals with STRIX, a novel trace- and graph-structure-aware scoring module. GALA+ then produces ranked diagnoses, incident summaries, and stratified action recommendations. We further introduce SURE-Score, a human-guided evaluation framework co-developed with industry SRE experts for assessing RCA-specific output quality beyond conventional text similarity metrics. On two microservice benchmarks, GALA+ consistently achieves the strongest overall results, surpassing the best LLM-based baseline by more than 25 percentage points in AC@1, while also receiving the highest ratings from both SURE-Score and independent human SRE evaluation.

</details>


### [190] [Pseudo2CodeQA: A Benchmark for LLM-Based Structured Algorithmic Reasoning in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09068)
*Shadikur Rahman,Umme Ayman Koana,Syed Muhammad Danish*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Pseudo2Code基准测试集（包含300个手工验证的编程任务）和Pseudo2Code Agentic框架（多阶段伪代码驱动的代码生成流水线），实验表明该框架显著优于现有商业和开源基线，证明结构化伪代码能提升代码生成的功能正确性、推理质量和算法忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自然语言转代码方面表现优异，但其遵循结构化算法推理的能力尚未被充分理解，现有基准无法系统评估伪代码对代码生成质量的影响。

Method: 构建Pseudo2Code基准（300个跨领域、三难度级别的真实编程任务，包含问题描述、结构化伪代码、参考实现和可执行测试套件），采用双阶段人工验证协议；提出Pseudo2Code Agentic Framework多阶段流水线，将伪代码作为显式中间推理表征；采用基于评分标准的评估框架（涵盖正确性、完整性、相关性、清晰度、推理质量和伪代码遵循度）及执行测试。

Result: Pseudo2Code Agentic Pipeline在整体评分上达到4.78，显著超过最强基线模型的4.31；100个任务的人工评估显示人工判断与自动评估高度一致。

Conclusion: 结构化伪代码能够提升代码生成的功能正确性、推理质量和算法忠实度，为可解释代码生成和可靠的AI辅助软件开发提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved impressive performance in natural language-to-code generation; however, their ability to follow structured algorithmic reasoning remains insufficiently understood. We introduce Pseudo2Code, a benchmark designed to systematically evaluate the impact of structured pseudocode on code generation quality and algorithmic faithfulness. The benchmark consists of 300 manually validated real-world programming tasks spanning multiple domains and three difficulty levels (Easy, Medium, and Hard). Each task contains a problem description, structured pseudocode, reference implementation, and executable test suite. To ensure benchmark reliability, we adopt a dual-stage human validation protocol and release fully executable benchmark instances. Beyond the benchmark, we propose the Pseudo2Code Agentic Framework, a multi-stage pipeline that leverages pseudocode as an explicit intermediate reasoning representation for code generation. We evaluate both commercial and open-source language models using a rubric-based evaluation framework that measures correctness, completeness, relevance, clarity, reasoning quality, and pseudocode adherence, complemented by execution-based testing. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed Pseudo2Code Agentic Pipeline consistently outperforms strong commercial and open-source baselines, achieving an overall score of 4.78 compared to 4.31 for the strongest baseline model. Furthermore, a human evaluation study involving 100 benchmark tasks shows strong agreement between human judgments and automated assessments. Our findings provide empirical evidence that structured pseudocode improves functional correctness, reasoning quality, and algorithmic faithfulness in code generation. We release Pseudo2Code to support future research on structured reasoning, interpretable code generation, and reliable AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [191] [A Unified Issue Resolution Benchmark for Requirement Clarification, Planning, and Code Generation for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09072)
*Xin Zhou,Chun Yong Chong,Kisub Kim,Yun Peng,Rui Shu,Zihan Wu,Xu Han,Guowen Yuan,Zeyang Zhuang,Jounghoon Kim,Jeongjin Ju,Seongmin Ju,Taein Yoon,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-RPG，一个仓库级基准，结合可执行补丁评估与验证过的中间参考（需求澄清和实现规划），用于诊断编码智能体在仓库修改任务中的失败原因。实验发现现有智能体平均解决率仅31.5%，隐式需求恢复是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级基准仅评估最终补丁是否通过测试，无法刻画智能体在需求澄清、实现规划、代码生成等中间环节的失败模式，难以指导改进方向。

Method: 构建SWE-RPG基准（163个任务，来自31个Python/Java仓库，含113个bug修复和50个功能添加），引入两个中间参考标注（需求澄清和实现规划），支持对完整智能体轨迹进行追溯性、与参考对齐的诊断，并评估3个编码智能体（Claude Code、Codex、OpenCode）搭配6个大模型后端。

Result: 评估的编码智能体平均解决率仅31.5%。基于中间参考的诊断显示，隐式需求恢复是主要瓶颈，占智能体运行失败的24.5%-46.0%。

Conclusion: 隐式需求恢复被识别为提升编码智能体的关键改进方向，SWE-RPG提供的中间参考诊断框架可为后续研究提供支撑。

Abstract: Large language model-powered coding agents are increasingly used to modify existing code repositories, for example, by adding features or fixing bugs. Yet existing repository-level benchmarks typically evaluate only whether the final patch passes tests. Satisfying a user request requires a long chain of interdependent reasoning and decisions: an agent must recover explicit and implicit requirements, formulate a repository-grounded implementation plan, and translate it into correct code. A pass/fail outcome cannot characterize how an unsuccessful trajectory diverges from the requirements and implementation process needed for a correct patch. To address this gap, we introduce SWE-RPG, a repository-level benchmark that combines executable patch evaluation with validated ground-truth references (GTs) for (1) Requirement Clarification and (2) Implementation Planning. These intermediate GTs support retrospective, GT-aligned diagnosis of complete coding-agent trajectories across clarification, planning, code generation, and artifact submission. SWE-RPG comprises 163 tasks from 31 Python and Java repositories, including 113 bug fixes and 50 feature additions. We evaluate 3 coding agents, including Claude Code, Codex, and OpenCode, with 6 large language model backends, including Claude-Sonnet-5 and GPT-5.6-Terra. Results show that the evaluated popular coding agents still struggle to implement user requests in existing repositories, achieving an average resolved rate of only 31.5% on SWE-RPG. Intermediate-GT diagnosis further identifies implicit requirement recovery as the main bottleneck, accounting for 24.5%--46.0% of agent runs. This result suggests implicit-requirement recovery as a key candidate direction for improving coding agents. The benchmark data and evaluation code are available at https://github.com/Xin-Zhou-smu/SWE-RPG-Bench.

</details>


### [192] [Memoir: Learning, Verifying, and Evolving False-Positive Memories for Static Application Security Testing Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09181)
*Shenyuan Guan,Qiaodan Hou,Yanjun Chen,Xincheng Wen,Jia Feng,Keke Lian,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Memoir，一个基于记忆的框架，通过将历史误报（FP）警报转化为可复用的语义记忆来识别SAST工具的假阳性，在CWE-Bench-Java上达到99.43%的F1分数，并在工业场景中跨工具泛化良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST工具在安全开发中不可或缺，但会产生大量误报，增加人工审查成本并降低开发者信任。现有误报消减方法面临两大挑战：不同SAST工具和漏洞类别间差异大，难以学习可复用的历史误报模式；已有方法的知识是静态的，无法随新验证案例的积累而更新。

Method: Memoir框架包含两个核心模块：(1)历史语义记忆构建——通过LLM引导的标注、模式聚类和记忆合成，将历史误报警报转化为结构化的语义记忆；(2)记忆驱动的识别与演化——检索相关记忆，在分类一致性检查和安全不变量验证后进行语义核对做出最终预测，并将验证后的预测回馈到记忆库中，使知识库随新案例持续演化。

Result: 在CWE-Bench-Java基准上，Memoir取得99.43%的F1分数，召回率98.88%，精确率达到完美水平，持续优于其他基线方法。在顶级IT公司的生产软件系统工业案例研究中，学习到的记忆库无需重新训练即可在不同SAST工具间有效泛化。

Conclusion: Memoir通过将历史误报转化为可演化的语义记忆，有效解决了SAST误报消减中模式学习困难和知识静态化的问题，在准确性和跨工具泛化能力上均表现出色，具有显著的工业应用价值。

Abstract: Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools have become indispensable in modern secure software devel- opment. However, these tools often generate false-positive (FP) alerts, imposing substantial manual inspection costs and reducing the trust from developers. Existing FP reduction methods still face two primary challenges. First, the large differences among SAST tools and vulnerability categories make it difficult for these methods to learn recurring patterns in historical false positives. Moreover, the knowledge used by these methods are largely static and cannot be updated as newly validated cases accumulate. To address these challenges, we propose Memoir, a memory- driven framework for identifying false positives by transform- ing historical FP alerts into reusable semantic memories. It consists of two key modules. First, historical semantic memory construction converts historical FP alerts into structured semantic memories through LLM-guided annotation, pattern clustering, and memory synthesis to capture reusable behavioral patterns. Moreover, memory-driven identification and evolution retrieves relevant memories and performs semantic verification against taxonomy consistency and security invariants before making the final prediction. It then incorporates verified predictions back into the memory repository, allowing the knowledge base to evolve as new cases accumulate. We evaluate Memoir on CWE- Bench-Java to demonstrate its effectiveness in real-world security analysis. Specifically, Memoir achieves an F1-score of 99.43% with a Recall of 98.88% and perfect Precision, consistently outperforming other baselines. Furthermore, an industrial case study on production software systems from a top IT company shows that the learned memory base generalizes effectively across different SAST tools without retraining.

</details>


### [193] [Software Engineering for and with GUI Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09278)
*Shengcheng Yu,Yuchen Ling,Junyang Xing,Quan Zhou,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述了2018年1月至2026年4月间的336篇GUI智能体论文，从软件工程视角审视该领域的研究格局、架构、评估与生命周期问题。研究发现该领域自2024年急剧扩张，移动端和网页场景占主导，模块化感知-推理-行动循环架构日益普及，但恢复机制、人工升级、安全执行和可审计性等关键环节仍不成熟，评估聚焦任务成功率而缺乏可比性，测试与维护支持不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体领域快速发展，涌现大量框架、基准和应用，但技术仍显脆弱、工程化不完整、缺乏充分的真实世界验证。现有研究缺少软件工程的系统视角，本文旨在填补这一空白，为该领域提供全面的软件工程视角审视。

Method: 作者系统性地回顾了2018年1月至2026年4月间的336篇GUI智能体论文，通过五个研究问题分别考察研究格局、架构设计、评估方法、软件生命周期关注点以及未来机遇。

Result: 研究发现该领域自2024年以来急剧扩张；移动端和网页场景保持主导地位；模块化感知-推理-行动循环架构日益增多，但恢复、人工升级、安全执行和可审计性发展不足；评估虽更具交互性但仍以任务成功为中心且难以跨协议比较；测试、维护、可观测性、隐私工程和系统化人工监督等生命周期方面均未充分发展。

Conclusion: 能力提升本身无法确保部署就绪。未来研究应将可靠执行与生命周期中心的测试和可复现评估相结合，并整合权限与隐私控制及成本感知的以人为中心的治理，以构建可靠、可维护、安全且可部署的GUI智能体系统。

Abstract: GUI agents have advanced rapidly, producing a growing body of frameworks, benchmarks, and applications. However, this growth has outpaced the maturity of the field. GUI agents remain technically brittle, incompletely engineered, and insufficiently validated for sustained real-world use. They are evolving into closed-loop software systems. Within these systems, model reasoning is coupled with interface perception, execution feedback, recovery, and human oversight. This evolution calls for a software engineering perspective that remains largely absent from existing research. We address this gap by reviewing 336 GUI-agent papers from January 2018 to April 2026. Five research questions examine the research landscape, architectures, evaluation, software lifecycle concerns, and future opportunities. Our findings show that the field has expanded sharply since 2024, while mobile and web settings remain dominant. Architectures increasingly adopt modular perceive-reason-act loops, but recovery, human escalation, safety enforcement, and auditability remain underdeveloped. This architectural imbalance extends to evaluation. Evaluations are becoming more interactive, but they remain centered on task success and are difficult to compare across protocols. More broadly, existing studies provide limited support for testing beyond benchmarks and for maintaining agents after release. Observability, privacy engineering, and systematic human oversight are also underdeveloped. Together, these findings show that capability improvements alone cannot ensure deployment readiness. Future research should connect dependable execution with lifecycle-centered testing and reproducible evaluation. It should also integrate permission and privacy controls with cost-aware, human-centered governance. This integration is necessary to build dependable, maintainable, secure, and deployable GUI-agent systems.

</details>


### [194] [OpenCodeReview: Determinism over Non-Determinism for Cost-Effective Agent-Based Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09290)
*Zhengfeng Li,Lei Zhang,Xianwei Wu,Zhengqi Zhuang,Yingjie Xu,Boge Wang,Shaofei Zhu,Chuan Wang,Guoping Rong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenCodeReview，一种基于确定性工程构建的LLM代码审查代理系统，通过在规则引导调度、接地文件审查和独立反思三个关键环节注入确定性，解决了现有LLM代码审查代理的非确定性和上下文局部性问题。在AACR-Bench基准上，相较主流编码代理（如Claude Code、Codex），SEM-F1最高提升2.17倍（25.10% vs 11.57%），同时消耗更少的token（5-15倍），并在6种LLM后端上保持稳定优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码审查代理存在两大相互交织的弱点：(1)非确定性——无限制的工具使用导致审查结果不稳定；(2)上下文局部性——审查者只能访问diff，限制了可发现问题的深度。这两大弱点引发出三个挑战：上下文检索错位、多文件PR中的连贯性与效率权衡、以及破坏信任的幻觉评论。作者旨在通过确定性工程来解决这些挑战。

Method: OpenCodeReview在三个关键流水线节点注入确定性：1)规则引导调度（Rule-Guided Dispatch）——利用多层规则系统确定性地选择文件和审查标准，消除代理驱动分诊的可变性；2)接地文件审查（Grounded File Review）——通过ReAct循环暴露精选工具集替代自由探索，文件级并行子代理平衡上下文连贯性与效率，并按需恢复跨文件依赖；3)独立反思（Independent Reflection）——采用先证伪（falsification-first）过滤器，在非对称信息边界下，反射器仅看到diff而非代理的工具增强探索，去除幻觉评论而不产生自我强化偏差。

Result: 在AACR-Bench基准上（200个真实PR、10种语言、1,505条专家验证评论），OpenCodeReview在6种LLM后端上均优于主流编码代理（Claude Code和Codex），SEM-F1最高提升2.17倍（25.10% vs 11.57%），同时token消耗减少5-15倍。

Conclusion: 确定性工程为不确定的LLM代理提供了一条有效的设计路径：通过有选择地注入确定性而非赋予最大自由，能够在保持召回率的同时显著提升精确度，降低资源消耗并增强审查结果的可靠性。项目已开源。

Abstract: LLM-based code review agents promise scalable, always-on review, yet current systems suffer from two intertwined weaknesses: (1) non-determinism--unbounded tool use makes review outcomes unstable, and (2) context locality--the reviewer's access remains bounded to the diff, capping discoverable issue depth. Both give rise to three challenges: misaligned context retrieval, a coherence-efficiency trade-off in multi-file pull requests, and hallucinated comments that erode trust. To address these, we introduce OpenCodeReview, built on deterministic engineering for uncertain agents: rather than granting maximal freedom, we inject determinism at three deliberate pipeline points. Rule-Guided Dispatch uses a multi-layer rule system to deterministically select files and review criteria, eliminating variability of agent-driven triage. Grounded File Review replaces free-form exploration with a curated tool set exposed through a ReAct loop, while file-level parallel SubAgents balance context coherence against efficiency and recover cross-file dependencies on demand. Independent Reflection introduces a falsification-first filter under an asymmetric information boundary--the reflector sees only the diff, not the agent's tool-augmented exploration--removing hallucinated comments without self-reinforcing bias, improving precision while preserving recall. On AACR-Bench (200 real-world PRs, 10 languages, 1,505 expert-verified comments), OpenCodeReview outperforms mainstream coding agents (e.g., Claude Code and Codex) across six LLM backends, achieving up to 2.17x higher SEM-F1 (25.10% vs. 11.57%) while consuming 5-15x fewer tokens. We open-source OpenCodeReview at https://github.com/alibaba/open-code-review.

</details>


### [195] [Search-Based Generation of Undetected Quantum Circuit Mutants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09318)
*Eñaut Mendiluze Usandizaga,Thomas Laurent,Paolo Arcaini,Shaukat Ali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出QUMUG，一种基于搜索的量子变异测试方法，利用可参数化量子门生成具有挑战性的变异体。相比现有工具，QUMUG生成的变异体挑战性高3倍，且能促使测试套件增加5倍的测试用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子变异分析工具使用固定的基于门的变异，生成的变异体容易被检测到，降低了其在评估测试套件质量方面的有效性。由于真实故障量子程序可用性有限，量子变异分析对测试套件评估至关重要，因此需要生成更具挑战性的变异体。

Method: QUMUG采用基于搜索的方法，利用可参数化量子门生成变异体。通过搜索算法优化变异参数，找到能通过给定测试套件的非等价变异体。评估了四种搜索算法，包括遗传算法等。

Result: 在30个量子程序上的评估中，QUMUG生成的变异体比现有工具生成的挑战性高3倍。遗传算法最有效，平均每个程序生成494个未被检测的变异体，成功率达99.67%，非等价比例为94.3%。生成的变异体促使测试套件比现有工具多增加5倍的测试用例。此外，一阶变异对增强测试套件更有效，高阶变异则凸显了对新测试用例的需求。

Conclusion: QUMUG通过可参数化量子门与搜索算法的结合，显著提升了量子变异测试中变异体的挑战性和有效性，为量子软件测试套件评估提供了更可靠的手段。一阶与高阶变异各有价值，分别用于增强测试套件和识别独特测试需求。

Abstract: Quantum mutation analysis is emerging as an essential technique for evaluating test suites due to the limited availability of real faulty quantum programs. However, existing quantum mutation analysis tools use fixed gate-based mutations, resulting in easy-to-detect mutants, which reduces their effectiveness in assessing the quality of test suites. We propose QUMUG, a search-based approach for generating challenging mutants by utilising parameterisable quantum gates. QUMUG employs search algorithms to optimise mutation parameters and find non-equivalent mutants passing a given test suite. In our evaluation over 30 quantum programs, QUMUG produced mutants that are three times more challenging than the mutants generated by existing tools. Among the four evaluated search algorithms, the genetic algorithm was the most effective, generating an average of 494 undetected mutants per program with a 99.67% success rate and 94.3% non-equivalent ratio. The generated mutants demonstrated their effectiveness by requiring the addition of five times more test cases to the test suite than the mutants generated by existing tools. We also analysed the behaviour of higher order mutants in quantum circuits, and showed that while first order mutations are more effective for enhancing the test suite, higher order mutants highlight the need for new unique test cases.

</details>


### [196] [White paper: A perspective on civilian-to-defence research transfer to SDD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09349)
*Rute C. Sofia,Daniel Mendez,Simon Barner,Hao Shen,Julian Woermann,Andrea Stocco,Axel von Arnim,Holger Pfeifer,Alexander Pretschner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"生命周期悖论"这一核心概念，指出国防平台采购周期长达数十年，而其搭载的软件和AI模型却须在数天或数小时内迭代演进，这是软件定义国防（SDD）必须解决的根本问题。论文提出基于民用技术的连续DevOps式闭环路径，涵盖系统工程、AI工程和连接性基础设施三个维度，并给出短中长期三阶段建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 军事能力日益由软件决定，但国防平台的采购周期（数十年）与软件AI模型的演进速度（数天/小时）之间存在根本性时间错配，这一"生命周期悖论"是当前软件定义国防面临的核心障碍。

Method: 提出SDD三支柱框架：（1）软件与系统工程（设计、采购、认证）；（2）AI工程（自主性组件的安全与可信）；（3）连接性与基础设施工程。核心方法是从民用技术出发，通过DevOps式连续循环实现：基于模型的系统工程与仿真测试前置设计验证，战术连接与低功耗边缘计算支撑实战部署，并将持续合规、保证和变体管理作为横切关注点贯穿始终。

Result: 论证了该闭环路径在汽车、制造、航天、能源等领域已有成熟能力支撑，当前关键任务是将其在对抗性或国防认证条件下进行验证。论文提出短中长期三条实施路径：短期进行对抗性测试与连接性试点；中期建立增量式认证管道；长期在实战条件下完成闭环验证。

Conclusion: 弥合SDD差距需多方协同承担分布式责任：研究者转向对抗性条件优化方法，工业界将工具暴露于实战需求，政策制定者塑造监管工具，国防机构与作战人员协同验证。在保持民用利益的前提下，通过三阶段推进可逐步实现弹性化的软件定义国防。

Abstract: Military capability is increasingly determined by software. Yet defence platforms are procured on decade-long timescales, while the software and AI models they carry must evolve in days or hours. This paper calls this mismatch the lifecycle paradox, and argues it is the central problem Software-Defined Defence (SDD) must solve.
  SDD rests on three dimensions: software and systems engineering (design, procurement, certification), AI engineering (sovereignty and trust of learned components), and connectivity and infrastructure engineering (timely exchange of information among sensors, AI, and operators). The proposed path to resilient SDD starts from civilian technologies, addressed through a continuous, DevOps-style loop: model-based systems engineering and simulation-based testing front-load design and verification; tactical connectivity and low-power edge execution carry that design into contested operation; continuous compliance, assurance, and variability management run as cross-cutting concerns. This loop is sustainable given capabilities already proven in automotive, manufacturing, space, and energy. The next step is validating them under adversarial or defence-certified conditions, with short-, medium-, and long-term paths to closing gaps.
  Closing the SDD gap while preserving civic benefits is a distributed responsibility: researchers must redirect methods toward adversarial conditions; industry must expose tooling to operational needs; policymakers must shape regulatory instruments; and defence agencies must validate results with operators. Recommendations span three horizons: a short-term baseline of adversarial testing and connectivity pilots; a medium-term pipeline of incremental certification; and a long-term validation closing the loop under operational conditions.

</details>


### [197] [SmellCC: A Tool for Automated Code Smells Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09477)
*Xiaoting Zhang,Yujie Zhang,Zhipeng Gao,Xing Hu,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SmellCC，一个基于LLM的Visual Studio Code扩展，将SonarQube的静态检测与Chain-of-Thought及few-shot学习相结合，实现Python代码坏味道的自动检测与一键重构，清洁率达96.8%，准确率达91.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码坏味道严重威胁软件可维护性并积累技术债务，但开发者在紧张的发布周期下缺乏人工处理这些缺陷的资源。现有静态分析工具（如SonarQube）虽然能精确检测，但只是被动告警系统，重构负担仍全落在开发者身上。

Method: SmellCC是一个Visual Studio Code扩展，通过LLM驱动的流水线增强SonarQube功能。它采用Chain-of-Thought（CoT）推理和few-shot学习技术，针对前10个最常见的代码坏味道提供原地一键式修复（in-place, one-click remediation），在开发过程中主动预防技术债务积累。

Result: 定量评估显示SmellCC表现出色：代码坏味道清洁率达96.8%，修复准确率达91.3%。重构后的代码保持语法正确且行为不变（behavior-preserving），有效提升长期软件可维护性。

Conclusion: SmellCC通过将LLM能力无缝集成到现有静态分析工具链中，显著减少了开发者手动修复代码坏味道的工作量，在保证代码质量和行为一致性的前提下，实现了高效的技术债务预防与清理。

Abstract: Code smells significantly threaten software maintainability by accumulating technical debt, yet developers often lack the resources to manually address these flaws under tight release schedules. While static analysis tools like SonarQube provide precise detection, they function largely as passive alert systems, leaving the burden of refactoring on developers. To bridge this gap, we present a novel cleaning tool, namely SmellCC, a Visual Studio Code extension that augments SonarQube with an LLM-based pipeline to automatically detect and refactor Python code smells. By employing Chain-of-Thought (CoT) and few-shot learning, SmellCC provides in-place, one-click remediation for the top-10 most frequent smells, effectively preventing the accumulation of technical debt during development. Our quantitative evaluation demonstrates that our SmellCC is promising in helping developers effectively eliminate code smells (96.8\% cleaning rate) with high accuracy (i.e., 91.3\%), ensuring that the refactored code remains syntactically correct and behavior-preserving, thereby significantly improving long-term software maintainability.

</details>


### [198] [The Unseen Delta: Characterizing the Compiler Optimization Landscape via Top-Down Differential Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09530)
*Zhibo Liu,Huaijin Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自顶向下的差分分析方法，通过细粒度的层次化微架构指标来校准编译器优化差异，从而定位和修复编译器性能缺陷。实验揭示了GCC与Clang生成的二进制文件之间存在显著且出人意料的性能差异，并开发了二进制补丁框架来移植竞争编译器的优秀代码序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编译器性能缺陷检测方法采用自底向上方式，局限于特定的底层代码属性和已知优化规则，难以量化问题的整体影响，且常常忽视关键的微架构低效问题。同时，不同编译器对相同源码生成二进制文件的性能差异根因尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出自顶向下的差分分析方法：将编译器优化差异与细粒度、层次化的微架构指标进行校准，提供运行时行为的全面视角；采用基于采样的方法高效定位造成性能差异的关键代码片段，实现针对性根因分析；并开发二进制补丁框架，通过移植竞争编译器的优秀代码序列修复已识别的性能问题。

Result: 经验评估揭示了GCC和Clang生成的二进制文件之间存在巨大且往往出人意料的性能差异；对根因进行分类后发现编译器优化中存在系统性挑战；二进制补丁框架在实践中成功修复了已识别的性能问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解和分析编译器优化缺陷提供了全新的视角，通过自顶向下的差分分析方法弥补了现有自底向上方法的不足，对提升编译器性能和软件执行效率具有实践意义。

Abstract: Compiler optimizations are essential for achieving high performance in modern software. However, recent studies highlight the persistence of performance bugs, i.e., subtle defects where the compiler generates functionally correct but computationally inefficient code, leading to significant performance degradation. Existing detection and testing methods typically employ a bottom-up approach, focusing on specific low-level code properties and remaining confined to known optimization rules. Consequently, they struggle to quantify the holistic impact of identified issues and often overlook critical microarchitectural inefficiencies.
  We observe a key indicator of untapped potential: different compilers often produce binaries with significant performance differences for identical source code. However, the root causes of these discrepancies remain largely unexplored and difficult to pinpoint using current techniques. To bridge this gap, we introduce a top-down differential analysis methodology. This approach calibrates compiler optimization differences with fine-grained, hierarchical microarchitectural metrics, offering a comprehensive view of runtime behavior. Using a sampling-based approach, this method efficiently pinpoints the critical code snippets responsible for performance differences, enabling targeted root cause analysis.
  Our empirical evaluation uncovers substantial and often surprising performance differences between binaries generated by GCC and Clang. A categorization of root causes reveals systemic challenges in compiler optimizations. To quantitatively validate our findings and demonstrate practical impact, we developed a binary patching framework that fixes identified performance issues by transplanting superior code sequences from competing compilers. This work provides a novel lens for understanding and analyzing optimization defects.

</details>


### [199] [Representative Dataset Generation Framework for AI-based Failure Analysis during real-time Validation of Automotive Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09651)
*Mohammad Abboush,Christoph Knieke,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于硬件在环（HIL）仿真和实时故障注入（FI）的新方法，用于在系统验证阶段为机器学习应用生成和收集单故障及并发故障下的代表性数据样本，包括时序数据和文本故障日志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动方法在系统开发生命周期的实时系统验证中已取得显著成果，但代表性数据集的可用性仍是主要挑战，需要探索为不同工程应用生成不同形式代表性故障数据的新解决方案。

Method: 提出基于硬件在环（HIL）仿真和实时故障注入（FI）方法的框架，能够在单故障和同时故障条件下生成并收集数据样本，支持生成时序数据和包含故障日志的文本数据。

Result: 实验结果表明该框架能够有效模拟并捕捉系统组件在故障条件下的行为。

Conclusion: 该框架在系统验证阶段生成代表性故障数据方面具有适用性，为机器学习应用提供了可靠的数据支持。

Abstract: Recently, thanks to its ability to extract knowledge from historical datasets, the data-driven approach has been widely used in various phases of the system development life cycle. During real-time system validation, remarkable achievements have been accomplished in developing an intelligent failure analysis based on historical data. However, despite its superiority over other conventional approaches, e.g., model-based and signal-based, the availability of representative datasets persists as a major challenge. Thus, for different engineering applications, new solutions to generate representative faulty data in different forms should be explored. Therefore, in this study, a novel approach based on Hardware-in-the-Loop (HIL) simulation and real-time Fault Injection (FI) method is proposed to generate and collect data samples under single and simultaneous faults for Machine Learning (ML) applications during system validation phases. The developed framework can generate not only sequential data, but also textual data including fault logs. The results show the applicability of the proposed framework in simulating and capturing the system behaviour under faults within the system components.

</details>


### [200] [Security Tests as Executable Specifications for LLM Code Generation: Benefits, Trade-offs, and Coverage Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09740)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了将安全测试作为可执行规范用于大语言模型安全代码生成的策略，提出了SecTDD框架来分离三个因素（测试前置展示、失败后迭代修复、失败选择与表征方式），通过2,705条轨迹、31个任务实例和多个基准验证了可执行反馈能修复安全代码生成，但效果取决于模型、任务、反馈入口点和测试覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能生成功能正确但存在安全漏洞的代码，而安全干预措施可能破坏预期功能。作者希望探究安全测试能否作为可执行规范，在代码生成前及迭代修复过程中同时保障功能性和安全性。

Method: 提出SecTDD框架，通过控制实验分离三个因素：是否前置展示测试、失败执行是否触发修订、失败如何选择和表征。采用行为划分的可见/隐藏测试，使用字节一致的初始候选进行修复对比，在多个安全代码基准、16个CWE类别和两个模型家族上进行系统评估。

Result: 前置展示所有可见测试使隐藏的功能与安全联合成功率平均提升19.3个百分点，但仅改善9个基准-模型条件中的7个并损害2个。结构化反馈修复80个初始失败候选无回归，固定原始反馈修复83个但导致3个回归，两者在其他方面几乎无差别（6胜6负453平）。通过所有可见测试的候选在每种常规机制下仍会失败于隐藏行为族。

Conclusion: 可执行反馈能有效修复安全代码生成，但其收益高度依赖模型、任务、反馈入口点和测试覆盖率。测试覆盖是关键瓶颈——即使通过所有可见测试，隐藏行为族仍可能失败，说明需要更全面的测试设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate functionally useful code that remains vulnerable, while security-focused interventions may break intended behavior. We investigate security tests as executable specifications both before generation and during iterative repair. We develop SecTDD, a controlled test-feedback scaffold that separates three factors: whether tests are shown upfront, whether failed executions trigger revision, and how failures are selected and represented. The evaluation uses behavior-partitioned visible and hidden tests and byte-identical initial candidates for repair comparisons. Across 2,705 trajectories, 31 task instances, three secure-code benchmarks, 16 CWE categories, and two model families, showing all visible tests upfront increases hidden functional-and-security joint success by 19.3 percentage points on average, but improves only seven of nine benchmark-model conditions and harms two. In shared-candidate comparisons, structured feedback repairs 80 initially unsuccessful candidates with no joint regressions; fixed raw feedback repairs 83 but causes three regressions. Structured and raw feedback are otherwise nearly indistinguishable head-to-head (six wins, six losses, and 453 ties). Candidates that pass all visible tests still fail hidden behavior families under every common regime. These results show that executable feedback can repair secure-code generation, but its benefits depend on the model, task, feedback entry point, and especially test coverage.

</details>


### [201] [SpecPath: Testing Coding Agents Across Contract-Equivalent Specification Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09799)
*Yangfan Wu,Haozhe Wang,Huanyu Yang,Jianmin Ji,Fangzhen Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出编码智能体存在的"规范路径敏感性"问题——相同最终含义的需求历史会导致同一智能体系统产生行为不同的程序。作者引入SpecPath诊断评测框架，通过固定仓库、最终合约、验证器、智能体系统和执行预算，仅改变到达合约的修订路径来测试智能体的鲁棒性。实验显示，尽管五个任务和十四种配置的总体准确率几乎不变，但35/100个直接规范成功的完整代码块在至少一条等价历史上失败，表明实现成功不能保证规范路径不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体评测只关注最终需求是否被满足，忽略了"需求是如何被表述/演变"这一关键变量。研究者想知道：智能体是真正解决了活跃的规范，还是仅仅跟随了表述中最显著的路径。这促使将演化需求评估重新定义为"活跃合同解决"问题。

Method: 引入SpecPath诊断评测框架：固定仓库、最终合同、验证器、智能体系统和执行预算，仅改变通向合同的修订路径。使用配对可执行结果而非单个补丁的通过/失败判定，揭示智能体在合同等价的历史中是否实现相同的测试行为。在五个软件任务和14种编码智能体配置上进行对照实验。

Result: 总体上，直接准确率和修订历史准确率几乎不变，但35/100个在直接规范上成功的完整代码块在至少一条等价历史中失败。这表明规范路径敏感性是真实存在的失败模式。

Conclusion: 在一个统一的需求上实现成功并不能保证规范路径不变性。评估演化需求需要受控测试，检验智能体对规范变为最终版本的路径是否鲁棒。

Abstract: Modern coding agents increasingly appear capable of following complex software requirements, yet their success leaves a critical ambiguity: do they resolve the active specification, or merely follow the most salient path by which it was stated? We identify specification-path sensitivity, a failure mode in which requirement histories that are equivalent in their final meaning lead the same agent system to produce behaviorally different programs. This reframes evolving-requirement evaluation as active-contract resolution: before writing code, an agent must determine which requirements still count.
  Building on this view, we introduce SpecPath, a diagnostic evaluation that holds the repository, final contract, verifier, agent system, and execution budget fixed while changing only the revision path that leads to the contract. Rather than treating each patch as an isolated pass or failure, SpecPath uses paired executable outcomes to reveal whether an agent realizes the same tested behavior across contract-equivalent histories. Across five calibrated software tasks and fourteen coding-agent configurations, aggregate direct and revision-history accuracy is nearly unchanged; nevertheless, 35 of 100 complete blocks that succeed on the direct specification fail on at least one equivalent history. These results show that implementation success on a consolidated request does not guarantee specification-path invariance. Evaluating evolving requirements therefore calls for controlled tests of whether agents are robust to the path by which a specification becomes final.

</details>


### [202] [Comprehending Python Repetition Structures: An Eye-Tracking Study with Novice Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09875)
*José Júnior Silva da Costa,Rohit Gheyi,José Aldo Silva da Costa,Márcio Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过眼动追踪实验比较了Python中四种循环结构（for循环、while循环、递归和列表推导式）的代码理解认知负荷，发现for循环视觉负担最低，其他结构分别在注视时长、回视次数等指标上显著增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码理解是软件维护的核心，但不同Python重复结构可能带来不同的认知负担。现有研究缺乏对这些结构在过程层面（如眼动指标）上的系统比较。

Method: 采用受控眼动追踪实验，32名有Python经验的本科生参与，使用拉丁方设计完成六项理解任务，同时收集完成行为数据和眼动指标，对完整代码片段及构建特定兴趣区（AOI）进行分析。

Result: for循环视觉负担最低；相对于for，while循环的兴趣区注视时长增加高达97%、回视次数增加114%（集中于计数器管理）；递归的回视次数增加50%（主要发生在基准条件与递归调用之间）；列表推导式的兴趣区时间增加62.5%、注视时长增加80.9%，水平回视表明逐词元密集解析。列表推导式与其他结构的比较产生了最清晰的统计显著差异，所有非for结构联合比较在所有眼动指标上均显著。

Conclusion: 研究提供了过程层面的证据，证明Python重复结构会引发不同的视觉努力模式，对代码可读性、代码审查、重构、新员工入职培训和可维护性具有实际启示。

Abstract: Code comprehension is central to software maintenance and evolution, yet different Python repetition structures may impose distinct cognitive demands. We conducted a controlled eye-tracking experiment with 32 undergraduate students with prior Python experience to compare comprehension of for loops, while loops, recursion, and list comprehensions (LCs). Participants solved six comprehension tasks in a Latin Square design while we measured completion behavior and eye-tracking metrics over full snippets and construct-specific Areas of Interest (AOIs). for loops showed the lowest visual effort. Relative to for, while loops increased AOI fixation duration by up to 97% and regression count by 114%, with regressions concentrated around counter management. Recursion showed a descriptive 50% increase in regressions, mainly between the base case and recursive call. LCs increased AOI time by 62.5% and fixation duration by 80.9%, with horizontal regressions suggesting dense token-by-token parsing. LC comparisons yielded the clearest statistically significant pairwise differences, while the combined comparison of all non-for structures was significant across all eye-tracking metrics. These findings provide process-level evidence that Python repetition structures induce distinct visual-effort patterns, with implications for readability, code review, refactoring, onboarding, and maintainability.

</details>


### [203] [Detecting Behavioral Changes in Python Refactoring Implementations with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09919)
*Jonhnanthan Oliveira,Rohit Gheyi,Márcio Ribeiro,Alessandro Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于基础模型（foundation model）作为Oracle的方法，通过分析git风格diff来识别Python重构过程中引入的行为变化。研究在Rope重构工具上评估，分析了217个转换对，发现13个不同bug，其中12个已被开发者接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python被广泛使用但其自动化重构仍具挑战性。理解重构过程中如何引入行为变化至关重要，因为这些问题会损害软件可靠性并降低开发者生产力。当前缺乏有效手段来检测重构工具引入的行为变化。

Method: 基于基础模型Oracle的方法，分析git风格diff以识别Python重构引入的行为变化。复用先前研究中的1,152次重构尝试，从中分析217个转换对。

Result: 在7种重构类型中发现13个不同bug，所有bug均已提交给相关开发者，其中12个issue根据issue-tracker证据被接受。

Conclusion: 当前Python重构工具需要提升鲁棒性，以确保自动化代码转换的正确性并支持可靠的软件维护。

Abstract: Python is a widely adopted programming language, valued for its simplicity and flexibility. However, automated refactoring for Python remains challenging, even though refactoring is an essential practice in software evolution aimed at improving internal code structure without changing external behavior. Understanding how behavioral changes are introduced during refactoring is crucial, as such issues can compromise software reliability and reduce developer productivity. We propose an approach based on a foundation model oracle that analyzes git-style diffs to identify behavioral changes introduced by Python refactorings. We evaluated our technique on Rope refactoring implementations, reusing 1,152 refactoring attempts from a prior study and analyzing 217 resulting transformation pairs with the oracle. Our model-based analysis uncovered 13 distinct bugs among the seven refactoring types studied. All reported bugs were submitted to the respective developers, and 12 of the 13 resulting issue reports were accepted according to issue-tracker evidence. These results highlight the need to improve the robustness of current Python refactoring tools to ensure the correctness of automated code transformations and support reliable software maintenance.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [ScaleSense: Cost-Intelligent Scaling Framework via Learned Resource Estimation in Alibaba AnalyticDB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07945)
*Yifan Wu,Yuhan Li,Zhenhua Wang,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou,Zonghao Chen,Liang Lin,Huan Li,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaleSense，一个面向云原生无服务器数据仓库的主动式查询级资源扩缩容框架，通过多面查询编码器、分位数资源预测器和自扩缩控制器，在性能-成本帕累托前沿上动态优化资源配置，在超136万条生产查询上实现了最优资源配置选择相对最佳基线76.7%的提升，并在性能优化策略下将货币成本降低最高5.22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生无服务器数据仓库通过存储与计算解耦实现了细粒度弹性，但异构ad-hoc查询的资源最优分配仍是重大工业挑战。生产负载分析发现存在'过度供给陷阱'——用户因惧怕灾难性资源耗尽而盲目过度配置资源，浪费大量货币预算，且无法缓解非CPU瓶颈（如I/O饱和）。

Method: ScaleSense包含三个核心组件：1) 多面查询编码器，联合建模查询计划拓扑和硬件规格；2) 基于分位数的资源预测器，估计多维度物理资源足迹，作为最优质资源配置的安全网；3) 自动扩缩容控制器，在性能-成本帕累托前沿上导航，根据业务优先级动态调整资源配置，且无需模型重训练。

Result: 在阿里巴巴AnalyticDB超过136万条生产查询上的评估显示：预测精度达到最先进水平且预测区间覆盖良好；最优资源配置选择相对最佳基线提升76.7%；在性能优化策略下满足用户定义性能要求的同时将货币成本降低最高5.22倍；推理延迟低，具备生产环境实用性。

Conclusion: ScaleSense有效解决了无服务器数据仓库中的'过度供给陷阱'问题，在性能和成本之间实现了关键权衡，为生产环境中异构查询的资源弹性管理提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Cloud-native serverless data warehouses achieve fine-grained elasticity by decoupling storage from compute, yet determining the optimal resource allocation for highly heterogeneous ad-hoc queries remains a formidable industrial challenge. Our analysis of production workloads in Alibaba AnalyticDB exposes a costly ``provisioning trap'': the fear of catastrophic resource depletion drives users to blindly over-provision resources, wasting immense monetary budgets without alleviating non-CPU bottlenecks (e.g., I/O saturation). To break this impasse, we propose ScaleSense, a proactive, query-level resource scaling framework. Specifically, it features a multi-faceted query encoder that jointly models plan topologies and hardware specifications. Crucially, a quantile-based resource predictor estimates multi-dimensional physical footprints, acting as a reliable safety net for optimal resource scaling. An auto-scaling controller then navigates the performance-cost Pareto frontier, dynamically tailoring allocations to specific business priorities without requiring model retraining. Evaluations on over 1.36 million production queries show that ScaleSense achieves state-of-the-art prediction accuracy with good prediction interval coverage. By achieving a 76.7% relative improvement in optimal resource configuration selection over the best baseline, this approach addresses the critical performance-cost trade-off while maintaining low-overhead inference latency, confirming its practical performance in production deployments. Under the performance-optimization policy, ScaleSense satisfies user-defined performance requirements while reducing monetary cost by up to 5.22x.

</details>


### [205] [Metadata Reconstruction from Values Alone: Recovering Column Semantics in Undocumented Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07946)
*Mike Helwig*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Rosetta是一个将语言模型放入确定性验证框架中的系统，用于从数据本身恢复生产数据仓库中加密的列名和值语义。它通过确定性分析器提取结构证据，让模型基于证据提出语义解释，并提供带置信度和溯源信息的元数据。实验表明其核心优势不在于生成更好的描述文本，而在于通过确定性证据决定何时"开口"（选择性覆盖），而非提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-SQL基准数据集的列名本身就有含义，但生产环境的数据仓库却充满加密标识符、残缺的文档甚至完全没有文档。作者希望解决从数据本身"恢复"列和值的含义这一核心问题，而不是依赖列名或文档。

Method: Rosetta将语言模型置于验证框架中：1）确定性分析器提取结构证据（值指纹、26种模式库、校验和判定）；2）模型基于证据提出语义解释；3）每个事实附带溯源和由证据类别限定的置信度。此外还设计了"承诺门"（commit gate）机制，要求预测先注册再输出，以代码强制而非提示请求的方式实现弃权。
