Result: 在11个BIRD数据库的680个配对列上（标识符被破坏后）：Rosetta在承诺的42%列上准确率0.475，远高于直接用模型的0.223（覆盖94%）。但在双方都回答的283列上，Rosetta的文本质量与单独使用模型相当，增益来自选择性而非生成能力。Abstention通过代码强制而非提示转移，能实现无证据覆盖率0.000。在盲测i2b2临床仓库上解码95.5%的ICD-9代码并弃权所有NDC药物代码。完整模式遮蔽下，naive翻译器执行准确率从0.92跌至0.42，而Rosetta以59%覆盖率保持86%准确率。

Conclusion: 确定性层是一个"能力检测器"而非"能力放大器"：它的作用在于决定系统何时发言（覆盖率），而非提高发言质量。论文如实报告了负面结果，包括作者自己提出的"权威阶梯"机制并非实现头号效果的原因。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL benchmarks ship schemas whose column names already say what the columns mean. Production warehouses are the inverse: cryptic identifiers, partial or absent documentation. We address the problem they pose first: recovering what columns and values mean from the data itself.
  Rosetta places a language model inside a verification harness: a deterministic profiler extracts structural evidence (value fingerprints, a 26-pattern library, checksum verdicts), the model proposes semantics conditioned on that evidence, and every fact carries provenance and a confidence bounded by its evidence class.
  Against human documentation on 680 paired columns across eleven BIRD databases, identifiers destroyed, the harness delivers metadata that is 0.475 accurate on the 42% of columns it commits to, against 0.223 on 94% for the same model used directly. Restricted to the 283 columns where both arms speak, the harness writes no better prose than the model alone; the gain is selection: deterministic evidence governs whether the system speaks (coverage +0.257 [0.128, 0.378]), not how well. The deterministic layer is a competence detector, not a competence amplifier. A backbone swap bounds the claim: the prose finding reproduces, but prompt-requested abstention does not transfer; a code-enforced commit gate (predictions registered first; measured on a third backbone and held-out databases) makes no-evidence coverage 0.000 on every backbone. On a blind i2b2 clinical warehouse Rosetta decodes 95.5% of 134 real ICD-9 codes from values alone and abstains on all 44 NDC drug codes. The catalog supports calibrated abstention at query time: under full schema opacity a naive translator falls from 0.92 to 0.42 execution accuracy while our gate answers at 86% accuracy over 59% coverage. Negative results are reported plainly, including that our own authority ladder is not the mechanism behind the headline.

</details>


### [206] [Scout: Scalable Document Extraction via Data Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08261)
*Yiming Lin,Chiyu Hao,Shreya Shankar,Aditya G. Parameswaran*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Scout是一个从大规模文档集合中提取数据的工具，通过从少量样本文档中生成并精炼规则（程序）来利用文档间的相似性，在不牺牲准确率的前提下大幅降低成本。实验表明Scout与读取完整文档的前沿LLM代理准确率相当，但在1000篇文档的集合上成本降低61到1000倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM提取数据准确但在大规模文档集合上成本过高。现实中文档集合具有丰富相似性，同一查询在相似文档中的答案往往出现在相似位置，只需读取小范围而不是整个文档。然而已有的利用相似性的方法要么假设文档结构固定，要么假设答案是输入子串并通过LLM生成程序直接定位，但程序搜索空间大且容易过拟合，连前沿agent也难以生成有效程序。

Method: Scout从少量采样文档中生成广泛的规则集，然后通过选择帕累托最优子集来精炼规则，在低成本的同时不牺牲准确率。作者证明了规则精炼问题是NP-hard问题，并给出了具有可证明近似保证的贪心解法。Scout还支持仅部分相似的文档集合（即文档聚簇内相似），采用无需LLM的采样策略从每个聚簇中抽取样本，并使用级联策略选择精炼规则子集，当所选规则不包含答案时回退到未精炼的规则集。

Result: 在六个真实数据集上的实验表明，Scout与读取每个完整文档的前沿LLM agent基准在准确率上持平，但在1000篇文档的集合上成本降低61倍到1000倍以上，且比最强的前程序基方法准确率高61%。

Conclusion: Scout通过生成并精炼可提取规则，有效利用大规模文档集合中的相似性，实现了与前沿LLM相当的数据提取准确率，同时大幅降低成本，为大规模数据提取提供了一种高效且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Extracting values from large document collections powers data analysis across many domains. Frontier LLMs extract such values accurately, but processing an
  entire collection with one is prohibitively costly. Yet this cost is largely avoidable: real-world collections exhibit rich similarity, so for the same query
  over similar documents, the answer tends to recur in similar locations; an LLM need only read that small span, not the whole document. Prior methods that
  exploit this similarity fall short: they either assume a rigid document structure, or assume the answer is a set of substrings of the input and use an
  LLM-generated program to return it directly. Even a frontier agent fails to generate effective programs to directly locate the answer's span, as the search
  space is large and programs learned from a small sample tend to overfit. We present Scout, a tool that generates accurate and cost-effective programs (that
  we call rules) to extract data at scale. From a few sampled documents, Scout generates a broad rule set and refines it by selecting a pareto-optimal subset
  with low cost without sacrificing accuracy. We prove rule refinement is NP-hard and give a greedy solution with a provable approximation guarantee. Scout
  handles collections that are only partly similar, where similarity holds within clusters of documents. In this setting, a sampling strategy, using no LLM,
  draws samples from each cluster; and a cascade strategy selects a subset of refined rules, falling back to the unrefined rule set when the selected rules
  don't contain the answer. Experiments on six real-world datasets show that Scout matches the accuracy of the strongest baseline, a frontier LLM agent that
  reads each full document, while being 61x to over 1000x cheaper on a collection of 1,000 documents, and is 61% more accurate than the strongest prior
  program-based approach.

</details>


### [207] [InSituANN: Revisiting IVF for PCIe-Efficient Billion-Scale Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08751)
*Yuemeng Xu,Zongxi Liu,Junyu Long,Yiming Huang,Jiarui Guo,Yangyujia Wang,Jiachen Xu,Dongyuan Yu,Zongwei Lv,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: InSituANN是一个基于IVF的十亿级向量搜索引擎，在单个商用GPU上运行。它将基向量保留在主机内存中，在GPU上执行细粒度搜索，同时利用GPU进行紧凑的路由和可选的剪枝操作，从而避免通过PCIe传输高维基向量。在SIFT-1B上构建索引仅需5.2分钟（比HNSW快约350倍），在十亿级数据集上端到端吞吐量比PCIe受限的Rummy基线提升104.9x-4298.2x，比DiskANN提升2.4x-4.6x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU的有限VRAM容量使得完全驻留在GPU上的十亿级索引难以部署，而在CPU-GPU异构设计中，将基向量放在主机内存虽然避免了容量限制，但粗粒度卸载到GPU进行细搜索会在PCIe上产生大量的基向量数据传输瓶颈。作者旨在解决这一瓶颈，实现十亿级向量搜索在单块商用GPU上高效运行。

Method: InSituANN采用IVF（倒排文件）架构，核心设计是：将原始基向量保留在主机内存中，在GPU上执行原位（in situ）细搜索，利用GPU处理紧凑的倒排索引路由和可选的剪枝操作。查询处理过程中避免高维基向量在PCIe上的传输，同时保持IVF架构的简单性。此外，论文还设计了超快速的IVF索引构建路径。

Result: 在SIFT-1B上，InSituANN在5.2分钟内构建IVF索引，比实测30.4小时的HNSW构建快约350倍。在十亿级数据集上匹配召回率时，端到端吞吐量比PCIe受限的Rummy基线提升104.9x-4298.2x，比SIFT-1B和DEEP-1B上的DiskANN提升2.4x-4.6x。同时具备更强的召回-吞吐量权衡和比图基替代方案更低的索引空间开销。

Conclusion: InSituANN通过在主机内存中保留基向量并用GPU进行原位细搜索，有效消除了PCIe传输瓶颈，使得十亿级向量检索在成本高效的硬件上变得切实可行。相较于图基方法（如HNSW）和现有异构方案（如Rummy、DiskANN），在构建速度、查询吞吐量和索引空间等多个维度均有显著优势，并已开源。

Abstract: Approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) over billion-scale vector datasets has become a foundational operator for modern retrieval systems, powering large-scale recommendation, semantic search, and LLM/RAG workloads. Although GPUs offer massive parallelism and high-bandwidth memory for batched vector search, their limited VRAM capacity makes fully GPU-resident billion-scale indexes difficult to deploy. In CPU-GPU heterogeneous designs, keeping the base vectors in host memory avoids this capacity limit, but naively offloading fine search to the GPU introduces a new bottleneck: large volumes of base-vector data must be streamed over PCIe.
  We present InSituANN, an IVF-based ANNS engine that enables billion-scale vector search on a single commodity GPU. InSituANN keeps original base vectors in host memory, performs fine search in situ, and uses the GPU for compact routing and optional pruning. As a result, query processing avoids PCIe transfers of high-dimensional base vectors while retaining the simplicity of IVF. Beyond query performance, we further design an ultra-fast IVF construction path for InSituANN. On SIFT-1B, InSituANN builds the IVF index in 5.2 minutes, about 350x faster than the measured 30.4-hour HNSW build. At matched recall on billion-scale datasets, InSituANN improves end-to-end throughput by 104.9x-4298.2x over the PCIe-bound Rummy baseline and by 2.4x-4.6x over DiskANN on SIFT-1B and DEEP-1B. Together with strong recall-throughput trade-offs and lower index space than graph-based alternatives, these gains make billion-scale retrieval practical on cost-efficient hardware.
  We open-source InSituANN at https://github.com/mindtravel/InSituANN-OpenSource.

</details>


### [208] [SiriusDeliver: Automating Data Warehouse Delivery at Tencent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09185)
*Haining Xie,Xiaokai Zhou,Jiaming Yang,Siqi Shen,Ziwei Wang,Yifeng Zheng,Tengyue Xu,Yipeng Shi,Zefang Zong,Yang Li,Peng Chen,Jie Jiang,Debiao He,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出SiriusDeliver，一个面向生产环境数仓任务交付的端到端自动化Agent，通过层次化交付代理、工件生命周期控制和轨迹驱动的技能演化三个组件，在腾讯云WeData大规模部署中实现87.2%端到端成功率和73.5%自主提交流程率，将交付中位时间从228分钟降至23分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数仓任务交付是一个复杂的生产过程，涉及上下文检索、工作流配置、代码生成、平台提交和故障诊断等多个环节。虽然LLM和编码Agent改善了软件开发，但不足以应对生产级数据仓库交付的挑战，因为后者需要依赖感知的编排、生命周期感知的工件控制以及对平台实践演化的持续适应。

Method: SiriusDeliver包含三个核心组件：(1) 层次化交付Agent，负责编排数仓技能；(2) 工件生命周期控制模块，在平台执行前后验证和修订工件；(3) 轨迹驱动的技能演化机制，从交付轨迹中维护可复用技能。

Result: 在腾讯云WeData上部署两个月，覆盖6个业务团队和4种数仓任务类型，服务3600个月活跃用户和18240个交付会话。端到端成功率达到87.2%，自主提交率为73.5%。A/B测试表明，交付中位时间从228分钟降至23分钟，工程师工作量从95分钟降至11分钟，且最终交付成功率与人工相当。

Conclusion: SiriusDeliver通过组合层次化编排、生命周期控制和持续技能演化，能够有效自动化生产级数仓任务的端到端交付，在保证交付成功率的同时大幅提升效率和降低人工投入。

Abstract: Enterprise data warehouses (DWs) support business-critical analytics, but warehouse task delivery remains a complicated production process involving context retrieval, workflow configuration, code generation, platform submission, and failure diagnosis. Although large language models (LLMs) and coding agents have improved software development, they are insufficient for production DW delivery, which requires dependency-aware orchestration, lifecycle-aware artifact control, and continuous adaptation to evolving platform practices. We present SiriusDeliver, an end-to-end delivery automation agent for production warehouse task submission. SiriusDeliver integrates three components: a hierarchical delivery agent that orchestrates warehouse skills, an artifact lifecycle control module that verifies and revises artifacts before and after platform execution, and a trace-driven skill evolution mechanism that maintains reusable skills from delivery trajectories. We evaluate SiriusDeliver through offline datasets and large-scale production deployment on Tencent Cloud WeData. Offline experiments on real-world warehouse delivery cases show that SiriusDeliver improves delivery success and automation efficiency over representative baselines. During a two-month deployment across 6 business teams and 4 warehouse task types, SiriusDeliver served 3,600 monthly active users and supported 18,240 delivery sessions, achieving an 87.2% end-to-end success rate and a 73.5% autonomous submission rate. A one-month A/B test shows that SiriusDeliver reduces median delivery time from 228 to 23 minutes and engineer effort from 95 to 11 minutes, while maintaining comparable final delivery success.

</details>


### [209] [AkasicDB: Demonstrating Omni RAG with a Unified Vector-Graph-Relational DBMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09214)
*Geonho Lee,Jeongho Park,Donghyoung Han,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了AkasicDB数据库系统，原生支持向量检索、图遍历和关系过滤的联合执行，实现了首个原生集成的Vector-Graph-Relational RAG（即Omni RAG）工作流，并通过交互式演示展示其优于纯向量方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统虽不断融合向量检索与结构化知识（如Graph RAG），但数据库架构难以高效支撑此类复杂工作流，依赖数据库外管道或数据库内非原生集成，导致高开销。

Method: 在先前工作Chimera基础上扩展原生向量支持，构建AkasicDB系统，在单一执行框架内联合执行向量相似性搜索、图遍历和关系过滤，实现Omni RAG的原生集成。

Result: 通过交互式聊天演示，用户可执行并可视化Omni RAG查询，体验其优于纯向量方法的检索与推理能力，同时观察到现有数据库架构在支持Omni RAG时的实际局限性。

Conclusion: AkasicDB证明了原生统一执行框架对于复杂RAG工作流的高效支持是可行的，为未来数据库系统设计与RAG集成提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems increasingly combine vector retrieval with structured knowledge, such as Graph RAG and Filtered vector search. However, existing database architectures struggle to support such complex RAG workflows efficiently, as they rely on out-of-DB pipelines or in-DB non-native integration, leading to high overhead. This demo paper presents AkasicDB, a database system that natively supports such RAG workflows by jointly executing vector similarity search, graph traversal, and relational filtering within a single execution framework. AkasicDB extends our prior work, Chimera, with native vector support to enable such unified execution. Based on AkasicDB, we demonstrate the first native integration of Vector-Graph-Relational RAG, which we refer to as Omni RAG. Through an interactive chat-style demonstration, users execute and visualize Omni RAG queries, directly experiencing its superior retrieval and reasoning over vector-only approaches while observing the practical limitations of existing database architectures in supporting Omni RAG. A demonstration video is available at https://youtu.be/8d09_dtrEIM

</details>


### [210] [SafeQL: Search-based Refinement for Safe and Efficient LLM-based Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09260)
*Geonho Lee,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SafeQL提出了一种基于搜索的查询细化范式，将数据库管理系统从被动角色转变为主动指导者，通过解释DBMS反馈来增量修复SQL查询中的错误组件，在Bird和Spider基准测试上显著提升了执行准确率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的Text-to-SQL系统虽然无需任务微调，但生成的SQL查询经常违反数据库模式（如引用不存在的表、属性、函数或值），且系统在查询细化过程中仅依赖DBMS返回的错误消息，DBMS处于被动角色，导致错误反复出现。

Method: SafeQL提出搜索驱动的查询细化范式：解释DBMS反馈，增量修复错误组件而非整体重新生成查询；在安全查询空间内进行引导式搜索，候选查询通过DBMS执行逐步验证，最终收敛到可执行的查询。

Result: 在Bird和Spider基准测试上，SafeQL相比基于重新生成的现有方法，在执行准确率和效率方面均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: 将DBMS作为主动指导者参与查询细化过程，通过增量修复和引导式搜索可以有效避免错误反复生成，提升Text-to-SQL系统的可靠性与执行效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced Text-to-SQL by enabling natural language interfaces to databases without task-specific fine-tuning. However, existing LLM-based systems remain unreliable, often generating SQL queries that are invalid under the database schema, referencing non-existent tables, attributes, functions, or values. Such errors persist because interactions with the database management system (DBMS) are typically limited to error messages, leaving it in a largely passive role during query refinement. This paper proposes SafeQL, \textit{a search-based refinement paradigm that redefines the role of the DBMS as an active guide in the refinement process}. Instead of regenerating entire queries after execution failure, SafeQL interprets DBMS feedback to incrementally repair only the erroneous components. Each refinement step is formulated as a guided search within a \textit{safe query space}, where candidate queries are progressively validated through DBMS execution, thereby converging to an executable query and preventing repeated regeneration of errors. Experiments on the Bird and Spider benchmarks show that SafeQL significantly improves execution accuracy and efficiency compared to regeneration-based methods.

</details>


### [211] ["Will This Data Break My Task?" - Interactive Synthesis of Task-Aware Data Unit Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09376)
*Hao Chen,Arnab Phani,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PrismaDV是一个复合AI系统，通过联合分析数据和下游任务代码，为表格数据合成任务感知的数据单元测试。它利用多个LLM驱动的步骤（数据画像、列访问检测、数据流分析和隐式数据假设推断）来生成数据约束，并通过"数据-代码假设图"将约束与源代码关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据单元测试框架仅从观测数据本身推导测试，忽略了数据下游消费代码的语义，导致生成的测试缺乏任务感知性，无法准确反映数据在实际应用中的约束要求。

Method: PrismaDV采用复合AI系统架构，将测试生成分解为多个LLM驱动的步骤：数据画像、列访问检测、任务代码中的数据流分析、隐式数据假设推断。系统随后合成数据单元测试代码，并维护一个"数据-代码假设图"将生成的数据约束链接回任务源代码。

Result: 系统通过交互式Web界面在五个真实数据集和60个下游任务上进行了演示，支持生成、检查和细化关于数据的自然语言假设及可执行的数据约束。用户可浏览数据-代码假设图，对比任务感知测试与任务无关基线在错误数据批次上的表现，并交互式编辑假设和约束。此外，系统还包含一个自定义提示优化器，可随时间适应特定数据集。

Conclusion: PrismaDV通过同时考虑数据和代码语义来生成更准确、更具任务针对性的数据单元测试，并通过交互式界面和自适应优化机制提升了数据验证的实用性和可解释性。

Abstract: Data is a central resource for modern enterprises and institutions, and data errors propagating through data pipelines lead to serious impact in production. Therefore, data validation is essential for ensuring the reliability of downstream applications. This led to the development of data unit tests, executable programs that test data before moving it around through large data pipelines. However, existing frameworks derive data unit tests from observed data alone, ignoring the semantics of the code that consumes the data downstream.
  To this end, we present PrismaDV, a compound AI system that synthesizes task-aware data unit tests for tabular data by jointly analyzing data and downstream task code. PrismaDV decomposes the test generation into multiple LLM-powered steps: data profiling, detection of column accesses, data flow analysis in the task code, and the inference of implicit data assumptions. It subsequently synthesizes code for the data unit test, and maintains an internal ``data-code assumption graph'' that links generated data constraints back to the task's source code.
  We demonstrate PrismaDV through an interactive web-based interface where attendees run the system on five real-world datasets with 60 downstream tasks, synthesize, inspect and refine both natural language assumptions about the data and executable data constraints. The interface allows attendees to navigate the data-code assumption graph, compare task-aware data unit tests against task-agnostic baselines on erroneous data batches, and interactively edit assumptions and data constraints. Furthermore, attendees can observe how a custom prompt optimizer adapts the system to specific datasets over time.

</details>


### [212] [Monotonicity-Guided Bottom-Up Petri Net Discovery: The SPECpp Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09398)
*Leah Tacke genannt Unterberg,Lisa L. Mannel,Wil M. P. van der Aalst*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SPECpp是一个基于Petri网自底向上的过程发现框架，通过利用库所的单调性质高效表征行为，并实施多种策略在时间和资源约束下获取高质量模型，该方法无需预定义的结构构造，可充分发挥Petri网的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自顶向下的过程发现方法（如Inductive Miner）依赖预定义结构（顺序、选择、循环、并发），限制了模型的表达力。Petri网虽能表达复杂行为，但其全局行为分析困难，且候选库所数量呈指数级增长，需要高效策略来发现高质量模型。

Method: SPECpp框架采用自底向上的发现方法，利用单个库所的单调性质进行高效表征和发现，使复杂结构（如自由选择结构和长期依赖）能够有机涌现，而非依赖预定义构造。框架实现了多种策略来应对候选库所及其组合的指数级搜索空间，在时间和资源约束下寻求高质量模型。

Result: SPECpp支持快速实验，并使用合成事件数据和真实事件数据评估了所提出的多种发现策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 自底向上的SPECpp框架相比自顶向下方法能够利用Petri网更完整的表达能力，通过策略化搜索在有限资源约束下获得高质量的过程模型。

Abstract: Process discovery is one of the central challenges in process mining. Petri nets are particularly attractive because simple local constructs can express complex behavior, including concurrency. While their global behavior may be difficult to analyze, individual places can be efficiently characterized using monotonic properties, enabling bottom-up discovery. Unlike top-down approaches such as the Inductive Miner, which rely on predefined constructs for sequences, choices, loops, and concurrency, our approach allows such structures to emerge organically and can exploit the full expressive power of Petri nets, including free-choice constructs and long-term dependencies. The main challenge is the exponential number of candidate places and their combinations. We present the SPECpp framework which implements strategies to obtain high-quality models under time and resource constraints. SPECpp supports rapid experimentation and is used to evaluate these strategies using both synthetic and real-life event data.

</details>


### [213] [Carnot: Interpretable, Interactive, and Optimized Execution of Deep Research Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09532)
*Matthew Russo,Yash Agarwal,Tianyu Li,Zhuohan Gu,Michael Cafarella,Omar Khattab,Tim Kraska,Samuel Madden*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Carnot是一个面向AI驱动分析的交互式执行引擎，将自然语言请求编译为物理执行图，通过交互式笔记本界面让用户能够批判计划、增量执行算子、检查中间数据并编辑代码或语义指令，同时支持基于成本和延迟约束的查询优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业数据湖的自然语言查询工具存在两类问题：深度研究代理作为黑盒运行，隐藏推理和检索步骤，缺乏成本与延迟控制；而语义算子方案在企业规模数据湖上过于昂贵。分析师因此无法拦截幻觉前提、验证中间结果或纠正系统轨迹。

Method: Carnot将自然语言请求编译为物理执行图，并通过交互式笔记本界面呈现。用户可以批判执行计划、增量执行操作符、检查中间数据、直接编辑底层代码或语义算子指令。查询优化器根据用户提供的成本或延迟约束优化查询。

Result: 演示展示了Carnot如何在真实企业用例驱动的工作负载上帮助用户实现高效且可验证的洞察。

Conclusion: Carnot通过将AI驱动分析的执行过程透明化并赋予用户交互控制权，弥合了黑盒深度研究代理与高成本语义算子之间的鸿沟，实现可验证且成本可控的企业数据湖自然语言查询。

Abstract: Enterprises increasingly seek to query data lakes using natural language via AI-driven tools like semantic operators or deep research agents. However, the latter operates as an opaque black box, hiding its intermediate reasoning and data retrieval steps, and failing to expose controls for managing API costs and execution latency. Meanwhile, the former can be prohibitively expensive for enterprise-scale data lakes. Consequently, analysts using these systems lack the agency to intercept hallucinated premises, verify intermediate results, or correct the system's trajectory. We present Carnot, an interactive execution engine for AI-driven analytics. Carnot compiles natural language requests into physical execution graphs and surfaces them through an interactive notebook interface. Rather than waiting blindly for a final output, users can critique the plan, incrementally execute operators, inspect intermediate data, or directly edit the underlying code or semantic operator instructions. Carnot's query optimizer will optimize the query with respect to cost or latency constraints provided by the user. Our demo will showcase how Carnot helps users achieve efficient and verifiable insights on workloads motivated by real enterprise use cases.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [LGNNIC: Acceleration of Large-Scale GNN Training using SmartNICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07733)
*Liad Gerstman,Aditya Dhakal,Dejan Milojicic,Avi Mendelson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出LGNNIC架构，利用SmartNIC协同远程内存节点，在分布式GNN训练中通过邻居采样和量化两项预处理技术减少节点间通信数据量，缓解网络拥塞，实现最高62.4x训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着图规模的增大，将图数据完全存储在单节点CPU-GPU系统上变得不切实际。将图分布到多个远程内存节点会带来训练期间节点间网络拥塞这一主要瓶颈，亟需降低通信开销的方法。

Method: 提出LGNNIC架构，利用与远程内存节点共置的SmartNIC（如NVIDIA BlueField-2）卸载关键预处理任务。包括两项互补技术：在SmartNIC上执行邻居采样（Neighbor Sampling）以进行小批量采样，以及对采样批次进行量化（Quantization）。同时设计了基于DMA的低开销同步机制和基于Socket的高开销基准方案用于评估。

Result: 在包含BlueField-2 SmartNIC远程内存节点和A100 GPU计算节点的PoC系统中，邻居采样在Sockets和DOCA-DMA下分别实现最高62.4x和17.5x加速，主要归因于数据事务时间减少；量化额外带来最高3.6x和1.3x加速。

Conclusion: LGNNIC通过SmartNIC卸载邻居采样和量化预处理，能显著减少分布式GNN训练中的数据传输量，有效缓解网络拥塞并大幅提升训练速度，且在多种通信基础设施下均表现出有效性。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are widely used across domains such as natural sciences, social network analysis, chip design, and recommendation systems. However, as graph sizes grow, storing and processing them entirely on a single-node CPU-GPU system becomes increasingly impractical. A promising approach is to distribute the graph across multiple remote memory nodes, though this introduces a major bottleneck: inter-node network congestion during training. To address this, we propose LGNNIC, a novel inter-node system architecture that leverages SmartNICs co-located with remote memory nodes-a configuration already available in modern systems-to reduce communication overhead in distributed GNN training. LGNNIC offloads key preprocessing tasks to SmartNICs, reducing the volume of data transferred to computational (training) nodes and alleviating network congestion. We introduce two complementary techniques executed on the SmartNICs during the preprocessing phase: Neighbor Sampling, which performs mini-batch sampling, and Quantization of the sampled batches. To evaluate LGNNIC under different communication infrastructures, we designed both an optimized low-overhead DMA-based synchronization mechanism and a high-overhead socket-based alternative used as a benchmark. We evaluate the core SmartNIC offloading mechanisms across standard GNN workloads and sampling hyperparameters using a proof-of-concept (PoC) system comprising one remote-memory node with an NVIDIA BlueField-2 SmartNIC and one compute node with an A100 GPU. Both Neighbor Sampling and Quantization on the remote node demonstrated substantial training speedups in most configurations. Neighbor Sampling achieved up to 62.4x and 17.5x speedups with Sockets and DOCA-DMA, respectively, primarily due to reduced data transaction time. Quantization provided additional speedups of up to 3.6x and 1.3x, respectively, by reducing data transfer.

</details>


### [215] [ElastiCo: Elastic Configuration and Interference-Aware Orchestration for GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07971)
*Jinghao Wang,Yihang Zhou,Xiaoyang Sun,Chunming Hu,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Albert Y. Zomaya,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ElastiCo是一个弹性共置框架，通过资源形状变换、弹性影子定价和干扰感知共置三种机制，使GPU集群中的训练和推理工作负载安全共享GPU资源，将平均作业完成时间缩短至2.94倍，集群吞吐量提升2.02倍，GPU利用率从约25%提升至46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU集群调度器将深度学习训练和离线大语言模型推理视为孤立的资源消费者，采用刚性静态分配方式，导致训练作业在周期性空闲阶段占用整个设备、推理任务在突发需求下过度配置GPU，造成大量GPU容量闲置。

Method: ElastiCo通过三个集成机制实现弹性共置：(1)资源形状变换，将每个作业表示为一系列可行的资源-性能配置；(2)弹性影子定价，通过动态的资源影子价格将多资源分配问题分解为每个作业的配置选择子问题；(3)干扰感知共置，利用基于硬件计数器和任务级特征训练的预测器估计GPU共享下的性能退化。

Result: 在64-GPU测试平台和大规模轨迹驱动模拟（最高512 GPU）上评估，平均作业完成时间（JCT）最多降低2.94倍，集群吞吐量提升2.02倍，GPU利用率从约25%提高到46%。该框架已作为原生Kubernetes中间件实现，无需修改用户代码。

Conclusion: ElastiCo证明通过弹性共置框架可以有效弥合训练与推理工作负载之间的资源利用鸿沟，在不牺牲性能的前提下显著提升GPU集群的利用率与吞吐量，为异构工作负载的混合调度提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern GPU clusters must simultaneously serve deep learning training and offline large language model inference workloads, yet existing schedulers treat these as isolated resource consumers with rigid, static allocations. This leaves substantial GPU capacity underutilized: training jobs reserve entire devices despite periodic idle phases, while offline inference tasks over-provision GPUs despite bursty demand patterns. We present ElastiCo, an elastic co-location framework that enables training and inference workloads to safely share GPUs through three integrated mechanisms. First, Resource Shape Transformation exposes each job as a family of feasible resource-performance configurations. Second, Elastic Shadow Pricing decomposes the resulting multi-resource allocation problem into per-job configuration selection subproblems via dynamic per-resource shadow prices. Third, Interference-Aware Co-location uses a predictor trained on hardware-counter and task-level features to estimate pairwise performance degradation under GPU sharing. Implemented as native Kubernetes middleware requiring no user-code modifications, ElastiCo is evaluated on a 64-GPU testbed and through large-scale trace-driven simulations (up to 512 GPUs), reducing the average JCT by up to 2.94x, increasing the cluster throughput by 2.02x, and increasing the GPU utilization from approximately 25% to 46%.

</details>


### [216] [eIRWR: Enhanced Iterative Random Walk with Restart for Scalable Root Cause Analysis in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08073)
*Saiful Khan,Afrah Farea*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了增强型迭代替换随机游走（eIRWR）方法用于微服务架构中的根因分析。该方法通过幂律电报传输锐化、自环与反向边增强以及外层循环信念细化三个创新点，在阿里巴巴微服务追踪数据集的大规模拓扑上取得了MRR=0.75（中等可见性）和0.94（高可见性）的成绩，较最优聚合指标基线提升2.8倍，且推理时间低于25ms，适合在线部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构中根因分析需要从数百上千个相互依赖的服务中定位故障源头，但现有的基于图的随机游走方法（如重启随机游走）从原始异常向量重启，该向量被下游明显的"受害者"服务所主导，导致源头的微弱信号被淹没，定位效果不佳。

Method: 首先通过受控消融实验证明"弹性阻尼"在数学上等同于提高重启概率，因此以重启调优的个性化PageRank（PPR）作为基线。在此基础上提出eIRWR方法，包含三个关键改进：(a)幂律电报传输锐化——将重启质量集中在最可疑节点上；(b)为转移矩阵增加自环和反向边，使概率在级联源头处累积；(c)通过外层循环迭代细化信念。

Result: 在来自阿里巴巴微服务追踪数据集的三个大规模拓扑（12K-25K节点）上，eIRWR在中度根因可见性下达到MRR=0.75，较最优聚合指标基线提升2.8倍，优于重启调优的PPR；在高度可见性下达到MRR=0.94，且在图含17,000个节点时运行时间低于25ms。

Conclusion: eIRWR通过多项增强策略有效利用了以往被忽略的定位信号，在定位精度和推理效率上均显著优于现有基线，具备在线部署的可行性，为大规模微服务系统的根因分析提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) in microservice architectures needs to pinpoint the originating faulty service responsible for the cascading symptoms seen across hundreds or thousands of interdependent services. Graph-based random walk methods propagate anomaly evidence over the service dependency graph. However, existing anomaly-restart walks leave much of the localization signal unused: they restart from the raw anomaly vector, which is dominated by loud downstream victims rather than the quieter source. Through a controlled ablation, we first show that the "resilience damping" often applied to the transition matrix is mathematically equivalent to raising the restart probability; we therefore benchmark against a restart-tuned Personalized PageRank (PPR) rather than its default configuration. We then present Enhanced Iterative Random Walk with Restart (eIRWR), which (a) concentrates restart mass on the most suspicious nodes through power-law teleportation sharpening, (b) augments the transition matrix with self-loops and backward edges so that probability accumulates at cascade sources, and (c) refines its belief across an outer loop. On three large-scale topologies (12K-25K nodes) from the Alibaba Microservice Trace Dataset, eIRWR attains a Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) of 0.75 at moderate root-cause visibility, a 2.8 times improvement over the best aggregate-metric baseline and well above a restart-tuned PPR. At high visibility, it reaches MRR= 0.94, while running in under 25ms on graphs with 17,000 nodes, making it suitable for online deployment.

</details>


### [217] [Effect of Abstractions and Prompting Strategies on LLM-Guided High-Performance Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08085)
*Jiří Klepl,Maty'aš Brabec,Martin Kruliš*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在LLM引导的并行HPC代码优化中，传统自动化优化抽象（如计算管道和优化调度框架）是否能提升优化效果。通过PolyBench基准测试评估发现，直接向LLM提供特定优化目标生成C代码，比使用传统框架构建优化管道获得更好的性能和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码性能优化对现代软件开发至关重要，但需要深厚的底层硬件知识和并行处理经验。LLM的出现为自动化代码优化带来新可能，然而传统自动化优化依赖领域特定表示和严格规则，而LLM能利用高层代码语义进行超越可验证自动化的变换。核心问题是：传统优化抽象能否提升LLM引导的性能和正确性。

Method: 使用PolyBench基准测试套件，对比两种LLM引导的优化方式：一种是为LLM提供特定优化目标直接生成优化的C代码；另一种是利用传统自动化优化框架构建计算管道和优化调度方案来配合LLM使用。通过测量性能表现和有效性（validity rate）进行评估。

Result: 在评估的设置下，直接向LLM提供具体优化目标生成C代码的方式，相比使用传统框架构建计算管道和优化调度的方式，在测得性能和有效性方面均表现更好。

Conclusion: 传统自动化优化中使用的抽象并不会提升LLM引导优化的效果，反而直接驱动LLM生成代码更优。这暗示未来应探索替代方法来实现可验证的LLM引导代码优化。

Abstract: Code performance optimization is a vital aspect of modern software development, as it enables faster response times and reduced resource usage. These optimizations require a deep understanding of low-level hardware details and the intricacies of parallel processing, making them challenging even for experienced developers. With the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), which are increasingly capable of generating and understanding code, there is growing interest in incorporating these models into automated code optimization processes. Traditionally, this automation involves transcribing the source code into a domain-specific representation that can be auto-tuned using grid search or machine learning algorithms, while adhering to strict rules and a limited set of feasible transformations to ensure verifiability. LLMs incorporate high-level code semantics and can thus perform transformations that go beyond verifiable automated optimizations. This paper investigates whether the traditional abstractions used in automated code optimization improve the performance and correctness of LLM-guided optimizations of parallel HPC applications. We evaluate this using the PolyBench benchmark suite and demonstrate that, in our evaluated setting, LLMs provided with specific optimization goals achieve better measured performance and validity rates when generating C code compared to creating computation pipelines and optimization schedules with established frameworks, suggesting that future development should explore alternative approaches for verifiable LLM-guided code optimization.

</details>


### [218] [OasisKV: Scaling In-Decode KV Cache Beyond HBM with Lookahead Sparse Prefetching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08097)
*Can Xiao,Sukmin Cho,Junbong We,Zhixiong Niu,Jianyi Cheng,Yiren Zhao,Youngjin Kwon,Yongqiang Xiong,Rui Ma,Junyi Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OasisKV是一个面向LLM推理的内存中心化系统设计，通过将KV缓存从HBM中解耦存储，利用解码时注意力稀疏性和投机解码的lookahead令牌预测未来重要KV块并预取到HBM，从而缓解HBM容量压力，在保证精度的前提下显著提升推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理服务日益受内存而非算力限制，KV缓存在长上下文和长链推理场景下占据主导性的内存占用和内存流量，HBM容量成为稀缺且昂贵的资源，严重制约推理批处理大小和系统吞吐量。

Method: OasisKV将完整KV缓存存储与HBM解耦，利用解码时注意力的天然稀疏性，仅将最相关令牌的KV条目保留在HBM中。通过投机解码（SD）产生的lookahead令牌提前预测未来重要KV块，并采用高效的注意力后台流水线识别重要KV块，从更高容量内存层（主机或远程内存）预取并暂存到HBM供下一步解码使用。

Result: 基于vLLM实现，在2048令牌KV预算下保持与全注意力精度差距在0.7分以内。在推理工作负载上比密集vLLM提升1.69倍吞吐量（精度损失仅0.1分），多GPU长上下文服务最高提升2.1倍。在prefill-decode分离架构下，吞吐量约为密集的2倍，每个请求KV占用减少6.5-9.7倍，解码节点主机内存占用比全KV传输减少2.2-2.6倍。

Conclusion: OasisKV通过内存中心化的系统设计与lookahead预测驱动的KV预取策略，成功将KV缓存的稀疏性转化为实际吞吐量收益，在保持高精度的同时显著缓解HBM容量瓶颈，为长上下文和推理密集型LLM服务提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference serving is increasingly constrained by memory rather than compute. As long-context and long-form reasoning workloads become more prevalent, the key-value (KV) cache dominates both memory footprint and memory traffic during LLM token generation, i.e., decode. In particular, HBM capacity has become a scarce and costly resource that heavily limits inference batch size and system throughput. This paper presents OasisKV, a memory-centric LLM inference system design that alleviates HBM capacity pressure by decoupling full KV-cache storage from HBM during LLM decoding. Because decode-time attention is naturally sparse, OasisKV keeps only the KV entries of the most relevant tokens in HBMs for attention computation. We observe that future important tokens can be predicted accurately in advance using lookahead tokens drafted by speculative decoding (SD). OasisKV employs an efficient attention background pipeline to identify important KV blocks. They are then prefetched from higher-capacity memory tiers (e.g., host or remote memory) and staged in HBMs before being used in the next decode step.
  We implement OasisKV based on vLLM. The lookahead prediction is accurate enough to keep accuracy within 0.7 points of full attention under a 2,048-token KV budget. This lets OasisKV turn sparsity into throughput gain: $1.69\times$ over dense vLLM on the reasoning workload at 0.1 points of accuracy loss, and up to $2.1\times$ on multi-GPU long-context serving. Under prefill--decode disaggregation, OasisKV reaches about $2\times$ dense throughput while admitting each request with $6.5$--$9.7\times$ less KV and holding $2.2$-$2.6$ less decode-node host memory than full KV transfer.

</details>


### [219] [Evaluating and Improving Weak Scalability Analysis of Visualization Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08166)
*Marvin Petersen,Jonas Lukasczyk,Christoph Garth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出传统大规模数据可视化研究中弱可扩展性评估存在的问题——常见的数据缩放方法仅增加输入规模，未考虑输入复杂度或输出大小等工作负载因素，导致弱可扩展性评估不准确。作者在共享内存环境下提出了一种有效缓解不同算法和数据集上工作负载增长不一致的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱可扩展性是评估可视化算法在大规模数据下适用性的重要指标，但传统的数据缩放方法仅依赖简单放大输入数据集，忽略了算法工作负载受输入复杂度或输出大小等非输入规模因素的影响，导致弱可扩展性评估结果不准确。

Method: 作者在多种算法和数据集上系统比较了常见的数据缩放方法，识别出不同缩放方法在不同算法和数据集上的适用性差异，并提出了一种在共享内存环境中有效纠正工作负载增长不一致的方法。

Result: 实验表明，不同的数据缩放方法在不同算法和数据集上的弱可扩展性评估结果存在显著不一致性，作者提出的方法能有效缓解这些观测到的工作负载增长不一致问题。

Conclusion: 作者旨在推动可视化社区对大规模算法评估和报告方式的深入讨论，强调需要更严谨地评估可扩展性。

Abstract: Research on visualizing large-scale datasets traditionally relies on empirical evaluation of scalability, determining the effectiveness of specific computation methods, algorithmic strategies, or implementations. Weak scalability, which assesses the algorithm's performance as problem size and computing resources increase, is a valuable indicator for a method's applicability at scale. However, sufficiently large data sets with increasing size are needed for weak scalability studies. To this end, it is customary to use simple scaling techniques to increase problem size by generating larger input data sets from a base data set. Nevertheless, many visualization algorithms' workload depends on factors beyond input size, such as input data complexity or output size, leading to inaccuracies in the attributed weak scalability. In this work, we highlight different common data scaling methods on multiple algorithms and data sets, recognizing that the suitability of scaling approaches varies across algorithms and data sets. We present a method that effectively mitigates the observed inconsistencies in workload increases for the different scaling methods in a shared-memory setting. With this work, we aim to further the discussion on how to evaluate and report scalable visualization research.

</details>


### [220] [OpRAG: A Resource-Deterministic Runtime for GPU-Backed Multi-Stage RAG Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08340)
*Arup Kumar Sarker,Mills Staylor,Aymen Alsaadi,Gregor von Laszewski,Shantenu Jha,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OpRAG是一个面向GPU多阶段RAG工作流的资源确定性分布式运行时，通过将嵌入、检索、推理、记忆和更新建模为一等算子并转化为通信感知执行图，结合Arrow零拷贝数据平面、持久化工作节点、有界队列、CPU tokenizer预取、批量GPU嵌入和检索/生成重叠执行等技术，在不修改LLM解码内核的情况下，相比现有框架实现了约15-21%的端到端性能提升，并将混合检索延迟降低约59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统虽然LLM解码是GPU密集的，但其周边编排层（序列化开销、碎片化调度、低效批处理、CPU-GPU流水线停顿）会限制端到端性能。现有框架要么提供灵活控制流（如LangChain），要么提供可扩展任务并行（如分布式运行时），但都没有将RAG阶段暴露为资源感知、具有确定性执行语义的算子。

Method: OpRAG将嵌入、检索、推理、记忆和更新作为一等算子，将其降级为通信感知执行图；采用Arrow零拷贝数据平面、持久化工作节点、有界队列、CPU tokenizer预取、批量GPU嵌入以及检索/生成重叠执行等多项优化，以减少LLM推理周围的非模型开销。

Result: 使用Llama3-8B和Mistral-7B评估（FlashAttention-2、BF16、32K RAG块）：相比最近竞争者提升16.16%（Llama3-8B）和15.66%（Mistral-7B）；相比RayScalableRAG提升20.57%和20.71%；相比LangChain、LangGraph、CrewAI、AutoGen中最优基线快17.77%和17.48%；在Higress风格查询服务中，混合检索延迟降低59.20-59.62%，生成场景延迟降低52.48-53.55%，同时保持100% Recall@5。

Conclusion: 优化分布式编排层可以在不改动LLM解码内核的情况下显著提升GPU支撑的多阶段RAG性能，验证了资源确定性分布式运行时作为RAG工作流执行方案的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems combine preprocessing, embedding, retrieval, memory access, context construction, generation, and vector-index updates. Although LLM decoding is GPU-bound, the surrounding orchestration layer can still limit end-to-end performance through serialization overhead, fragmented scheduling, inefficient batching, and CPU--GPU pipeline stalls. Existing frameworks provide flexible control flow, while distributed runtimes provide scalable task parallelism, but neither exposes RAG stages as resource-aware operators with deterministic execution semantics. We present OpRAG, a resource-deterministic distributed runtime for GPU-backed multi-stage RAG workflows. OpRAG models embedding, retrieval, reasoning, memory, and upsert as first-class operators and lowers them into communication-aware execution graphs. It combines an Arrow zero-copy data plane, persistent workers, bounded queues, CPU tokenizer prefetching, batched GPU embedding, and overlapped retrieval/generation execution to reduce non-model overhead around LLM inference. We evaluate OpRAG using Llama3-8B and Mistral-7B with FlashAttention~2, BF16 execution, and 32K RAG chunks. In end-to-end GPU pipeline experiments, OpRAG improves over the nearest competitor by 16.16% for Llama3-8B and 15.66% for Mistral-7B, and over RayScalableRAG by 20.57% and 20.71%, respectively. Against LangChain, LangGraph, CrewAI, and AutoGen, OpRAG is 17.77% and 17.48% faster than the best framework baseline. In Higress-style query serving, OpRAG reduces hybrid retrieval latency by 59.20--59.62% and generation-scenario latency by 52.48--53.55%, while preserving 100% Recall@5. These results show that optimizing the distributed orchestration layer can substantially improve GPU-backed multi-stage RAG without modifying the LLM decoding kernel.

</details>


### [221] [Empirical Analysis of Cloud-Edge Infrastructure Complexity: Practitioner Pain Points and Architectural Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08400)
*Pawissanutt Lertpongrujikorn,Hai Duc Nguyen,Mohsen Amini Salehi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 基于101次半结构化访谈（覆盖86个组织）的实证研究，验证了部署复杂性和入门困难是云原生开发的主要瓶颈，并提出了四种架构方向（统一对象抽象、内部开发者平台、声明式AI/ML流水线、WebAssembly边缘运行时）来缓解基础设施复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云、边缘和IoT计算的普及带来了巨大的基础设施复杂性，严重阻碍了开发者生产力和创新。作者希望通过实证数据验证这一假设，并以数据驱动的视角探索可行的架构缓解路径。

Method: 采用实证研究方法，基于101次半结构化访谈覆盖86个组织，对云原生开发实践、痛点与期望进行定量分析，并基于验证结果评估四种候选架构方向。

Result: 部署复杂性（38.6%）和入门困难（35.6%）是主要运维瓶颈；开发者优先关注生产力（53.5%）和自动化（44.6%）而非性能优化。四种架构方向均被视为可行路径，但需满足安全、运维集成和多租户隔离等生产级前提。

Conclusion: 分布式计算采纳的主要障碍并非执行性能而是基础设施复杂性，声明式治理且跨范式的高层抽象是缓解该瓶颈的有效架构路径。

Abstract: The proliferation of cloud, edge, and Internet of Things (IoT) computing has created unprecedented opportunities for distributed applications. However, this architectural shift introduces profound infrastructural complexity, acting as a significant barrier to developer productivity and innovation. In this paper, we present an empirical analysis based on 101 semi-structured interviews across 86 organizations to investigate the state of cloud-native development practices, pain points, and expectations. Our findings quantitatively validate that deployment complexity (38.6%) and onboarding difficulty (35.6%) are the dominant operational bottlenecks, while developers heavily prioritize productivity (53.5%) and automation (44.6%) over raw performance optimization. Based on these empirical insights, we examine four architectural directions that address the validated pain points: unified object abstractions (Object-as-a-Service), platform engineering via Internal Developer Platforms, declarative AI/ML serving pipelines, and lightweight edge runtimes based on WebAssembly. Furthermore, we detail the multi-stakeholder ecosystem required for adopting novel infrastructure paradigms, emphasizing that security, operational integration, and strict multi-tenant isolation are prerequisites for production readiness. Our results demonstrate that the primary barrier to distributed computing adoption is not execution performance but infrastructural complexity, and that declaratively governed, higher-level abstractions across multiple paradigms offer viable architectural paths toward alleviating it.

</details>


### [222] [FlashBoot: Sub-Second Weight Loading for Large Models at Rack Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08482)
*Issac Zhu,Hscos Zhang,Keith Jiang,Jack Li,Hugh Yin,Jason Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlashBoot是一个面向Mixture-of-Experts模型的硬件友好、框架工作流协同设计的权重加载子系统，基于SGLang构建。其核心组件FabricArena提供连续、可导出、节点间可寻址的张量内存布局，FlashLoad实现CPU批量零拷贝加载，FlashClone通过远程映射机制替代NCCL设置实现GPU间模型复制。在NVL72上，FlashClone使远程权重内存映射时间降至约10ms（对比NCCL的10-110秒），单节点权重加载加速50倍，机架级并发加载加速270倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旗舰级MoE模型在参数总量和专家数量两个维度同时快速增长，在弹性部署场景中权重加载延迟成为显著瓶颈。即使是最先进的NVIDIA GB300 NVL72平台，现有加载器也未能充分利用带宽，主要存在三类结构性损失：权重内存碎片化导致传输远低于链路带宽、跨节点复制受NCCL通信器初始化阻塞（耗时10-110秒）、以及现有跨节点GPU克隆路径串行化且无法支撑多节点并发启动。

Method: 提出了FlashBoot权重加载子系统，包含三个层次的设计：(1) FabricArena——构建连续、可导出且节点间可寻址的张量内存布局，消除内存碎片化；(2) FlashLoad——将CPU权重作为单一整体进行一次批量零拷贝传输；(3) FlashClone——通过远程映射机制直接从远端GPU复制驻留模型权重，完全消除NCCL通信器设置开销。整个系统基于SGLang框架实现框架与工作流的协同设计。

Result: 在NVL72平台上使用DeepSeek-V4-Pro和DeepSeek-V4-Flash验证：FlashClone将远程权重内存映射时间从NCCL的10-110秒降至约10ms，每个克隆操作可持续维持>=700GB/s吞吐。相比现有最优方案，单节点权重加载从20.1秒加速到0.4秒（50倍加速），机架级并发权重加载从87秒加速到0.32秒（270倍以上加速）。

Conclusion: 通过内存布局重构（FabricArena）与传输路径优化（FlashLoad和FlashClone）的系统级协同设计，能够彻底消除现有MoE权重加载中的结构性瓶颈，实现数量级以上的部署加速。该系统在真实旗舰模型和工作负载上验证了有效性，并计划开源。

Abstract: Flagship Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are growing fast along two axes at once: total parameter count and the number of experts. In elastic deployment scenarios, many GPUs across many nodes must become serving-ready quickly, and this growth makes weight loading a noticeable part of the latency budget. Even on NVIDIA's GB300 NVL72, today's state-of-the-art loaders leave most of that bandwidth unused. The losses are structural: (C1) weight memory is fragmented into tens of thousands of per-tensor objects, so transfers run far below link bandwidth; (C2) cross-node replication is gated by NCCL communicator setup, which costs 10-110 s before a single weight byte moves; and (C3) the existing cross-node GPU->GPU clone path is serial and scales poorly to concurrent multi-node bring-up. We present FlashBoot, a hardware-friendly, framework-workflow co-designed weight-loading subsystem built on SGLang. At its core is FabricArena, a contiguous, exportable and inter-node addressable tensor memory layout. On top of it, FlashLoad loads from CPU as a single bulk, zero-copy transfer, and FlashClone replicates a resident model from a remote GPU via a remote-mapping mechanism that removes NCCL setup. In experiments on NVL72 with DeepSeek-V4-Pro and DeepSeek-V4-Flash, FlashClone maps remote weight memory in ~10 ms (versus 10-110 s for NCCL) and sustains >=700 GB/s per clone. Against the state of the art, FlashBoot accelerates single-node weight loading by up to 50x (from 20.1 s to 0.4 s) and concurrent rack-level weight loading by >270x (from 87 s to 0.32 s). Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [223] [C2C-Explorer: An Exploration Framework for Chip-to-Chip Interconnect Architectures in LLM Cloud Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08611)
*Jiayi Li,Di Wu,Qingxu Li,Hongxiao Zhao,Jiaqi Yang,Anjunyi Fan,Wenbin Zhang,Boqiang Wu,Shuting Liu,Shifeng Fang,Jianbo Dong,Dimin Niu,Bonan Yan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: C2C-Explorer是一个自适应贝叶斯设计空间探索（DSE）框架，用于在大语言模型（LLM）工作负载下优化芯片间（C2C）通信硬件架构。它集成了LLM工作负载驱动的流量生成器、可扩展互连模拟器（支持最多512个芯片的交换机/全网格拓扑）和指标引导评估器，实现了从工作负载到硬件的协同设计优化。该框架在32-XPU DeepSeek-R1-671B推理工作负载下实现了44.1%的吞吐量提升和98.4%的内存减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM规模的扩展，多处理器芯片架构已成为必然趋势，芯片间（C2C）通信的重要性日益凸显。然而，高效设计面向LLM工作负载的C2C硬件架构面临三大挑战：生成真实的LLM特定C2C流量、在大规模场景下精确模拟硬件级通信、高效探索指数级增长的C2C设计空间。现有方法无法同时解决这些问题。

Method: C2C-Explorer采用自适应贝叶斯DSE框架，集成了三个核心组件：1) LLM工作负载驱动的流量生成器，产生真实的LLM特定C2C通信流量；2) 可扩展互连模拟器（支持交换机/全网格拓扑，最多512个芯片），采用混合周期和事件模型加速大规模模拟；3) 指标引导评估器，评估候选架构的性能。整个流程构成从工作负载到硬件的优化管线，实现系统性的C2C架构协同设计。

Result: 通过FPGA-based C2C原型验证，该模拟器在不同流量模式下实现了2.46-8.23%的端到端时序误差。混合周期和事件模型相比纯周期精确基线将大规模模拟加速了最多7.8倍。在32-XPU DeepSeek-R1-671B推理工作负载中，C2C-Explorer识别出的配置将吞吐量（goodput）提升了44.1%，并将内存减少了98.4%。

Conclusion: C2C-Explorer首次将LLM实际工作负载、可扩展互连模拟和贝叶斯设计空间探索统一到同一框架中，有效解决了LLM场景下C2C硬件架构设计的关键挑战，在推理性能上取得了显著提升。该框架已开源，为LLM时代的C2C通信架构协同设计提供了实用工具。

Abstract: The scaling-up of large language models (LLMs) necessitates computing systems to have multi-processor-chip architectures, elevating the importance of chip-to-chip (C2C) communication. However, designing efficient C2C hardware architectures for LLM workloads faces three key challenges: generating realistic LLM-specific C2C traffic, accurately simulating hardware-level communication at scale, and efficiently exploring the exponentially large C2C design space. We propose C2C-Explorer, an adaptive Bayesian DSE framework that integrates a LLM-workload-driven traffic generator, a scalable interconnect simulator (switch/full-mesh, up to 512 chips), and a metric-guided evaluator into a workload-to-hardware optimization pipeline, enabling systematic C2C architectural co-design under realistic LLM workloads. Validated against FPGA-based C2C prototypes, the C2C simulator achieves 2.46-8.23% end-to-end timing error across diverse traffic patterns. Its hybrid cycle and event model further accelerates large-scale simulation by up to 7.8$\times$ over a pure cycle-accurate baseline. Applied to a 32-XPU DeepSeek-R1-671B inference workload, C2C-Explorer identifies configurations that improve goodput by 44.1% and reduce memory by 98.4%. C2C-Explorer is open-source and available at https://github.com/Selinaee/C2C-Explorer.

</details>


### [224] [Randomized Tree-Intersection Leader Election](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08817)
*Yuval Emek,Shay Kutten,Ido Rafael,Gadi Taubenfeld*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种在同步完全图CONGEST模型中的随机化领导者选举算法，通过单一分支参数ℓ实现了时间复杂度和每节点消息复杂度之间的高度可调权衡，总消息复杂度保持在O(√n log^1.5 n)次线性范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式系统中，领导者选举是基础性问题，而带宽受限网络与高速网络对时间效率和消息开销的需求往往矛盾。现有算法难以在时间复杂度和消息复杂度之间提供灵活的调节能力，需要一种可根据网络带宽条件进行平滑权衡的解决方案。

Method: 利用动态截断的ℓ叉树扩展，结合新颖的"静默脉冲"验证机制。通过强制扩展形成精确体积的近完全树，节点可以在不超过次线性消息界限的前提下安全地聚合拓扑权重。通过调节分支参数ℓ，实现O(log_ℓ√(n log n))的时间复杂度和O(ℓ)的每节点消息复杂度。

Result: 算法实现了O(log_ℓ√(n log n))轮时间复杂度和O(ℓ)每节点消息复杂度，总体消息复杂度为O(√n log^1.5 n)（严格次线性）。通过调节ℓ，可在O(1)每节点消息负担与O(1)时间之间平滑切换。

Conclusion: 该算法为系统设计者提供了高度灵活的权衡机制：低带宽网络可用O(1)每节点开销运行，高带宽环境可在O(1)时间内完成选举，兼顾了两类场景的需求。

Abstract: We present a randomized leader election algorithm for synchronous complete $n$-node graphs in the \textsf{CONGEST} model that introduces a highly tunable trade-off between time complexity and the per-node message complexity. By adjusting a single branching parameter, $\ell$, system designers can smoothly shift the algorithmic burden from execution time to per-node message complexity, all while maintaining a strictly sublinear total message complexity of $O(\sqrt{n} \log^{1.5} n)$. We achieve this by utilizing dynamically truncated $\ell$-ary tree expansions coupled with a novel ``silent pulse'' verification mechanism. By forcing the expansions to form exact-volume almost-complete trees, nodes can safely aggregate topological weights without overshooting the sublinear message bounds. Specifically, our algorithm achieves $O(\log_\ell \sqrt{n \log n})$ time (round) complexity and $O(\ell)$ per-node message complexity. This flexibility allows networks with tight bandwidth constraints to operate with a minimal $O(1)$ per-node burden, while high-bandwidth environments can collapse the election into $O(1)$ time units.

</details>


### [225] [Universal Rendezvous of Anonymous Agents with Footprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08981)
*Bibhuti Das*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文针对匿名移动智能体的确定性会合问题，在带足迹模型下提出了一种通用会合算法，适用于所有底层连通图（有限或可数无限）的实例，从而肯定地回答了Das和Pelc在SPAA 2026中提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个匿名移动智能体在匿名连通图中确定性会合是分布式计算中的基础问题。带足迹模型允许智能体在访问未标记节点时留下永久足迹。Das和Pelc提出的开放问题是：在带足迹模型中，是否对所有可行实例都存在通用会合算法？本文旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: 本文提出了一种通用会合算法，适用于底层图为连通（有限或可数无限）的所有实例。算法利用了足迹模型的特性，即智能体访问未标记节点时留下永久且相同的足迹，通过设计统一的策略使得任意两个从不同节点出发的匿名智能体能够在有限时间内相遇。

Result: 证明了在带足迹模型下，对于所有底层图为连通图（有限或可数无限）的实例，存在通用会合算法，给出了该开放问题的肯定答案。

Conclusion: 本文回答了Das和Pelc提出的开放问题，证明了带足迹模型下存在覆盖所有可行实例的通用确定性会合算法，扩展了会合问题在该模型下的理论与可行性边界。

Abstract: Deterministic rendezvous for two anonymous mobile agents starting simultaneously from two distinct nodes of an anonymous connected graph and navigating synchronously in the graph requires that they meet at some node. An instance of the rendezvous problem is the underlying graph, together with two distinct nodes that are the initial positions of the agents. Such an instance is said to be feasible if there is a deterministic algorithm guaranteeing rendezvous, possibly valid only for this instance. A rendezvous algorithm is said to be universal for a class of instances if it guarantees rendezvous for all feasible instances from this class.
  We consider the model with footprints: whenever an agent visits an unmarked node, it leaves a permanent footprint on it, and all footprints are identical. This paper aims at answering the open problem from the paper by Das and Pelc (SPAA 2026), asking whether there exists a universal rendezvous algorithm for the class of all instances in the model with footprints. We propose a universal rendezvous algorithm for the class of all instances where the underlying graph is connected (finite or countably infinite) in the model with footprints. This shows the existence of a universal algorithm for the class of all instances in the model with footprints, which is an affirmative answer to the open problem.

</details>


### [226] [A Resource-centric Analysis and Optimization of NoSQL Workloads using Distressed Resource Volume Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09173)
*Gunika Verma,Aashutosh A,Pooja Srinivas,Yogesh Simmhan,Ayush Choure,Harshit Shah,Mayukh Das,Prashant Sasatte,Chetan Bansal,Abhijit Pai,Suraj Dixit,Achint Agrawal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于大规模云数据库NoSQL工作负载的开源基准测试管道（LoadStar），包括从Cosmos DB真实集群提取的开源工作负载、新的可靠性指标DRV，以及用于副本放置优化的Orbit PAM算法，实验结果展示了显著的资源节省和可靠性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模托管云数据库依赖负载打包与迁移（PAM）算法实现高效运行，但缺乏公开的NoSQL工作负载严重限制了云数据库资源和可靠性优化的研究。

Method: 1) 从Cosmos DB真实集群提取并开源NoSQL工作负载；2) 提出DRV（Distressed Resource Volume）可靠性指标来量化用户体验质量；3) 开发LoadStar策略仿真框架，基于非参数统计模型估计真实流量模式的QoS；4) 提出Luna模型用于预测未来负载分布；5) 设计Orbit PAM算法，利用预测结果触发和重新平衡压力副本以减少尾部错误。

Result: 通过LoadStar验证，Orbit相比现有Cosmos DB策略和最差适应（worst-fit）优化基线，能以更低错误率交付更高负载，最多减少35%的资源消耗。该方案已部署到生产环境，预计每年节省数亿美元成本，同时为数百万客户提升服务可靠性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个可复用的端到端基准测试管道（工作负载+指标+仿真框架），并验证了基于负载预测的PAM算法在真实大规模云数据库中的有效性和经济价值。

Abstract: Large-scale managed cloud databases leverage sophisticated load Packing and Migration (PAM) algorithms, which provide the efficiencies necessary for running these services at scale on cloud resources. Research into optimizing the resources and reliability of cloud databases at massive scales is limited by a lack of public NoSQL workloads. We address this in the context of Cosmos DB, Microsoft's flagship cloud-hosted NoSQL database. We first propose open-source NoSQL workloads from real Cosmos DB clusters, and analyze these traces to derive a novel reliability metric, Distressed Resource Volume (DRV), which captures the quality of service experienced by the end user. We then develop an open-source policy simulation framework, LoadStar, powered by a non-parametric statistical model of estimating the QoS of real traffic patterns. These form a reusable benchmark pipeline for validating policies for resource-centric NoSQL workloads. We then define a resource optimization problem for placing Cosmos DB replicas onto VM nodes, develop the Luna model for forecasting future load distributions, and the Orbit PAM algorithm that uses these forecasts to trigger and rebalance stressed replicas, to reduce tail-errors. Our experiments, validated using LoadStar for these workloads, demonstrate Orbit's benefits over the existing Cosmos DB policy and a worst-fit optimized baseline, with higher load delivered at lower error rates and up to $35\%$ reduction in resources. These have been deployed in production, with potential savings of $\$100M$s/yr while improving service reliability for millions of customers.

</details>


### [227] [Beyond Fast Contractions: Attenuation and Recovery of Matrix-Engine Speedups in High-Order Finite Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09175)
*Yinuo Wang,Lin Gan,Tianqi Mao,Zeyu Song,Wubing Wan,Jiayu Fu,Zekun Yin,Yuyang Jin,Xiaohui Duan,Wei Xue,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了ARM LX2 CPU上矩阵引擎（SME）相对SIMD（SVE）在实际科学计算应用SPEFFM3D刚度算子中的性能差距。虽然SME在单精度峰值上有4倍优势，但在完整算子中仅能实现1.1倍加速。通过因子化诊断方法，作者发现瓶颈在于逐点计算、间接场移动与同步、以及不规则系数传递。通过显式SIMD优化、字段布局调整和向量分块系数流等技术，将完整算子的加速比提升至1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代处理器提供的矩阵引擎峰值吞吐量远超传统SIMD，但科学应用难以端到端地获得这一优势。作者旨在探究并弥合这一差距，以SPECFEM3D的刚度算子为案例进行深入分析。

Method: 在LEFX2 CPU上，以高性能SVE实现为基线，通过因子化诊断方法将性能损失归因于逐点计算、间接场移动与同步、以及不规则系数传递三个因素。针对性地采用显式SIMD、字段布局调整（缓解间接移动与同步）和向量分块系数流（降低不规则访问开销）三种优化策略，并辅以无收缩控制分析收缩内核的理论性能上限。

Result: SME的4倍单精度峰值优势在孤立张量收缩中降至2.2倍，在完整算子中仅剩1.1倍。通过显式SIMD将完整算子加速比提升至1.3倍；结合字段布局调整与向量分块系数流，在高阶情况下进一步提升至1.6倍。收缩控制的性能上限为1.11-1.32倍。

Conclusion: 实现矩阵引擎的性能潜力需要对整个算子路径进行协同设计，而非仅仅替换收缩内核。单纯依赖矩阵引擎的张量收缩优势无法转化到端到端应用性能，必须同时解决算子中非收缩部分的瓶颈。

Abstract: Modern processors increasingly provide matrix engines whose peak arithmetic throughput greatly exceeds conventional SIMD, but scientific applications rarely realize this advantage end to end. We examine this gap in SPECFEM3D's dominant stiffness operator on the Arm LX2 CPUs that power the flagship Lineshine supercomputer. Against a matched, high-performance SVE baseline on the same cores, SME's $4\times$ single-precision peak advantage falls to $2.2\times$ for isolated tensor contractions and $1.1\times$ for the complete operator. Our factorized diagnostic attributes the loss to pointwise computation, indirect field movement and synchronization, and irregular coefficient delivery. Explicit SIMD mitigates pointwise work, raising the full-operator speedup to $1.3\times$. Field-layout changes mitigate indirect movement and synchronization, while vector-blocked coefficient streaming reduces irregular-access costs; together they raise speedup to $1.6\times$ at high order. A contraction-free control bounds further contraction-only gains at $1.11$--$1.32\times$. Realizing matrix-engine performance therefore requires co-designing the entire operator path, not merely replacing its contraction kernel.

</details>


### [228] [UnionSparse: An Index-Efficient Sparsity Framework for Low-Bit Sparse LLM Inference on Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09291)
*Tianhao Jiang,Hang Gu,Teng Wang,Qianyu Cheng,ZhenDong Zheng,Cheng Tang,Qiyue Su,Wenqi Lou,Lei Gong,Chao Wang,Xi Li,Xuehai Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对边缘GPU上低比特稀疏LLM推理中稀疏元数据开销过大的问题，提出UnionSparse框架，通过索引高效位图编码（IE-BME）和低比特共享内存并行解码（LSPD）方法，在W4A4量化及30%-70%稀疏度下，相比FlashLLM和SpInfer分别提升2.30x和1.43x，相比CUTLASS和cuBLAS Tensor Core分别提升1.56x和3.46x，确立了负载提取效率作为低比特稀疏推理的一阶关注点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘GPU上的LLM推理结合稀疏性和低比特量化来满足设备内存、延迟和功耗限制，但现有的量化方案缩减了权重负载却未按比例缩减稀疏元数据，导致索引传输和非零值提取成为SpMM的瓶颈问题。作者引入负载-元数据比（PMR）指标来量化这一问题，并指出提升PMR能提高解码阶段的有效计算强度。

Method: 提出UnionSparse框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 索引高效位图编码（IE-BME），用于摊销元数据开销并使稀疏遍历与片段组装对齐；(2) 低比特共享内存并行解码（LSPD）SpMM内核，优化小批量执行效率。两者结合共同提升PMR，从而改善解码阶段的计算强度。

Result: 在W4A4量化和30%-70%稀疏度条件下，UnionSparse相比FlashLLM和SpInfer分别实现2.30x和1.43x的加速，相比CUTLASS和cuBLAS Tensor Core分别实现1.56x和3.46x的加速。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 负载提取效率（PMR）应作为低比特稀疏推理在边缘GPU上的一阶性能关注点，UnionSparse通过索引高效编码和并行解码机制有效解决了这一瓶颈，为边缘低比特稀疏推理提供了重要的性能改进方向。

Abstract: Edge LLM inference combines sparsity and low-bit quantization to meet device memory, latency, and power limits. Yet quantization shrinks weight payloads without proportionally reducing sparse metadata, so index traffic and nonzero extraction become critical SpMM bottlenecks. We introduce the Payload-to-Metadata Ratio (PMR) and show that improving PMR raises effective compute intensity in decoding.
  We present UnionSparse, an index-efficient framework that combines Index-Efficient Bitmap Encoding (IE-BME) with a SpMM kernel using Low-Bit Shared-Memory Parallel Decoding (LSPD). IE-BME amortizes metadata and aligns sparse traversal with fragment assembly, while LSPD improves small-batch execution. Under W4A4 quantization and 30%--70% sparsity, UnionSparse outperforms FlashLLM and SpInfer by 2.30x and 1.43x, and CUTLASS and cuBLAS Tensor Core by 1.56x and 3.46x, respectively. These results establish payload-extraction efficiency as a first-order concern for low-bit sparse inference on edge GPUs. Source code is available at: https://github.com/Victor-Alen/UnionSparse.

</details>


### [229] [Beyond the Limits: Flexible and Congestion-Aware Cluster Scheduling for the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09308)
*Oliver Larsson,Thijs Metsch,Cristian Klein,Erik Elmroth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对云环境中工作负载调度的两个关键局限性提出互补策略：一是引入软SLO限制以允许受控过度承诺并容忍轻微瞬时违规，提升集群效率；二是基于LLC和内存带宽等共享资源的实时拥塞感知进行调度与重调度。实验表明，软SLO限制可将纠正性重调度操作减少49%，资源感知调度可将节点级拥塞降低8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云环境工作负载调度通常基于简化的资源需求假设，忽视应用级性能目标和硬件资源争用问题。严格硬性执行SLO导致资源利用率低下和能效差，同时缺乏对LLC和内存带宽等共享资源的拥塞感知，造成资源使用低效和性能退化。

Method: 提出两种互补策略：(i) 集成软SLO限制，允许受控过度承诺并容忍微小瞬时违规，提高集群效率；(ii) 基于LLC和内存带宽等共享资源的实时拥塞信息，引入资源感知的调度和重调度机制。

Result: 软SLO限制相比硬限制方法减少49%的纠正性重调度操作，同时保持可接受的性能保证；资源感知调度将节点级拥塞降低8%，进一步缓解SLO违规。

Conclusion: 将应用级灵活性与硬件级洞察相结合融入调度和重调度决策中，能有效提升集群资源效率、降低节点拥塞并减少SLO违规，验证了所提两种策略的有效性。

Abstract: Workload scheduling in cloud environments often relies on simplistic assumptions about application resource needs and hardware utilization. Overlooking application-level performance objectives and hardware resource contention that leads to inefficient resource usage and degraded performance. This paper addresses two key limitations of current approaches. First, unnecessarily strict enforcement of service level objectives (SLOs) often leads to resource underutilization and poor energy efficiency. Second, lack of congestion awareness in shared resources such as last-level cache (LLC) and memory bandwidth. In this paper, we propose two complementary strategies to address these limitations: (i) integrating soft SLO limits that allow controlled overcommitment and tolerate minor, transient violations to improve cluster efficiency, and (ii) introducing resource-aware scheduling and rescheduling based on real-time congestion insights for shared resources such as last-level cache (LLC) and memory bandwidth. Our results show that soft SLO limits reduce corrective rescheduling actions by 49% compared to hard-limit approaches while maintaining acceptable performance guarantees. Additionally, resource-aware scheduling decreases node-level congestion by 8% and further mitigates SLO violations, demonstrating the effectiveness of incorporating application-level flexibility and hardware-level insights into scheduling and rescheduling decisions.

</details>


### [230] [How Accurately Can the Energy Use of Spark Applications Be Estimated Based on Resource Utilisation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09359)
*Youssef Moawad,Kathleen West,Vasilis Bountris,Philipp Thamm,Yehia Elkhatib,Lauritz Thamsen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文以Apache Spark在Kubernetes上的运行为案例，研究了基于模型和数据中心资源利用率追踪的能耗估计方法的准确性，并与Intel RAPL硬件计数器（真实能耗）进行对比，发现使用外部监控信号相比Spark任务追踪迹可以显著降低能耗低估误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云环境中对用户访问节点级硬件能源计数器受限，使得可持续性核算（能源与碳归属）只能依赖功率模型和资源利用率追踪，但这些估计方法的准确性需要在进行直接计数器验证时加以检验。

Method: 使用Apache Spark（运行于Kubernetes）作为数据流运行时和集群资源管理器的案例研究，将基于模型的能耗估计与Intel RAPL封装和DRAM实际能耗进行对比，比较不同的CPU使用信号和内存系数，在AWS裸金属云和本地集群两个环境中进行验证。

Result: 在AWS上，采用外部监控可将封装能耗的符号误差从-29.58%降至-24.41%，在本地集群上从-24.00%降至-16.22%，表明外部监控相比Spark任务追踪能减少能耗低估。

Conclusion: 外部监控信号相较于Spark任务迹能够提供更准确的能耗估计，有助于在无法访问硬件计数器的云环境中实现更透明的可持续性核算。

Abstract: Distributed batch data processing applications are widely executed on cloud-based resources where restricted user access to node-level hardware energy counters hinders transparent sustainability accounting. Energy and carbon attribution methodologies therefore depend on power models and available resource utilisation traces, yet the accuracy of these estimates has to be validated while direct counters are available. In this work, we use Apache Spark running on Kubernetes as a case-study dataflow runtime and cluster resource manager to compare model-based energy estimates to Intel RAPL package and DRAM energy on an AWS bare-metal cloud and an on-premises cluster, comparing different CPU usage signals and memory coefficients. We show that external monitoring improves signed package-energy error relative to Spark task traces, reducing underestimation from -29.58% to -24.41% on AWS and from -24.00% to -16.22% on-premises.

</details>


### [231] [$\tilde{\text{O}}$ptimal Distributed Maximum Flow Approximation in Undirected Planar Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09500)
*Yaseen Abd-Elhaleem,Michal Dory,Oren Weimann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向一般无向平面图的最大st-流问题的分布式近最优近似算法，在Õ(D)轮内实现(1-o(1))近似，移除了此前算法中s和t必须位于同一面的限制，并消除了n^{o(1)}因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前PODC'25的工作虽然给出了最大st-流问题精确算法的匹配界Õ(D²)，但其(1-o(1))近似算法只适用于s和t在同一面的特殊情况，且存在D·n^{o(1)}轮的额外开销。移除这一限制并达到近最优的Õ(D)轮是该领域的关键开放问题。

Method: 核心技术贡献是在分布式环境下实现经典Reif集中式算法。通过在平面图的对偶图G*上进行精细的递归切割(incision)过程，巧妙地模拟了对偶图上的动态变化，同时仅利用原始图G上的通信。

Result: 首次实现了一般无向平面图上最大st-流问题的分布式近最优算法，达到Õ(D)轮和(1-o(1))近似比，同时移除了s和t同面的限制并消除了n^{o(1)}因子。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了分布式平面图优化算法领域的一个重要空白，将最大st-流问题的近似算法从受限情况推广到一般情况，实现了与最短路径问题已知结果相匹配的近最优复杂度。

Abstract: Persistent efforts in recent years have been devoted to devising distributed algorithms for fundamental optimization problems in planar graphs. In particular, for Single-Source Shortest-Paths, there is an $\tilde O(D^2)$-rounds exact algorithm [Li, Parter STOC'19] for directed planar graphs, and an $\tilde {O}(D)$-rounds $(1+o(1))$-approximation algorithm [Rozhon, Grunau, Haeupler, Zuzic, Li STOC'22] for undirected planar graphs (where $D$ is the graph's hop-diameter). Recently [Abd-Elhaleem, Dory, Parter, Weimann PODC'25], a matching bound for the exact case was obtained for the Maximum $st$-Flow problem. Namely, an $\tilde O(D^2)$-rounds exact algorithm for directed planar graphs. However, for the approximate case, they give a $D\cdot n^{o(1)}$-rounds $(1-o(1))$-approximation algorithm for undirected planar graphs that works only for the special case where both $s$ and $t$ lie on the same face.
  In this paper, we remove the restriction that both $s$ and $t$ must lie on the same face (we also eliminate the $n^{o(1)}$ factor). Namely, we present the first distributed near-optimal $\tilde{O}(D)$-rounds $(1-o(1))$-approximation algorithm for Maximum $st$-Flow in general undirected planar graphs. Our main technical contribution is a distributed implementation of the classical Reif's [SICOMP'83] centralized algorithm. This is achieved by a careful recursive incision procedure on the planar dual $G^*$ of the graph $G$. It is challenging, because we need to simulate dynamic changes (incisions) over the dual graph $G^*$, while we can only communicate over the input graph $G$.

</details>


### [232] [A Preliminary Study on Simultaneous Coscheduling for Discrete GPU vs. Fused GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09647)
*Poorna Gunathilaka,Nabayan Chaudhury,Kirshanthan Sundararajah,Wu-chun Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究初步评估了NVIDIA GH200超级芯片与H100 PCIe平台上的CPU-GPU协同调度性能，以稀疏共轭梯度（CG）为例，考察了三种内存管理范式（显式拷贝、托管内存、映射内存）下的不同工作划分，发现GH200使多种混合工作划分更具竞争力，并使托管内存在多个矩阵上变得实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU-GPU协同调度能够同时利用两种处理单元执行应用，但其效率高度依赖工作负载划分和内存架构，需要评估新型集成平台（如GH200）相对于传统离散GPU平台的优势。

Method: 以稀疏共轭梯度（CG）为案例，在GH200超级芯片和H100 PCIe离散平台上，对比三种内存管理范式（显式拷贝、托管内存、映射内存）下的多种CPU-GPU工作划分方案，评估运行时间和可编程性之间的权衡。

Result: 与H100 PCIe平台相比，GH200使多种混合CPU-GPU工作划分方案具有竞争力，并使托管内存在多个矩阵上变得实用可行。

Conclusion: 集成的CPU-GPU平台（如GH200）能够在协同调度工作负载中同时提升性能和可编程性。

Abstract: CPU-GPU coscheduling enables simultaneous execution of an application across both processing units, but its efficiency depends on workload partitioning and memory architecture. This preliminary study evaluates coscheduling on the NVIDIA GH200 Superchip compared to a discrete H100 PCIe platform. Using sparse conjugate gradient (CG) as a case study, we assess various work divisions across three memory-management paradigms: explicit copy, managed memory, and mapped memory. Our evaluation highlights the run time and programmability tradeoffs of reducing manual CPU-GPU data movement. The results show that compared with the H100 PCIe platform, GH200 makes several hybrid CPU-GPU work divisions competitive and makes managed memory practical for several matrices. These results suggest that integrated CPU-GPU platforms such as GH200 can improve both performance and programmability for coscheduled workloads.

</details>


### [233] [SpSYRK: Half the Work in Distributed Sparse Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09713)
*Thomas McFarland,Julian Bellavita,Giulia Guidi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用对称性的分布式稀疏SYRK算法，通过仅计算输出的下三角部分并优化通信重排，在Perlmutter超算的32节点上相比最优Sparse SUMMA实现了2倍加速，且可无缝替代现有分布式SpGEMM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏SYRK（C=AA^T）在机器学习、图分析和基因组学中广泛用于相似度搜索（如Jaccard相似度），但现有分布式稀疏矩阵乘法算法（如Sparse SUMMA）将稀疏SYRK当作通用矩阵乘法处理，未利用输入输出的对称性，导致性能未被充分挖掘。

Method: 提出两种分布式稀疏SYRK算法：1）将输出的非对角块在进程网格的上三角和下三角之间划分，每个对角块仅计算下三角部分，减少每进程的通信和计算量；2）第二种变体重排通信顺序以完全避免物化A的转置。同时建立基于算术强度的代价模型来预测性能。

Result: 在Perlmutter超算32节点上，算法在局部乘法主导运行时的矩阵上相比优化版Sparse SUMMA取得2倍加速；在通信受限输入上优势收窄（代价模型可预测），但第二种变体在高层进程数下持续实现优越的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为任何需要计算C=AA^T的分布式SpGEMM应用的即插即用替代方案，三角输出可直接被后续操作消费，降低计算和内存开销。

Abstract: The symmetric rank-$k$ update (SYRK), $\C = \A\A^\top$, computes the dot product between each pair of rows of $\A$, producing the Gram matrix $\C$. Its sparse variant underpins similarity search in machine learning, graph analytics, and genomics, including Jaccard similarity on datasets too large for a single node. Yet, despite the symmetry in its inputs and outputs, existing distributed sparse matrix multiplication algorithms, such as Sparse SUMMA, treat sparse SYRK as generic multiplication, leaving performance untapped.
  In this paper, we present distributed sparse SYRK approaches that leverage symmetry. The approach partitions the off-diagonal blocks of the output between the upper and lower triangular portions of the process grid and computes only the lower-triangular part of each diagonal block, reducing per-process communication and computation compared with state-of-the-art distributed SpGEMM. A second variant reorders communication to avoid materializing $\A^\top$.
  On 32 nodes of the Perlmutter supercomputer, the algorithm achieves a $2\times$ speedup over an optimized Sparse SUMMA on matrices where local multiplication dominates the runtime; the advantage narrows on communication-bound inputs, a dependence the cost model predicts from the arithmetic intensity. Our variant rectifies this and consistently achieves superior scaling at high process counts. The approach is a drop-in replacement for any application computing $\C = \A \A^\top$ via a distributed SpGEMM routine, and its triangular output can be consumed directly by subsequent operations, reducing both compute and memory footprint.

</details>


### [234] [Defining Decentralization: An Ontological Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09748)
*Jakub Kacper Szeląg,Aydin Abadi,Mohammad Naseri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出了计算机科学中"去中心化"缺乏统一定义的问题（称为"去中心化问题"），并提出了基于图论的去中心化本体论框架，将其定义为计算机通信系统的关系性和主体特定属性，同时引入两个新度量（Void Tolerance和Imperviousness）以及一个浏览器实现工具，用于对不同系统进行自动化分类和度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化虽在计算机科学中有超过半个世纪的历史，但一直缺乏普适性定义，尤其在去中心化AI和机器学习范式（如协作训练、分布式推理、区块链和智能体AI）涌现的背景下，现有方法常将去中心化与信任分布或特定实现模式混淆，导致系统分析不一致、作品间可比性受限、形式推理严谨性不足。

Method: 从形式语义学、认识论和实用主义三个层面分析去中心化的基础，提出基于图的去中心化本体论框架，将去中心化定义为计算机通信系统的一种关系性和主体特定属性，并正式区分去中心化与分布式；引入Void Tolerance和Imperviousness两个新度量指标，并开发基于浏览器的实现工具以支持自动化分类和度量计算。

Result: 该框架在联邦学习和区块链架构上的实例化应用中产生了一致且可比较的评估结果，而现有定义在这些场景下会产生不完整或矛盾的结论，证明该方法为异构系统中去中心化分析提供了领域无关的基础。

Conclusion: 本文提出的图基去中心化本体论框架填补了去中心化定义缺失的研究空白，为跨异构计算机通信系统的去中心化分析提供了可操作、可度量的统一基础，有望提升系统分析一致性和跨工作可比性。

Abstract: Decentralization as a concept in computer science has existed for over half a century. Despite its fundamental role across domains such as security, distributed computing, artificial intelligence, cloud infrastructures, and Internet of Things (IoT) architectures, there remains no universally accepted definition of decentralization applicable across computer communication systems. This has become increasingly problematic with the emergence of decentralized AI and machine learning paradigms, including collaborative training, distributed inference, blockchain-based, and agentic AI, where decentralization is often treated as a core design objective. Meanwhile, existing approaches frequently conflate decentralization with related notions such as distribution of trust or specific implementation paradigms. Such ambiguity creates inconsistencies in system analysis, limits comparability between works, and weakens the rigor of formal reasoning surrounding communication architectures and protocol design. In this work, we define this research gap as the Decentralization Problem.
  We analyze the formal-semantic, epistemological, and pragmatic foundations of decentralization and introduce a graph-based ontology defining it as both relational and subject-specific property of computer communication systems. The framework formally distinguishes decentralization from distribution and supports evaluation through two novel metrics: Void Tolerance and Imperviousness. We also provide a browser-based implementation that enables automated classification and metric computation of arbitrary systems. Instantiations to federated learning and blockchain architectures show consistent, comparable assessments where existing definitions produce incomplete or contradictory conclusions, providing a domain-independent foundation for analysing decentralization across heterogeneous systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [World Simulator: Queer Erotica and the Absurdity of AI Video Models That Promise the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07510)
*Adam Cole,Mick Grierson*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为"World Simulator"的视频装置艺术作品，该作品将同性色情素材输入AI视频生成管道，揭示这些被标榜为"世界模拟器"的AI模型因训练数据的过滤而系统性地无法识别和再现人类身体与性经验，从而产生荒诞的幻觉输出，以此批判AI"世界模拟器"宣称的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI视频模型被广泛标榜为"世界模拟器"，声称能够模拟无限现实，但这些模型却系统性排除了人类具身经验的重要方面（尤其是性）。作者希望揭示这种普遍宣称（universal claims）与模型内在盲区（inherent blindness）之间的诗意摩擦，挑战这一过度自负的标签，并进一步反思模拟本身的价值。

Method: 作者创作了一个视频装置艺术作品，将明确的同性色情内容输入AI视频到视频（video-to-video）的生成管道中。由于训练数据中缺少对这类图像的识别能力，系统会产生超现实的幻觉替代品，将亲密行为转化为厨房电器、奇异的建筑构造和抽象的肉体等平庸场景。

Result: 该系统在缺乏相应训练数据的情况下发生了显著幻觉，将亲密的性行为场景转译为与之完全无关的荒诞画面（如厨房器具、奇异建筑等），直观地可视化了AI模型在感知和再现身体方面的结构性盲区。

Conclusion: 通过这一艺术实践，作品不仅批判了"世界模拟器"这一标签的狂妄之处（因为模型在结构上对身体视而不见，却号称能模拟整个世界），还进一步提出对"模拟"本身价值的追问——探索是否可能存在更具包容性、更具生命力与肯定性的感官表征形式。

Abstract: Increasingly, AI video models are marketed as "world simulators," suggesting their ability to model infinite realities. Despite such claims, these models systematically exclude significant aspects of embodied human experience, particularly sexuality. World Simulator is a video installation exploring the poetic friction between these universal claims and the models' inherent blindness. To do so, the work feeds explicit gay erotica into an AI video-to-video pipeline. Lacking the training data to recognize these images, the system hallucinates surreal alternatives, transforming intimate acts into banal scenes of kitchen appliances, strange architectures, and abstract flesh. By visualizing the limits of synthetic knowledge, the work challenges the hubris of the "world simulator" label, asking how a system, trained primarily on large filtered video datasets, can claim to simulate the world while remaining structurally blind to the body. Beyond this critique, we question the value of simulation itself, asking what forms of sensual representation might offer more expansive, life-affirming possibilities.

</details>


### [236] [SAMOT: State-Aware Step Modulation and Optimal Transport Matching for Audio-Visual Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08147)
*Kai Peng,Yunzhe Shen,Miao Zhang,Leiye Liu,Wei Ji,Jingjing Li,Yongri Piao,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出SAMOT框架，通过自适应动态步长调制（ADSM）和基于最优传输的匹配调制（OT-MM）解决音视频实例分割中模态状态变化和模态间分布差异两大挑战，在AVIS基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频实例分割（AVIS）存在两大核心挑战：一是复杂的模态状态变化破坏长程建模；二是模态间结构与分布差异阻碍实例级关联。现有方法（固定步长Transformer、递归Mamba）缺乏对模态状态变化的适应性，隐式匹配方法忽略分布不一致性问题。

Method: 提出两个关键模块：1）ADSM（自适应动态步长调制）利用时间变化、跨模态差异和历史上下文自适应调制Mamba步长，在快速响应模态状态变化与稳定长程建模间取得平衡；2）OT-MM（最优传输匹配调制）将实例级跨模态匹配显式建模为熵正则化最优传输问题，通过log域Sinkhorn迭代求解，并用MMD正则化器强化分布级一致性。

Result: 在AVIS基准上取得SOTA性能：FSLA提升+3.76，HOTA提升+2.75，mAP提升+2.58，并通过全面的定性可视化验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的ADSM和OT-MM有效解决了AVIS中模态状态变化适应性和跨模态实例匹配的分布差异问题，验证了自适应步长调制与显式最优传输匹配在音视频实例分割任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Instance Segmentation (AVIS) aims to simultaneously classify, segment, and track sounding objects within video sequences. Unlike Audio-Visual Semantic Segmentation (AVS), AVIS involves instance-level modeling across longer video sequences, introducing two key challenges: (1) complex modality-state changes disrupt long-range modeling, and (2) substantial structural and distributional discrepancies between modalities hinder precise instance-level association. Existing methods rely on fixed-step Transformers and recursive Mamba models, lacking adaptability to modality-state changes. In addition, methods performing implicit matching ignore the inherent distributional inconsistencies. To address these issues, we propose a framework with Adaptive Dynamic Step Modulation (ADSM) and Optimal Transport-based Matching Modulation (OT-MM). ADSM adaptively modulates Mamba step sizes using temporal variation, cross-modal discrepancy, and historical context, balancing rapid response to modality-state changes with stable long-range modeling. OT-MM explicitly formulates instance-level cross-modal matching as an entropy-regularized optimal transport problem solved via log-domain Sinkhorn iterations, and further enforces distribution-level coherence with an MMD regularizer. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on the AVIS benchmark (+3.76 FSLA, +2.75 HOTA, +2.58 mAP), verified through comprehensive qualitative visualizations. The code and model are available at https://github.com/happylife-pk/SAMOT.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Preserving Item Semantics for Free: Rethinking Token Initialization in LLM-Based Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07816)
*Donald Loveland,Liam Collins,Bhuvesh Kumar,Danai Koutra,Neil Shah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单的参数无关干预方法——直接用语义嵌入空间的质心初始化SID令牌嵌入，以保留语义几何结构，从而提升基于LLM的生成式推荐系统性能，在纯SFT下Recall@5最高提升16%，冷物品Recall@5最高提升60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐中，LLM常用的语义ID（SID）令牌通常被随机高斯向量初始化，丢弃了SID底层的连续几何结构，迫使LLM从交互数据中重新学习令牌关系，导致嵌入围绕物品流行度而非语义组织，且昂贵的持续预训练（CPT）无法可靠恢复原始语义几何。

Method: 提出一种零参数干预方法：将SID令牌嵌入直接初始化为其在语义嵌入空间中对应质心的向量。该方法仅需几行代码，无需额外训练或推理开销，可作为即插即用方案集成到现有基于LLM的生成式推荐系统中。

Result: 该方法在纯SFT下Recall@5最高提升16%，以少40%的SFT步骤达到峰值性能；冷物品Recall@5最高提升60%；在需要额外CPT的数据集上，以一半的CPT训练轮数达到可比性能。

Conclusion: 保留SID的几何结构（超越共享前缀结构）为基于LLM的生成式推荐提供了一种简单而有效的语义先验，能显著改善模型泛化能力和冷物品推荐性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative recommendation (GR) leverage large language models (LLMs) as recommender backbones, enabling LLMs to directly generate recommendations conditioned on item-interaction histories. In these systems, items are often represented through semantic IDs (SIDs) added to the LLM vocabulary as special tokens. Ideally, SIDs imbue item token representations with semantic priors, thereby improving model generalization. However, standard vocabulary expansion typically initializes these tokens as random Gaussian vectors, discarding the SIDs' underlying continuous geometry and forcing the LLM to relearn token relationships from interaction data. To demonstrate the consequences of this design, we first show that training from this initialization tends to organize SID embeddings around item popularity rather than semantics. We further show that, despite partially reducing the reliance on popularity and improving cold item performance, the computationally expensive process of continual pretraining (CPT) fails to reliably recover the original semantic geometry. To address these findings, we propose a simple, parameter-free intervention that initializes SID token embeddings directly from their corresponding centroids in the semantic embedding space. Requiring only a few lines of code and no additional training or inference overhead, this drop-in approach improves pure-SFT Recall@5 by up to 16%, reaches peak performance with up to 40% fewer SFT steps, and improves cold-item Recall@5 by up to 60%. Moreover, on datasets that benefit from additional CPT, centroid initialization reaches comparable performance while requiring half as many CPT epochs. Together, our findings show that preserving SID geometry, beyond shared-prefix structure, provides a simple and effective semantic prior for LLM-based GR.

</details>


### [238] [PushDualGen: Enabling LLMs to Generate Semantic IDs with Interpretable Copy for Industrial Push Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07989)
*Manjia Lin,Da Li,Yan Wang,Yong Jin,Zheming Ding,Wei Yuan,Lei Yan,Yanan Xia,Lu Zhang,Fan Yang,Xuanping Li,Yanan Niu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PushDualGen，一个轻量级生成式推荐模型，通过先生成语义ID再生成可跳过的解释性副本来实现可追踪推荐，已在快手推送系统中部署，有效播放率相对提升8.50%，不满意率相对下降37.70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快手推送系统面向近十亿用户，生成式推荐虽然实现了端到端个性化，但其黑盒特性导致推荐逻辑难以追踪，阻碍部署。现有方案OneRec-Thinking虽然通过CoT解决了可解释性问题，但显著增加了推理成本，难以支撑大规模工业应用。

Method: PushDualGen采用双生成策略：先生成语义ID（SID），再生成一个可跳过的解释性副本，实现推荐与解释的解耦，既保持生成式推荐的个性化能力，又提供可追踪性，同时通过轻量化设计降低推理开销，满足大规模工业部署需求。

Result: PushDualGen已在快手推送推荐系统中上线。在线A/B测试显示，用户有效播放率相对提升8.50%，不满意率相对下降37.70%；长期来看，模型优化了内容生态，为长尾视频提供了更多曝光机会。

Conclusion: PushDualGen通过轻量级双生成架构有效解决了生成式推荐的可解释性与推理成本之间的矛盾，在实际工业场景中验证了其显著的用户吸引力和满意度提升效果，并促进了内容生态的良性发展。

Abstract: Push recommendation in KuaiShou proactively delivers personalized content to nearly one billion users to facilitate their engagement. Recently, generative recommendation has achieved end-to-end user personalization through semantic ID.
